### [1] [Detect Early, Escalate Rarely: Anytime Detection of AI-Generated Video from the Compressed Bitstream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19476)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将AI生成视频检测重新定义为流式感知任务，利用码流中的运动场替代像素域前向计算，实现计算量降低5个数量级的在线检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦AI生成视频的在线检测，属于视频伪造检测领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景；不涉及目标检测（object detection）、小目标检测、旋转框检测等遥感目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化模型设计、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法。虽然涉及计算量优化（codec stage比pixel CNN计算量低5个数量级），但优化对象是视频流处理管线而非神经网络模型本身，且应用场景与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：AI生成视频的在线检测（streaming perception）；应用场景：视频伪造检测；传感器类型：视频码流（bitstream）；目标类别：AI生成视频 vs 真实视频。与遥感场景无关。

Model architecture: 不提出新检测器。核心思路是利用视频编码器已写入码流的运动场（motion field）进行解析（parse），而非像素域前向传播（pixel-domain forward pass）。使用一个端校准阈值（end-calibrated threshold）实现任意时间有效决策。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 计算量优化方法：利用码流中已有的运动场信息替代像素域CNN前向计算，将检测从离线评分转为在线流式感知。计算量降低5个数量级（five orders of magnitude less compute），可在CPU上运行。但这不是传统的模型轻量化方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS），而是任务重新定义带来的计算管线优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提到codec stage在CPU上计算量比pixel CNN低5个数量级（达到full-length AUC 0.64），以及gate机制在分布漂移下的表现。未涉及星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GenVidBench（主要）、AIGVDBench（验证stage-1排序可复现）；评价指标：AUC（full-length AUC 0.64）、stopping-time false-positive rate、accuracy（defer 15% clips时从0.75提升至0.78）、McNemar检验（p<10^{-6}）。

Experiments: 主要结果：codec stage在GenVidBench上达到full-length AUC 0.64，计算量比pixel CNN低5个数量级；gate机制在数据匹配校准分布时保持stopping-time FPR在目标水平，分布漂移时FPR上升；defer 15%的clips使accuracy从0.75提升至0.78，计算量减少7倍（McNemar p<10^{-6}）；stage-1排序在AIGVDBench上可复现。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 方法依赖视频码流中的运动场信息，遥感图像（尤其是单帧遥感图像）不存在码流结构；3. 针对AI生成视频检测这一特定任务设计，泛化到其他任务需重新设计；4. 分布漂移下gate的FPR会上升，鲁棒性有限。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可思考的方向是：在遥感视频卫星（如吉林一号视频卫星）的连续帧目标检测中，是否可以利用帧间运动信息（如光流）来减少逐帧全图推理的计算量？但该论文利用的是编码器已写入码流的运动场，而非额外计算光流，这一思路在遥感视频场景中可能不直接适用。总体而言，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的参考价值极低。

Abstract: Detectors for AI-generated video are evaluated offline. A clip is decoded to pixels and scored once, increasingly by a large vision-language model. Detection, however, is deployed online. We recast the task as streaming perception and score the motion field the codec already wrote into the bitstream. Reading that field is a parse, not a pixel-domain forward pass. Because the running aggregate is monotone, one end-calibrated threshold is anytime-valid at the data-dependent decision time. Recalibrating at each prefix is not. Escalation is priced in closed form. A compute budget maps to a deferral window, on a frontier monotone exactly where the deferral condition holds. On matched GenVidBench the codec stage reaches full-length AUC 0.64 at five orders of magnitude less compute than a pixel CNN, on CPU. Its gate holds the stopping-time false-positive rate at target while the real data match its calibration, and drifts above it under distribution shift. Deferring 15% of clips lifts accuracy from 0.75 to 0.78 at $7\times$ less compute (paired: McNemar $p<10^{-6}$). The stage-1 ordering replicates on AIGVDBench. We introduce no new detector. The contribution is the reframing, two guarantees, and the measured frontiers. Code, configurations, and evaluation splits: https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/streamdet.

</details>


### [2] [Crowd4D: Scene-Aware Monocular 4D Crowd Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19517)
*Hongbo Kang,Tianyi Zhou,Qingyang Yang,Hongwei Wen,Jing Huang,Yu-Kun Lai,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Crowd4D，一种从单目视频中恢复大规模场景下场景一致的4D人群运动重建框架，通过人-场景交互代理(HSIP)和人群结构相干性正则化(CSCR)实现场景感知的联合优化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于单目视频中大规模场景的4D人群运动重建，属于计算机视觉中的人体/场景三维重建领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：4D人群运动重建（4D crowd reconstruction）；应用场景：大规模真实场景下的单目视频人群重建；传感器类型：单目RGB相机；目标类别：人群（人体）。不涉及遥感目标检测中的舰船、飞机、车辆等。

Model architecture: Crowd4D框架，包含多阶段优化策略；关键模块：Human-Scene Interaction Proxy (HSIP) 由 Scene Interaction Point Clouds (SIPC) 和 Scene Interaction Surface (SIS) 组成；Crowd Structural Coherence Regularization (CSCR)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法，不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向离线场景重建，不具备星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标，仅提及在复杂大规模真实场景中进行了实验。

Experiments: 摘要声称Crowd4D在复杂大规模真实场景中一致优于现有SOTA方法，但未给出具体数值对比或消融实验结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未提供定量实验结果和数据集细节；3. 单目视频人群重建任务本身计算量大，未讨论实时性；4. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速策略。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。但以下思路可间接参考：1) 多阶段优化策略（multi-stage optimization）的思想可用于遥感检测中的多阶段轻量化训练策略；2) 场景几何先验（scene geometry priors）的概念可类比于遥感中利用地理空间先验辅助小目标检测。整体而言借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Recovering scene-consistent 4D crowd motion from monocular video in large-scale scenes remains challenging due to severe depth ambiguity and complex scene geometry. Existing monocular crowd reconstruction methods typically rely on single-plane assumptions, leading to unreliable metric scale and spatial drift under complex terrain. We propose Crowd4D, the first scene-aware 4D crowd reconstruction framework that jointly optimizes the crowd and scene from a monocular RGB video in large-scale scenes. Crowd4D explicitly incorporates scene geometry and ensures consistency across image and scene spaces via a multi-stage optimization strategy. A key bottleneck of this task lies in accurate human-scene alignment, particularly in scale and position. However, human and scene reconstructions are typically decoupled. To address this, we introduce the Human-Scene Interaction Proxy, abbreviated as HSIP, as an intermediate representation derived from Scene Interaction Point Clouds and a Scene Interaction Surface, abbreviated as SIPC and SIS. These representations encode explicit scene-aware geometric priors and redefine the optimization space for large-scale monocular 4D crowd reconstruction. To further improve temporal stability under occlusions, we introduce Crowd Structural Coherence Regularization, abbreviated as CSCR, which leverages HSIP-based spatial priors to impose soft temporal consistency on pairwise relative displacements and directions within local crowd neighborhoods. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Crowd4D consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods and enables robust monocular 4D crowd reconstruction in complex, large-scale real-world scenes.

</details>


### [3] [D3VL: Understanding Driving Scenes from 3D Time Series Data and Video with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19528)
*Heesang Han,A. Lynn Abbott,Abhijit Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出D3VL，一个融合2D图像和3D LiDAR/立体相机时序数据的多模态大语言模型框架，用于自动驾驶场景理解与问答任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自动驾驶场景的3D多模态大语言模型（MLLM），不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、边缘部署等模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自动驾驶场景的视觉问答（VQA）与场景理解；应用场景：自动驾驶（autonomous driving）；传感器类型：LiDAR、立体相机（stereo cameras）、2D图像/视频；目标类别：交通场景中的物体（摘要未具体说明类别）。

Model architecture: D3VL框架，一种融合2D和3D时序数据的多模态大语言模型（MLLM），架构简单（simple architecture），具体网络细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：KITTI Question-Answering (QA) dataset、Waymo QA dataset extension（本文新提出）；评价指标：QA准确率（具体指标名称摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在KITTI QA数据集上相比基线方法提升11%的性能；Waymo QA extension用于评估模型在多样化驾驶条件下处理3D和时序数据的能力。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未涉及目标检测任务，而是视觉问答；3. 未提供任何模型效率指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；4. 未讨论模型压缩或边缘部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。弱相关角度：如果未来研究方向涉及遥感多模态（如光学+SAR融合）的大语言模型，可参考其2D+3D融合思路，但当前阶段借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have triggered the development of end-to-end MLLMs for autonomous driving. However, the main emphasis to date has been for MLLMs using 2D images and videos. In contrast, this paper considers MLLM effectiveness using 3D sensors, particularly LiDAR and stereo cameras. LiDAR presents unique challenges to integration within an MLLM, largely because of data sparsity and lack of a grid structure for the data. For similar reasons, fusion of camera and LiDAR data within an MLLM pipeline is also uncommon. However, most autonomous systems rely on LiDAR-based sensing, and incorporating 3D data has been proven to improve performance in traditional 3D scene perception tasks. This paper presents D3VL, a novel MLLM framework that integrates 2D and 3D time-series data in a single but simple architecture. The model aims to answer questions involving traffic scene understanding and safety. D3VL shows an 11% improvement in the KITTI Question-Answering (QA) dataset compared to baseline methods in processing 2D and 3D time-series data. This paper further introduces the Waymo QA dataset extension, which assesses models' capabilities in processing 3D and time-series data under diverse driving conditions. D3VL implementation code and WaymoQA extension can be found on our supplemental website: https://automotivesafety-lvlm.github.io

</details>


### [4] [Synthetic and Derived Training Images for Campus Waste Detection: A Multi-Seed Evaluation with YOLOv8n](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19535)
*Ali Behbahani,Newsha Javanmardi,Shahriar Ahmed,Ling Chen,Phouvadeth Vathana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究利用合成图像和衍生图像增强YOLOv8n在校园垃圾回收场景中的检测性能，但所有增强配置均未超越仅使用真实图像的基线模型。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文场景是校园垃圾回收（campus recycling），传感器是bin-mounted camera（垃圾桶安装摄像头），目标类别是垃圾物品，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、航空图像或空间在轨智能。虽然使用了YOLOv8n（轻量检测器），但轻量化不是论文核心，而是数据增强策略研究。

Reading priority: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：目标检测（object detection）；应用场景：校园垃圾回收（campus recycling），非遥感场景；传感器类型：普通RGB摄像头（bin-mounted camera）；目标类别：垃圾物品（recycling items），非遥感目标。

Model architecture: YOLOv8n（YOLOv8 nano版本），是轻量级目标检测器，但论文未对模型结构做任何修改或轻量化改进。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。模型本身是YOLOv8n（轻量版本），但论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等轻量化或部署技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及YOLOv8n作为基础模型，但未讨论其在边缘设备上的部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：148张校园照片（86训练/31验证/31测试），以及多种合成/衍生图像；评价指标：mAP@0.5（mean Average Precision at IoU threshold 0.5）；实验设置：12种联合训练配置，7种主要设置重复4个随机种子，计算bootstrap百分位区间。

Experiments: 真实图像基线mAP@0.5=0.691 [0.665, 0.722]；背景替换降至0.560；孤立目标图像为0.680；完整增强池为0.487；手部-前臂合成图像修正后差异为+0.034 [-0.063, 0.199]，无可靠提升；所有增强配置均未超越真实图像基线。

Limitations: 1. 测试集仅31张照片，样本量过小，无法得出类别级别的强结论；2. 所有数据增强策略均未提升性能，方法有效性存疑；3. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；4. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化技术。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点极少。唯一可能相关的是：YOLOv8n作为轻量检测器在资源受限场景的应用，但论文并未深入探讨轻量化或部署问题。对于遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题，该论文几乎没有可借鉴的内容。

Abstract: Incorrect disposal can contaminate campus recycling streams, and a bin-mounted camera could provide feedback as an item is discarded. We evaluated whether synthetic and derived images improve a YOLOv8n detector for this view. The real dataset contained 148 campus photographs: 86 for training, 31 for validation, and 31 for testing. Twelve joint-training configurations varied the amount and source of added images. We repeated seven principal settings with four matched seeds and computed bootstrap percentile intervals over those seeds. The real-only model reached a mean mAP@0.5 of 0.691 [0.665, 0.722]. Background replacement reduced the mean to 0.560 [0.499, 0.619], isolated-object images gave 0.680 [0.644, 0.724], and the full augmentation pool gave 0.487 [0.438, 0.537]. We also tested hand-and-forearm composites because every real photo showed a held object. Two cutouts in the initial composite set came from test photographs, so we discarded that experiment, rebuilt the set with training-split cutouts, and reran all four seeds. The corrected paired difference was +0.034 [-0.063, 0.199], which does not support a reliable hand-composite effect. Single-seed transfer experiments produced source-dependent rankings between joint mixing and sequential pretraining. None of the evaluated configurations exceeded the real-only baseline. The reported intervals quantify seed variation; the 31-photo test set remains too small for strong class-specific conclusions.

</details>


### [5] [ChronoStitch: Training-Free Composition of Visual KV Memories for Long-Horizon Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19547)
*Santiram Tiwari,Nishant Sinha,Kunal Kislay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ChronoStitch，一种无需训练的方法，用于组合独立缓存的视频KV记忆，解决长视频问答中时间相位冲突问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于长视频问答（long-video QA）中的KV缓存组合问题，属于多模态大模型（VLM）和视频理解领域，与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接或间接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：长视频问答（long-video question answering）；场景：通用视频理解；传感器类型：RGB视频；目标类别：无特定目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 基于Qwen2.5-VL-3B视觉语言模型，核心是KV缓存组合与多轴RoPE坐标重映射。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。文中提到"training-free"（无需训练），但这是指组合方法无需重新训练，并非模型轻量化技术（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到相比完整联合预填充（full joint re-prefilling）加速3.3倍，但这是推理加速而非模型轻量化。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：TempCompass（时间分割子集）；评价指标：事件排序准确率（event-ordering accuracy）。

Experiments: 在Qwen2.5-VL-3B上，ChronoStitch优于朴素组合（naive composition）和仅位置修正变体，提升事件排序准确率，运行速度比完整联合预填充快3.3倍。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化技术；4. 仅在通用视频QA数据集上验证，未在遥感数据上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1）"training-free"的组合策略思想可启发在轨场景下如何复用已有特征缓存而不重复计算；2）多轴RoPE坐标重映射中对时空位置关系的处理方式，对遥感视频流中多帧目标检测的时序对齐可能有启发。但整体与课题方向偏离较大。

Abstract: Long-video question answering requires a model to preserve visual evidence over time without repeatedly reprocessing the same video. A practical approach is to store the vision-language model's internal key-value (KV) cache for each video chunk and retrieve that state at query time. However, independently cached video chunks do not compose correctly: every chunk is prefilled from local rotary position zero, so naive concatenation collides temporal phases and removes the global order required for questions about what happened first, how often events occurred, or what changed across the video. This paper presents ChronoStitch, a training-free method for composing independently stored visual KV memories. The method first re-bases stored post-rotary keys onto a global three-axis multimodal RoPE coordinate system that preserves time, height, and width structure. We show why a one-dimensional scalar re-indexing is geometrically inconsistent for visual tokens because it turns spatial order within a frame into false temporal displacement. We then address the residual content gap left by positional repair: later chunks were originally encoded without attending to earlier chunks. ChronoStitch therefore selectively recomputes a small fraction of high-deviation later-chunk visual tokens while allowing them to attend over the composed cache. On Qwen2.5-VL-3B and the temporal split of TempCompass, ChronoStitch outperforms naive composition and position-only variants, improving event-ordering accuracy while running 3.3x faster than full joint re-prefilling.

</details>


### [6] [Real-Time EEG Cap Electrode Detection for Guided Point-of-Care Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20142)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Mads Sverker Nilsson,Nicki Skafte Detlefsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段视觉系统，用YOLO检测EEG脑电帽电极并验证其解剖位置，主要面向医疗/神经科学应用，与遥感卫星无关。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：EEG cap电极检测与解剖位置验证；场景：医疗/神经科学，webcam实时流；传感器类型：普通webcam（可见光）；目标类别：EEG电极（单类检测）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 两阶段系统：Stage 1 - 单类YOLO检测器（YOLOv10n backbone）定位电极；Stage 2 - 几何阶段，基于面部 landmarks 将检测结果分配给命名的10-20系统电极角色。

Onboard deployability: 在commodity CPU上640px分辨率下达到19 FPS实时吞吐量。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。未涉及星上/边缘部署。

Experiments: LOSO下mAP@.5=0.94±0.07（pooled 0.96）；leave-one-cap-out下Medium和Large保持0.97，Small降至0.72±0.28；几何增强提升in-plane-roll鲁棒性和时序电极召回率；landmark-driven head crop在0.6×表观尺度下将mAP@.5从0.23提升至0.45。

Abstract: We present a two-stage vision system that detects EEG cap electrodes in a live webcam stream and validates their anatomical placement in real time. A single-class YOLO detector localises electrodes; a geometric stage assigns each detection to a named 10-20 role from facial landmarks. Evaluating under subject-disjoint leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation across five subjects wearing the clinically-validated Small/Medium/Large caps, the detector attains mAP@.5 = 0.94 +/- 0.07 across five held-out folds (0.96 pooled). A dedicated leave-one-cap-out axis, holding out every frame of a cap regardless of subject, leaves Medium and Large mAP@.5 within 0.01 of LOSO (0.97, 0.97) while Small drops to 0.72 +/- 0.28, a gap confounded with subject familiarity rather than cap style. Geometric augmentation (rotation, perspective, mixup) improves in-plane-roll robustness and temporal-electrode recall at no inference cost, and a landmark-driven head crop extends the usable distance range, lifting mAP@.5 from 0.23 to 0.45 at 0.6 x apparent scale. A compact mobile-candidate backbone (YOLOv10n) keeps the detector at real-time throughput (19 FPS) on a commodity CPU at 640 px.

</details>


### [7] [VQ-Transplant: Efficient VQ-Module Integration for Pre-trained Visual Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19575)
*Xianghong Fang,Yuan Yuan,Dehan Kong,Tim G. J. Rudner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VQ-Transplant框架，通过替换预训练VQ tokenizer中的量化模块实现即插即用，避免端到端重训练，大幅降低计算成本（95%），同时保持接近SOTA的重建质量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于视觉tokenization中的向量量化（VQ）模块移植，属于生成式视觉表示学习范畴，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：离散视觉tokenization中的量化模块移植与高效训练；场景：通用视觉表示学习（ImageNet）；传感器类型：无；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 基于VQ的tokenizer（编码器-解码器架构），核心是替换native VQ模块为新VQ模块，保持编码器-解码器参数冻结，附加轻量级decoder adaptation策略。

Lightweight method: 轻量化体现在训练效率层面：通过冻结编码器-解码器+仅训练轻量decoder adaptation（5 epochs on ImageNet-1k），将训练成本降低95%。但这不是模型推理层面的轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等），与星上部署无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注训练效率而非推理效率，不涉及边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ImageNet-1k；评价指标：重建保真度（reconstruction fidelity），具体指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在VAR等工业级模型上验证，VQ-Transplant达到接近SOTA的重建质量，训练成本降低95%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及推理阶段轻量化；3. 仅在ImageNet-1k上验证，未在遥感数据上测试；4. 未说明移植后模型的实际推理效率变化。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可参考其"冻结主干+轻量适配"的思想，但方向完全不同（VQ tokenization vs. 遥感目标检测）。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) underpins modern discrete visual tokenization. However, training quantization modules for state-of-the-art VQ-based models requires significant computational resources which, in practice, all but prevents the development of novel, cutting-edge VQ techniques under resource constraints. To address this limitation, we propose {\bf VQ-Transplant}, a simple framework that enables plug-and-play integration of new VQ modules into frozen, pre-trained tokenizers by replacing their native VQ modules. Crucially, the proposed transplantation process preserves all encoder-decoder parameters, obviating the need for costly end-to-end retraining when modifying the quantization method. To mitigate decoder-quantization mismatch, we introduce a lightweight decoder adaptation strategy (trained for only 5 epochs on ImageNet-1k) to align feature priors with the new quantization space. In our empirical evaluation, we find that VQ-Transplant allows obtaining near state-of-the-art reconstruction fidelity for industry-level models like VAR while reducing the training cost by 95\%. VQ-Transplant democratizes quantization research by enabling resource-efficient integration of novel VQ techniques while matching industry-level reconstruction performance.

</details>


### [8] [EGRNet: A Lightweight Semantic Segmentation Network with Edge-Gated Refinement and Adversarial Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19617)
*Bareera Qaseem,Mohsin Kamal,Muhammad Naveed Aman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EGRNet轻量级语义分割网络，用于城市自动驾驶场景的实时语义分割，采用深度可分离卷积、空洞残差块和边缘门控精化模块，参数量仅0.46M，在Cityscapes上达到65.28% mIoU。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及轻量化网络设计和边缘部署，但任务为语义分割（非目标检测），场景为城市自动驾驶（非遥感/卫星/航空影像），传感器为车载相机（非遥感卫星传感器）。轻量化方法（深度可分离卷积、SE注意力、门控机制）对轻量化网络设计有借鉴意义，但与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 2分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但轻量网络设计技巧（门控融合、深度可分离卷积+空洞卷积、SE注意力）有一定借鉴意义。建议快速浏览方法部分即可，无需精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：语义分割（semantic segmentation）；场景：城市自动驾驶（urban autonomous driving）；传感器：车载相机（摘要未明确说明，但Cityscapes为车载街景数据集）；目标类别：城市道路场景语义类别（人、车、道路、建筑等）。

Model architecture: EGRNet：深度可分离卷积（depthwise separable convolutions）+ 空洞残差块（dilated residual blocks）+ Edge-Gated Refinement (EGR)模块（可学习门控机制融合原始与精化特征）+ Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE)注意力机制。

Lightweight method: 轻量网络设计：深度可分离卷积降低计算量；参数量仅0.46M；设计轻量级对抗攻击检测策略。未涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS等方法。

Onboard deployability: 参数量0.46M，计算开销低，适合边缘设备部署。但摘要未说明FLOPs、延迟（latency）、显存（memory）、能耗（energy consumption）等具体部署指标，也未提及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件适配。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Cityscapes；评价指标：mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) 65.28%。

Experiments: 在Cityscapes上达到65.28% mIoU，参数量0.46M。摘要未说明与SOTA方法的详细对比结果、消融实验、速度对比等。

Limitations: 1. 非遥感/卫星场景，Cityscapes为城市街景，与遥感图像（大视场、多尺度、旋转目标、复杂背景）差异大；2. 任务为语义分割而非目标检测，不涉及bounding box回归、旋转框检测、小目标检测等；3. 未提供FLOPs、推理延迟、帧率等部署关键指标；4. 未在遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）上验证。

Ideas for my research: 1. EGR模块中的可学习门控机制（learnable gating）可用于遥感目标检测中的特征融合，例如融合多尺度特征或深浅层特征，提升小目标检测性能；2. 深度可分离卷积+空洞卷积的轻量组合可借鉴到遥感检测骨干网络设计；3. SE注意力机制在轻量网络中的集成方式可参考；4. 0.46M参数量的极轻量设计思路对星上部署有参考价值，但需将任务从分割迁移到检测，并在遥感数据集上验证。

Abstract: As autonomous systems and smart cities continue to evolve, the demand for efficient and robust scene understanding becomes increasingly critical. Semantic segmentation plays a key role in enabling autonomous vehicles to comprehend complex urban environments. However, achieving high accuracy with minimal computational cost remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we present Edge-Gated Refinement Network (EGRNet), a lightweight and efficient deep learning model designed for real-time semantic segmentation in urban scenarios. The model incorporates depthwise separable convolutions to reduce computational complexity and dilated residual blocks for capturing rich multi-scale contextual information. Additionally, we introduce a novel Edge-Gated Refinement (EGR) module, which adaptively fuses original and refined features through a learnable gating mechanism, enhancing boundary preservation and edge-sensitive regions. To further improve feature representation, Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) attention is applied across the network. With only 0.46M parameters, EGRNet achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining low computational overhead. When evaluated on the Cityscapes dataset, the model attains a mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 65.28%, demonstrating strong accuracy with minimal resource consumption. Moreover, we introduce a lightweight adversarial attack detection strategy, ensuring robustness against adversarial inputs without compromising real-time performance. By combining efficiency, accuracy, and resilience, EGRNet is well-suited for deployment on edge devices in safety-critical real-time applications.

</details>


### [9] [Pathologist Attention-Aligned Report Generation for Prostate Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19624)

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及使用了两种具有不同内部注意力机制的模型（即图像token如何集成到语言解码器中）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。仅提到推理时提取注意力图的计算开销很小（minimal computational overhead），但这不是模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文面向的是病理诊断辅助场景，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建的多模态人眼注意力数据集，包含121张前列腺WSI，标注了病理学家的多尺度视口轨迹、口头描述和光标移动。评价指标：NLP-based metrics（如BLEU/ROUGE等，摘要未具体说明）和5个临床相关报告组件的准确率（accuracy）。

Experiments: 在两种不同注意力机制的模型上进行实验，NLP指标平均提升10.9%，5个临床组件准确率平均提升19.3%。推理时模型注意力图与病理学家注意力更对齐。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 数据集规模较小（121张WSI），且为医学领域专用；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术；4. 未讨论实时推理或资源受限场景下的部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但注意力对齐（attention-alignment loss）的思想在理论上可以迁移：例如在遥感目标检测中，可以利用专家标注的视觉注意力（如舰船/飞机目标的显著区域）来约束模型注意力分布，提升检测可解释性。不过这与轻量化主题关联不大。

Abstract: The allocation of visual attention by pathologists during cancer diagnosis is a highly selective process that critically shapes the information extracted from whole-slide images (WSIs). Human attention helps medical imaging tasks such as classification and segmentation, and becomes a strong semantic cue for identifying diagnostically informative regions for report generation. In this paper, we introduce human attention into the training of pathologist report generation models. To this end, we collected a multimodal human-attention dataset of 121 prostate WSIs annotated with pathologists' multi-scale viewport trajectories synchronized with the pathologists' verbal descriptions and cursor movements for five clinically relevant components (e.g., Gleason patterns). Using this dataset, we finetune two report generation models with an attention-alignment loss that regularizes the model attention over image patches to match the distribution of pathologist attention. We evaluate our approach on prostate cancer report generation and visual question answering using two models with different internal attention mechanisms (i.e., how image tokens are integrated into the language decoder). Experiments show average gains of 10.9% on NLP-based metrics and 19.3% in accuracy across five clinically relevant report components. Further, model attention maps extracted at inference time, with minimal computational overhead, align more closely with pathologist attention, providing stronger visual support for the generated reports by highlighting the regions that most influence the output.

</details>

*Soroush Elyasi,Nasim Dadashi Serej,Julie Wall,Massoud Zolgharni*

Main category: cs.CV

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦医学超声影像（echocardiography）的左心室分割任务，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测或轻量化方法；不涉及在轨部署或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：左心室分割（left ventricular segmentation）；场景：临床超声心动图；传感器：超声（ultrasound）；目标类别：左心室。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体分割模型架构。使用了VAE（变分自编码器）提取潜在特征，以及手工设计的超声描述符和分割导出的潜在特征进行域迁移分析。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：6个超声心动图数据集（six echocardiographic datasets）；评价指标：Dice系数（Absolute Dice drop）、R-squared、MAE、Spearman rho、CMD、log-Wasserstein、log-MMD等域差异度量。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 主要发现：几何感知预处理可改善部分迁移退化；强度z归一化对数据集可分性影响极小（变化<0.005）；用不同特征表示可预测Dice下降（R-squared=0.612, MAE=0.082, Spearman rho=0.681）；不同表示下最有效的域差异度量不同。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测方法。该论文属于医学图像分析中的域泛化/域迁移分析，研究问题、场景、方法均不匹配。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能间接参考的是：该论文对域迁移来源的系统性分析方法（区分几何因素、强度因素等）在遥感域适应场景中可能有方法论上的启发，但与本课题核心（轻量化+在轨目标检测）无直接关联。

Abstract: Cross-dataset generalisation remains a major barrier to clinical deployment of echocardiographic left ventricular segmentation, yet the sources of this shift are rarely disentangled. We examined whether transfer degradation could be estimated before deployment using handcrafted ultrasound descriptors, VAE latent features, and segmentation-derived latent features across six echocardiographic datasets. Geometry-aware preprocessing substantially improved several poor transfer cases, suggesting that much of the apparent domain shift reflects field-of-view and framing inconsistencies rather than intrinsic acoustic differences alone. Intensity z-normalisation changed dataset separability by less than 0.005, indicating that brightness and contrast are not the dominant shift axis. Absolute Dice drop on held-out source-target pairs was predicted with an R-squared value of 0.612, an MAE of 0.082, and a Spearman rho of 0.681. The variant without LV and fan-shaped features retained approximately 70% of this explanatory power, supporting mask-free transfer-risk monitoring. The most informative discrepancy measure depended on the representation, with CMD strongest in z-normalised handcrafted features, with an absolute r of approximately 0.86 and an R-squared value of approximately 0.70; log-Wasserstein strongest in VAE space, with an r of approximately -0.90 and an R-squared value of approximately 0.81; and log-MMD strongest in LV-segmentation latent features, with an r of approximately -0.92 and an R-squared value of approximately 0.84. Apparent vendor effects were largely dataset-confounded. Echocardiographic domain shift is therefore structured and measurable, and its impact on segmentation can be partly reduced through geometry-aware preprocessing and anticipated using representation-specific transfer-risk estimation.

</details>


### [11] [A Unified Variational Framework for Deep Weakly Supervised Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19669)
*Yin King Chu,Lingfeng Li,Sung Ha Kang,Jianping Zhang,Xue-Cheng Tai*

Abstract: We propose a unified variational framework for image segmentation under sparse pixel-level supervision. Our method is based on a simplex-constrained Potts model with a smooth perimeter regularizer, yielding a convex, smooth energy functional that can be used as a training loss in weakly supervised deep learning paradigms or optimized efficiently using iterative methods. Sparse labels are incorporated into the data fidelity term by constructing a fuzzy membership function via a function extension problem in a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS), which can effectively capture inhomogeneous intensity statistics. The derived discrete loss for training standard networks demonstrates robustness and consistent improvements over non-training and partial cross-entropy (PCE) baselines in experiments, achieving comparable performance without requiring ground-truth segmentation images.

</details>

Model architecture: 采用state-of-the-art卷积骨干网络（具体名称摘要未说明）结合Positional Encoding（位置编码）机制，用于建模椎间盘的解剖上下文信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PhenSPINE（自建），包含16,813张MRI图像，来自250名患者，涵盖4种标准MRI序列。评价指标：Macro F1-score，最佳结果为50.31%（Sagittal T2-weighted序列）。

Experiments: 对比了单序列与多序列融合策略，发现单序列Sagittal T2-weighted优于多序列融合，原因是多序列图像受周围解剖区域噪声干扰严重。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或部署方法。数据集和任务均属于医学影像领域。

Abstract: The accurate diagnosis of spinal pathologies depends heavily on radiological interpretation, yet automated systems are hindered by the lack of diverse, high-quality benchmarks. In this study, we present PhenSPINE, a Magnetic Resonance Imaging dataset comprising 16,813 images from 250 patients, curated to facilitate advanced deep learning research. We propose a robust diagnostic benchmark that integrates state-of-theart convolutional backbones with a Positional Encoding mechanism to explicitly model the anatomical context of intervertebral discs. Evaluating across four standard MRI sequences, our experiments demonstrate that the Sagittal T2-weighted sequence offers the most robust diagnostic value, achieving a superior Macro F1-score of 50.31%. We find that multisequence fusion strategies yield inferior performance compared to this single-sequence baseline, as the images across sequences in our dataset are significantly compromised by noise interference from surrounding anatomical regions. This work establishes a robust baseline and offers critical insights into sequence selection for spine analysis.

</details>


### [13] [SafeGen: Goal-Conditioned Video Diffusion of Safety-Critical Scenarios for VLM-Based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19701)
*Jiangfan Liu,Zexuan Cui,Tianyuan Zhang,Zonglei Jing,Zonghao Ying,Yaoyuan Zhang,Jiakai Wang,Xiaoqi Jiang,Aishan Liu,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SafeGen，一种基于目标条件扩散框架的安全关键场景生成方法，用于评估视觉语言模型自动驾驶系统（VLMADs）的安全性，通过生成从正常驾驶到灾难性结局的连贯视频轨迹来测试系统在罕见人车交互场景下的表现。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自动驾驶（AD）系统的安全评估场景生成，涉及视觉语言模型（VLM）和扩散模型，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：安全关键场景生成（safety-critical scenario generation）；应用场景：自动驾驶系统（AD systems）中的脆弱道路使用者（vulnerable road users）交互；传感器类型：视觉语言模型（VLM）驱动的自动驾驶系统；目标类别：人-车交互动态。摘要未说明遥感相关任务或场景。

Model architecture: SafeGen框架包含两个核心模块：(1) Context Grounded End State Reasoning — 利用VLM分析正常驾驶上下文并推断潜在脆弱性，生成结构化端状态规范；(2) End State Conditioned Video Evolution — 通过深度感知几何投影（depth-aware geometric projection）实例化高风险智能体，再通过边界条件扩散（boundary-conditioned diffusion）生成具有一致运动模式的中间帧。整体为goal-conditioned diffusion framework。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作为场景生成而非模型部署，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 在3个VLMADs（视觉语言模型自动驾驶系统）上进行实验。评价指标：Judge Overall Score（使用VLM裁判评估VLMADs的理解和决策能力）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 相比SoTA基线，SafeGen在Judge Overall Score上平均提升24.25%；使用SafeGen生成数据微调VLMAD后，在真实驾驶场景中性能平均提升15.9%。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴；(2) 摘要未说明生成视频的分辨率、帧率、计算开销等；(3) 依赖VLM进行端状态推理，计算成本可能较高；(4) 仅针对自动驾驶场景，未讨论泛化到其他领域。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：(1) 目标条件扩散（goal-conditioned diffusion）的思路或许可用于遥感数据增强或难例生成，但需大幅改造；(2) VLM用于上下文推理的思路在遥感场景理解中可能有潜在应用，但与本课题的轻量化目标检测方向偏离较远。

Abstract: VLMs are increasingly deployed in AD systems, creating an urgent need for rigorous safety evaluation under rare yet safety-critical scenarios. Among these, interactions with vulnerable road users represent a major source of real-world failures. However, existing safety-critical scenario generation methods predominantly rely on simulator-based pipelines, which suffer from a substantial sim-to-real gap and often fail to capture realistic, diverse, and unforeseen human-vehicle interaction dynamics. We present SafeGen, a goal-conditioned diffusion framework for safety-critical scenario generation in VLMADs. Our key insight is to formulate scenario generation as a goal-conditioned diffusion process, where a predefined catastrophic end-state serves as a strong supervisory signal, guiding the generation of temporally coherent video trajectories that naturally evolve toward safety-critical outcomes. Building on this formulation, we introduce Context Grounded End State Reasoning, which leverages VLMs to analyze benign driving contexts and infer latent vulnerabilities in human-vehicle interactions, producing structured end-state specifications that induce high-risk scenarios. Conditioned on these targets, we further propose End State Conditioned Video Evolution, which grounds semantic threats into physically plausible visual dynamics. Specifically, we instantiate high-risk agents within the scene via depth-aware geometric projection, followed by boundary-conditioned diffusion to generate intermediate frames with consistent motion patterns and temporal coherence. Extensive experiments across 3 VLMADs demonstrate that SafeGen increases the Judge Overall Score, a metric using a VLM judge to evaluate VLMADs' understanding and decision-making, by 24.25% on average compared to SoTA baselines. Furthermore, fine-tuning a VLMAD improves performance in real-world driving scenes by an average of 15.9%.

</details>


### [14] [Point-Selection Fine-Tuning Framework for Robust Point Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19711)
*Da Li,Chang Ma,Dongfu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PSFT点选择微调框架，通过选择低影响点抑制噪声、轻量提示注入和特征滤波，提升3D点云在噪声下的鲁棒性，同时保持参数高效。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究3D点云识别在噪声下的鲁棒微调方法，与遥感卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关系。虽然涉及参数高效微调（轻量化思想），但场景是3D点云分类，非遥感图像/卫星目标检测。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：3D点云分类的鲁棒微调；场景：通用3D点云识别（ModelNet-C, ModelNet40-C, ScanObjectNN-C）；传感器类型：3D点云（非遥感光学/SAR/高光谱）；目标类别：通用3D物体（非遥感特定目标）。

Model architecture: PSFT框架包含三部分：(1) 基于预池化特征的点影响力估计与自适应选择模块；(2) 基于选择子集的提示生成分支，生成层间提示token注入冻结backbone；(3) 轻量特征滤波器，含瓶颈MLP变换和Beta门控残差混合，用于精炼patch-token表示。

Lightweight method: 参数高效微调（parameter-efficient fine-tuning）：冻结backbone，仅通过提示token注入进行轻量下游适配；轻量特征滤波器（bottleneck MLP + Beta门控）。但未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及"parameter-efficient"和"lightweight feature filter"，但无具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ModelNet-C, ModelNet40-C（点云分类鲁棒性基准），ScanObjectNN-C；评价指标：corruption error（CE，腐败误差）；预训练backbone：ULIP-2, Uni3D-B等3D预训练模型。

Experiments: PSFT在所有测试的3D预训练backbone上一致降低ModelNet-C和ModelNet40-C的corruption error；在ScanObjectNN-C上使用ULIP-2和Uni3D-B取得最强结果。与全微调、朴素参数高效微调等策略对比。

Limitations: (1) 仅针对3D点云分类任务，非目标检测；(2) 非遥感场景，未涉及遥感图像特性（多光谱、SAR、大尺度变化等）；(3) 无部署相关指标；(4) 方法依赖预训练模型质量。

Ideas for my research: (1) 参数高效微调（冻结backbone+提示注入）的思路可借鉴到遥感目标检测模型的轻量化适配中，减少在轨更新参数量；(2) 特征选择/过滤的思想可用于遥感图像中噪声抑制（如SAR斑点噪声）；(3) Beta门控残差混合机制可尝试用于遥感检测模型的特征融合模块轻量化。但整体相关性弱，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Noisy and corrupted points can substantially degrade point cloud recognition performance, especially under challenging corruption settings. In particular, full fine-tuning of 3D pre-trained models may amplify the influence of outliers and overwrite robustness priors learned during pre-training, while naive parameter-efficient adaptation remains sensitive to corrupted tokens. To address this issue, we propose PSFT, a point-selection fine-tuning framework that improves robustness while remaining parameter-efficient. PSFT first estimates point-wise influence from pre-pooling features and adaptively retains minimally influential points to suppress outliers. Based on the selected subset, a prompt generation branch predicts layer-wise prompt tokens and injects them into a frozen backbone for lightweight downstream adaptation. To further mitigate residual noise after selection, we append a lightweight feature filter with bottleneck MLP transformation and Beta-gated residual blending to refine patch-token representations before prediction. Extensive experiments show that PSFT consistently reduces corruption error on ModelNet-C and ModelNet40-C across all tested 3D pre-trained backbones, while achieving the strongest ScanObjectNN-C results with ULIP-2 and Uni3D-B among the evaluated tuning strategies. Our implementation can be found at https://github.com/CVChMA/PSFT/tree/master.

</details>

Abstract: Pain is a complex and pervasive phenomenon affecting a large percentage of the population, and accurate assessment is essential for effective clinical management and intervention. Computational pain recognition systems enable continuous monitoring, support clinical decision-making, and help mitigate pain-related distress and functional decline. This study introduces a unified tokenization framework for heterogeneous 3D modalities in pain recognition that provides a single processing pipeline across behavioral and brain-activity 3D data, without requiring separate architectures for each modality or handcrafted inductive biases. The framework preserves spatial, temporal, and time--frequency structure while mapping diverse inputs into a shared token space. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach effectively processes facial videos and fNIRS data in both raw-signal and spectrogram-based representations. On the AI4Pain benchmark dataset, the proposed framework achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining high computational efficiency and enabling real-time assessment on both GPU and CPU hardware.

</details>

Experiments: 在AI4Pain测试集上达到56.00%准确率，为所比较方法中的最高报告值。四象限配置与全脸输入使用相同总像素预算，但准确率更高。单象限区域仅使用1/4像素，仍保持竞争力。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接参考的是"空间重组（spatial reorganization）"思路——将输入划分为子区域分别处理，但这在遥感目标检测中已有类似patch-based方法，且该论文不涉及检测任务，参考价值极低。

Abstract: Automatic pain assessment from facial video remains challenging due to the spatial heterogeneity of pain-related facial cues. This study proposes ReFace, a spatial reorganization pipeline that divides facial input into four spatial quadrants before tokenization, rather than processing the entire face as a single region. Evaluated on the AI4Pain dataset, the proposed approach achieves $56.00\%$ accuracy on the test set using video only, achieving the highest reported accuracy under the fixed AI4Pain benchmark protocol among the compared methods. Notably, the four-quadrant configuration processes the same total pixel budget as the full-face input, yet achieves higher accuracy, suggesting that spatial reorganization can improve performance under the proposed tokenization design. A single quadrant region, processing just one quarter of those pixels, remains competitive at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>

*Ioannis Kyprakis,Stefanos Gkikas,Eric Nichols,Yu Fang,Manolis Tsiknakis*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：疼痛定位（pain localization）；场景：临床非语言患者疼痛评估；传感器类型：可穿戴生理信号（EDA、BVP、呼吸、血氧饱和度）；目标类别：三类疼痛定位。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AI4Pain 2026 Challenge dataset，包含65名受试者在TENS诱导疼痛下的四模态可穿戴信号。评价指标：宏F1（macro-F1），疼痛检测F1=0.815，疼痛定位F1=0.552。

Experiments: Extremely Randomized Trees取得最高宏F1=0.539，超过最佳深度模型7.4个百分点。EDA频谱特征是最具判别力的特征。所有模型在疼痛检测（F1=0.815）与定位（F1=0.552）之间存在26个百分点的差距。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。摘要指出10秒分辨率下外周自主神经通路的解剖弥散性导致了疼痛定位的性能天花板。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于生物医学信号处理领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何核心技术问题。

Abstract: Automatic pain localization, which involves identifying the anatomical origin of pain from peripheral physiological signals without patient self-report, is a clinically critical but largely unaddressed problem, particularly for non-verbal patients. This paper presents a systematic comparison of classical feature engineering and deep sequence learning for subject-independent three-class pain localization using the AI4Pain 2026 Challenge dataset, which comprises four synchronously recorded wearable modalities: electrodermal activity, blood volume pulse, respiration, and peripheral oxygen saturation recorded from 65 participants under controlled TENS-induced pain. A 115-dimensional hand-crafted feature set spanning time-domain, frequency-domain, modality-specific, and cross-modal descriptors is benchmarked against end-to-end deep architectures. Extremely Randomized Trees achieves the highest macro-F1 of 0.539, outperforming the best deep model by 7.4 percentage points, with EDA spectral features emerging as the dominant discriminators. A consistent 26-point gap between pain detection (F1\,=\,0.815) and localization (F1\,=\,0.552) across all models points to a fundamental ceiling imposed by the anatomical diffuseness of peripheral autonomic pathways at 10-second resolution.

</details>


### [18] [Efficient Tracking and Understanding Object Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19743)
*Yihong Sun,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FluxGraph提出一种基于SAM2内部多掩码不一致性作为轻量触发器的反应式视频目标跟踪方法，在VOST等数据集上实现3.3-10.7倍加速，但面向通用视频理解，与遥感/卫星/在轨部署无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究通用视频中的目标跟踪（object tracking through state transformations），不涉及遥感图像、卫星平台、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景。轻量化方法（使用SAM2内部多掩码不一致性作为触发器）虽有一定加速效果，但面向的是通用视频理解任务，且未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化技术，也未讨论星上/边缘部署约束。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对"反应式触发"这一算法加速思路感兴趣，可作为跨领域启发参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频目标跟踪（video object tracking）与状态变换检测（state transformation detection）。应用场景：通用视频理解（VOST、VSCOS、M³-VOS、DAVIS17等数据集）。传感器类型：通用RGB视频。目标类别：通用视频中的物体。与遥感无关。

Model architecture: FluxGraph基于TubeletGraph改进，核心是使用SAM2（Segment Anything Model 2）的内部多掩码不一致性（multi-mask disagreement）作为轻量触发器，替代TubeletGraph中密集的逐帧实体分割和全场景实体跟踪。摘要未说明完整网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 轻量化方法：利用SAM2内部多掩码不一致性作为反应式触发器（reactive trigger），避免逐帧密集计算和全场景实体跟踪。这是一种算法层面的计算加速策略，而非模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）等典型轻量化方法。加速比：VOST上~3.3×，VSCOS/M³-VOS/DAVIS17上3.7-10.7×。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署关键指标。原始TubeletGraph在VOST上每目标帧约4.4秒，FluxGraph加速后仍远未达到实时（视频通常需≥30fps），更未讨论星上部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件适配）。不具备在轨部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：VOST（Video Object Segmentation and Tracking）、VSCOS、M³-VOS、DAVIS17。评价指标：跟踪性能（tracking performance）和状态图质量（state graph quality），摘要未说明具体指标名称（如J&F、mAP等）。

Experiments: 主要结果：FluxGraph在VOST上比TubeletGraph快~3.3×且跟踪性能提升、状态图质量保持；在VSCOS、M³-VOS、DAVIS17上加速3.7-10.7×且性能保持。摘要未说明消融实验、对比方法细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨部署完全无关；2. 轻量化仅体现在算法级加速，未涉及模型压缩技术；3. 加速后仍远未达到实时推理要求；4. 依赖SAM2这一大型基础模型，本身计算开销大，不适合资源受限的星上平台；5. 未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟等量化指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 反应式触发（reactive trigger）的思想——只在必要时激活计算——可借鉴到遥感在轨场景中，例如只在检测到感兴趣区域变化时才触发高分辨率目标检测，减少无效计算；2. 利用模型内部中间特征（如SAM2的多掩码不一致性）作为轻量判断信号，类似思路可用于遥感图像中快速筛选包含目标的图块；3. 但整体借鉴价值有限，因为该工作面向通用视频且依赖大模型，与星上轻量化目标检测的核心需求（小模型、低FLOPs、硬件友好）差距较大。

Abstract: Tracking objects through state transformations is essential for understanding real-world dynamics. However, existing methods are computationally expensive. TubeletGraph recently showed impressive capabilities, but its inference cost (~$4.4$ seconds per object-frame on VOST) precludes any real-time deployment possibilities. We observe that TubeletGraph's overhead arises from building a spatiotemporal partition of the input video: (1) entity segmentation is computed densely for every frame regardless of whether a transformation occurs, and (2) every entity in the scene is tracked, scaling cost with scene complexity rather than the number of transformations of interest. To address both, we propose FluxGraph, a reactive variant that uses SAM2's internal multi-mask disagreement as a lightweight trigger for transformation detection, and removes the need for tracking all entities in the given video. FluxGraph is ~$3.3\times$ faster than TubeletGraph on VOST while improving tracking performance and preserving state graph quality. Furthermore, we also observe consistent speedups of $3.7-10.7\times$ across VSCOS, M$^3$-VOS, and DAVIS17 while maintaining performance. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/YihongSun/FluxGraph.

</details>

*Kwonyoung Ryu,In-Jae Lee,Jonghyun Jin,Hyunjee Lee,Jongmin Lee,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于室内3D场景理解（ScanNet, Replica）中的全景分割与视角合成，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：全景分割（panoptic segmentation）+ 新视角合成（novel view synthesis）；场景：室内3D场景（ScanNet, Replica）；传感器类型：RGB相机；目标类别：室内物体（panoptic labels）。

Model architecture: 大视角合成模型（large view synthesis model），基于cross-view attention，无显式3D表示。采用冻结的预训练模型，将全景标签编码为二进制通道表示后输入模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。模型为大视角合成模型，计算开销可能较大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ScanNet, Replica；评价指标：分割质量（segmentation quality）、新视角合成PSNR（比Gaussian-based方法高7 dB以上）。

Experiments: 在ScanNet上分割质量与需要显式3D重建的Gaussian方法相当，新视角合成PSNR高出7 dB以上；在Replica上零样本迁移超越对比方法。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感场景，无法直接用于遥感目标检测；2. 模型为大视角合成模型，计算量和参数量可能很大，未讨论轻量化；3. 依赖输入视图的全景标签，在遥感场景中获取成本高；4. 未讨论实时推理或边缘/星上部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1. 利用预训练模型的跨视图对应关系进行标签传播的思路，或许可探索用于遥感多视角图像中的目标标注传播；2. 冻结大模型、仅编码标签输入的方式，可减少特定任务训练开销。但整体与遥感在轨轻量化目标检测方向差距较大。

Abstract: Large view synthesis models synthesize novel views through cross-view attention without explicit 3D representations, and recent studies have shown that they learn accurate spatial correspondence from RGB supervision alone. We observe that this correspondence generalizes beyond appearance. When non-photorealistic signals such as binary encoded panoptic labels are passed through the model, they are propagated to novel views with consistent spatial structure. These results indicate that the correspondence learned for RGB view synthesis can also propagate view-independent per-pixel labels. From this observation, we present the first work to extend large view synthesis models beyond appearance rendering to 3D scene understanding. We propose a panoptic segmentation pipeline that reuses a frozen view synthesis model to propagate panoptic labels from input views to novel views, without 3D reconstruction or any segmentation-specific training of the view synthesis model. Given panoptic labels on the input views, we encode them into binary channel representations and pass them through the same model to render target-view segmentation. On ScanNet, our method achieves segmentation quality on par with Gaussian based approaches requiring explicit 3D reconstruction, while outperforming them in novel view synthesis by more than 7 dB. The label propagation also transfers across datasets, surpassing these approaches on Replica without any fine-tuning.

</details>

Experiments: 主要结果：GBA或TEMPO总体精度最高（取决于评价标准）；所有产品在非洲和亚洲精度显著下降；大多数产品在高密度城市区域精度下降明显；按地理位置、人口密度、收入组进行了分层精度分析。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 不涉及任何检测模型或轻量化技术；3. 不提供模型部署相关指标；4. 仅聚焦于数据产品精度评估，无方法学创新。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于遥感数据产品验证领域，不涉及目标检测模型设计或轻量化技术，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无参考价值。

Abstract: Geo-spatial rasters of building footprint area are useful for a variety of tasks, such as monitoring urbanization, improving energy efficiency, and tracking greenhouse gas emissions. There are now multiple global building raster datasets, however there lacks an independent, comprehensive, and fair assessment of their accuracy. In this work, we evaluate the accuracy of four major global building products: Global Human Settlement Layer (GHSL), Microsoft's TEMPO (TEMPO), The Global Building Atlas (GBA), and Overture. As ground truth for assessing their accuracy, we use ORBITaL-Net, a globally diverse dataset of manually labeled building footprints. To ensure fairness, we evaluate products on grids of multiple spatial resolutions, and several conventional performance metrics. Our results indicate that either GBA or TEMPO generally achieves the highest overall accuracy, depending upon the particular evaluation criteria. We also stratify the accuracy of each product by several factors: geographic location, population density, and income groups. The results reveal that product accuracy can sometimes vary significantly with respect to these factors. Notably, all products are significantly less accurate in Africa and Asia. Most products also suffer significant accuracy reduction in high-density urban areas.

</details>


### [21] [DRGBT-1K: A Large-scale High-quality Benchmark for Dynamic RGBT Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19772)
*Zhaodong Ding,Chenglong Li,Zeyu Ding,Futian Wang,Jin Tang*

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个面向动态RGBT跟踪的大规模基准数据集DRGBT-1K，用于评估多模态目标跟踪方法在传感器模态和观测平台动态变化下的鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及UAV（无人机）平台和RGBT多模态数据，与遥感/卫星有一定交集，但核心任务是**多模态目标跟踪（tracking）**而非目标检测（detection），且不涉及任何轻量化、在轨部署或星上推理内容。属于泛视觉跟踪方向，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题弱相关。

Reading priority: 1分（不推荐精读）。与课题方向弱相关：任务为跟踪而非检测，不涉及轻量化/部署，不涉及卫星平台。仅当研究多模态遥感跟踪或跨平台协同感知时可作参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态目标跟踪（RGBT tracking），非目标检测。场景：动态RGBT跟踪，涉及UAV和手持设备采集的跨平台、跨视角、跨模态数据。传感器类型：可见光（RGB）+热红外（T）。目标类别：24类，包括行人、车辆等常见目标。摘要未说明是否包含遥感特定目标（如舰船、飞机）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文主要贡献是数据集和基准评估，而非提出新的模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、边缘部署或星上部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DRGBT-1K（自建），包含1,045个序列、795K RGBT帧对，由UAV和手持RGBT设备采集。另有未对齐版本DRGBT-1K和UGVT-1K（UAV-ground collaborative tracking）。评价指标：密集边界框标注、目标类别标签、挑战属性标签、帧级模态标签和平台标签。摘要未说明具体跟踪评价指标（如成功率、精度等）。

Experiments: 评估了20种代表性多模态跟踪方法，包括传统RGBT跟踪器、模态缺失RGBT跟踪器和DRGBT跟踪器。提供了在线评估平台和排行榜。摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 论文核心是数据集和基准构建，未提出新的轻量化或高效推理方法。2. 任务为跟踪而非检测，与课题方向存在任务差异。3. 未涉及卫星平台或星上部署场景。4. 未讨论模型效率、参数量、计算量等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 该论文的**跨平台/跨模态动态场景设计思路**可借鉴：在轨遥感目标检测也可考虑多源传感器（如SAR+光学）的动态融合场景。2. UAV-ground协同跟踪的**UGVT-1K数据集**可作为遥感多平台协同感知的参考。3. 论文对**模态缺失（modality-missing）**场景的评估设计，可启发在轨检测中部分传感器失效时的鲁棒性研究。4. 但整体借鉴价值有限，因不涉及轻量化和检测任务。

Abstract: Dynamic RGBT (DRGBT) tracking aims to continuously localize a target when the available sensing modalities and observation platforms vary over time. Compared with conventional RGBT tracking with fixed RGBT inputs and a fixed observation platform, DRGBT tracking is more consistent with real-world collaborative perception systems, where targets may be observed by heterogeneous sensors from different viewpoints. However, existing benchmarks are still insufficient for systematically evaluating tracker robustness under real dynamic modality variations and cross-platform transitions. To address this limitation, we make the following contributions. 1) We construct DRGBT-1K, a large-scale high-quality benchmark for DRGBT tracking. It contains 1,045 sequences captured entirely in real-world scenarios and 795K RGBT frame pairs collected using UAVs and handheld RGBT devices, encompassing diverse real-world scenes, pronounced viewpoint changes, modality variations, and target appearance discontinuities. 2) We provide comprehensive annotations for fine-grained evaluation, including dense bounding boxes, target category labels, challenge attributes, frame-level modality labels and platform labels. DRGBT-1K covers 24 target categories, more than 15 scene types and 15 challenge attributes. 3) We establish a comprehensive benchmark by evaluating 20 representative multimodal tracking methods, including conventional RGBT trackers, modality-missing RGBT trackers, and DRGBT trackers under a unified evaluation protocol. 4) We release an unaligned version of DRGBT-1K and derive UGVT-1K to support broader research on unaligned multimodal tracking and UAV-ground collaborative tracking. 5) We develop an online evaluation platform for DRGBT-1K and provide a leaderboard that collects all methods evaluated on this benchmark.

</details>

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于3D场景编辑（text-driven 3D scene editing），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：文本驱动的3D场景编辑；场景：多物体/单物体3D场景；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的编辑框架，包含两个核心模块：Attention-Guided Editing Camera Placement (ACP) 和 Multi-view Attention Alignment (MAA)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。论文提到使用仅5个编辑视图，延迟比现有方法降低7倍，但这属于编辑效率提升，并非模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。延迟降低7x是针对3D编辑流程，非推理部署延迟。

Datasets and metrics: 实验场景：多物体和单物体场景；评价指标：用户偏好（instruction fidelity, multi-view consistency, editing locality）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 在multi-object和single-object场景上，该方法在指令保真度、多视图一致性、编辑局部性方面获得最高用户偏好，仅用5个编辑视图，延迟比现有方法降低7倍。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化或边缘部署；4. 仅在3D场景编辑任务上验证，未涉及遥感图像理解。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。注意力引导的相机放置（ACP）思想在遥感多视角融合或卫星编队协同观测场景中可能有启发，但与本课题核心方向（在轨目标检测模型轻量化）关联度极低。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D scene editing with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) typically applies a 2D diffusion editor to views rendered from fixed training cameras, limiting both the spatial coverage of edits and the user's freedom to target specific objects in complex scenes. We present LB-Edit, a framework that addresses two coupled problems: where to place editing cameras for localized edits, and how to make per-view edits agree with one another so that the 3D scene remains consistent after fine-tuning. First, Attention-Guided Editing Camera Placement (ACP) probes the diffusion model's self- and cross-attention at multiple candidate camera distances to find where attention is well-contained in the region of interest, then places a compact, geometrically diverse editing camera set at that attention-optimal distance. Second, Multi-view Attention Alignment (MAA) steers the editor toward the same edit across views along two axes: it aligns appearance by sharing self-attention features via token-level correspondence, and aligns spatial location by lifting cross-attention maps onto the 3D Gaussians as a shared 3D attention field, suppressing both appearance and spatial drift. Experiments on multi-object and single-object scenes show that our method achieves the highest user preference in instruction fidelity, multi-view consistency, and editing locality, using as few as 5 editing views and reducing latency by up to 7x over existing methods.

</details>


### [23] [Frequency-Hierarchical Active k-Space Sampling for Diagnostic MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19779)
*Ruru Xu,Kian Anvari Hamedani,Zhikai Yang,Ilkay Oksuz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向加速MRI的自适应采样框架HieraSample，利用Mamba策略网络和余弦退火课程学习实现分层频率采样，提升高加速倍数下的诊断精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是医学影像（MRI）加速采样问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。虽然"active sampling"和"轻量化"在概念上有一定类比性，但应用场景和技术路线完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：MRI加速采样（active sampling for accelerated MRI）；场景：医学影像诊断（ACL诊断）；传感器类型：MRI（磁共振成像）；目标类别：ACL（前交叉韧带）损伤及严重程度分级。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: HieraSample框架包含：余弦退火课程学习策略（cosine-annealed curriculum，从20x加速逐步降至4x）、Mamba-based策略网络（用于选择高频坐标）、双分类器（疾病分类器+严重程度分类器）提取特征。与遥感目标检测模型架构无关。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文关注的是采样策略优化而非模型压缩/加速。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文面向MRI设备而非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：fastMRI+ knee benchmark；评价指标：AUC（ACL诊断和严重程度评估）。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）无关。

Experiments: 在fastMRI+ knee基准上，HieraSample在4x-10x加速倍数下匹配了全采样oracle的ACL诊断性能，在ACL严重程度评估上比近期Cartesian基线方法提升了最多20.4 AUC点。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。局限性包括：仅针对MRI模态、仅针对ACL单一诊断任务、未讨论模型计算开销和部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下概念可能有间接启发：（1）分层采样思想——遥感图像中不同频率分量（低频背景vs高频细节）也可采用差异化处理策略；（2）任务驱动的采样/特征选择——可类比遥感目标检测中根据任务需求动态选择特征通道或分辨率。但整体而言，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Active sampling for accelerated MRI must distribute a tight sampling budget across spatial frequencies that carry very different kinds of information. Low frequencies hold most of the anatomical context; high frequencies carry the fine details that drive pathology assessment. Existing active samplers either treat both regions identically or restrict the action space to entire Cartesian rows, which forces a poor compromise at high acceleration. We propose HieraSample, a task-driven framework built around this hierarchy. A cosine-annealed curriculum lowers the acceleration factor from 20x to 4x across 80 acquisition steps while keeping a fully-sampled low-frequency disk at every step; a Mamba-based policy then picks individual high-frequency coordinates from features extracted by dual disease and severity classifiers. The reward is the per-sample reduction in class-weighted cross-entropy after each action, so a positive reward corresponds directly to a more confident correct prediction. On the fastMRI+ knee benchmark, HieraSample matches the fully-sampled oracle on ACL diagnosis from 4x to 10x acceleration, and improves on a recent Cartesian baseline by as much as 20.4 AUC points on ACL severity.

</details>


### [24] [WASABI: Whole-graph Assignment-based Stabilizer for lAne topology By Inter-frame tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19781)
*Tetsuhiro Uchida,Myu Sasaki,Kensho Nakajima,Yasuhiro Shimada,Toru Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WASABI是一个面向自动驾驶场景的实时后处理管线，用于稳定车道拓扑（车道段及其连接关系）的检测输出，通过联合跟踪和拓扑感知精化提升帧内和帧间稳定性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦自动驾驶场景下的车道拓扑稳定化后处理，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测模型本身的轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）；不涉及星上/边缘部署。虽然涉及实时性约束（10 Hz / 20 ms），但场景和任务与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分，不推荐阅读。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于自动驾驶场景下的车道拓扑后处理工作。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：车道拓扑（lane topology）稳定化后处理；应用场景：自动驾驶（autonomous driving），BEV视角；传感器类型：车载传感器（onboard sensors）；目标类别：车道段（lane segments）及其连接关系（LCLC）。不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: WASABI是一个后处理管线（post-processing pipeline），包含：段跟踪与连接性联合跟踪（segment tracking with connectivity）、噪声鲁棒的拓扑感知精化（noise-robust topology-aware refinement）、资源受限实时设计（resource-constrained real-time design）。不是端到端检测模型，而是对已有感知输出的后处理稳定模块。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。仅提及"resource-constrained real-time design"以满足10 Hz/20 ms的实时约束，但未给出具体轻量化技术细节。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提及实时约束：10 Hz / 20 ms / up to 200 input lanes。但未给出参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。场景为车载（onboard），非星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：内部验证数据（internal validation data, 16 sequences）。评价指标：LCLC detection F1（从0.834提升至0.948）、centerline lateral error（从2.50 m降至0.95 m）、detection false-positives降低24.6%、LCLC toggle rate降低63.3%、boundary-label flicker rate降低30.2%。无遥感标准数据集。

Experiments: 在16个序列的内部验证数据上，WASABI将LCLC检测F1从0.834提升至0.948（+0.114, +13.6%），中心线横向误差从2.50 m降至0.95 m，检测假阳性降低24.6%。时序稳定性方面：LCLC toggle rate降低63.3%，边界标签闪烁率降低30.2%。无遥感场景实验。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对自动驾驶车道拓扑任务，与遥感目标检测无关；2. 仅使用内部数据集（16 sequences），未在公开遥感基准上验证；3. 未涉及模型轻量化核心方法；4. 后处理管线而非检测模型本身，对模型压缩的借鉴意义有限；5. 未给出参数量、FLOPs等量化指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1. 后处理稳定化思路——遥感在轨检测中也可考虑对时序检测结果进行帧间稳定化（如跟踪+拓扑精化）以提升输出质量；2. "resource-constrained real-time design"的设计理念可参考，但缺乏具体实现细节。总体而言，该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires understanding the road as a graph of drivable lanes and their connectivity, beyond the ego lane alone, to follow routes through intersections and reason about cross- and merging-traffic. Recent perception models infer such lane topology, i.e., lane segments together with their inter-lane connectivity (LCLC), from onboard sensors over a 360-degree BEV view. Due to neural perception's imperfections, their outputs retain structural instabilities such as missed detections, lost or incorrect LCLC, over-detection, and label flicker. This paper presents WASABI, a real-time post-processing pipeline that stabilizes lane topology outputs both within and across frames by treating lane segments and their LCLC connectivity as joint tracking targets, under onboard real-time constraints (10 Hz / 20 ms / up to 200 input lanes). The pipeline integrates segment tracking with connectivity, noise-robust topology-aware refinement, and a resource-constrained real-time design. On internal validation data (16 sequences), WASABI improves LCLC detection F1 from 0.834 to 0.948 (+0.114, +13.6%) and reduces centerline lateral error from 2.50 m to 0.95 m, while reducing detection false-positives by 24.6%. Temporal-stability metrics on the same data show LCLC toggle rate reduced by 63.3% and boundary-label flicker rate by 30.2%, confirming across-frame stabilization beyond per-frame accuracy.

</details>


### [25] [Physics-Aware Complex-Valued State Space Model with Scattering-Prior Feature Modulation for PolSAR Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19787)
*Fangyan Zhang,Fan Zhang,Shiqi Zhou,Jun Ni,Carlos López-Martínez,Qiang Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CV-SSMNet，一种物理感知的复值状态空间网络，通过散射感知特征调制实现PolSAR图像分类，在复值域中捕获长程空间依赖并融合极化散射先验知识。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与本研究方向基本无关。虽然涉及遥感（PolSAR图像分类），但任务类型是**图像分类**而非**目标检测**，且完全不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、星上部署等任何与模型轻量化或在轨部署相关的内容。论文关注的是分类精度和物理知识融合，而非效率或部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关。任务类型（分类 vs 检测）、关注点（精度 vs 效率/部署）均不匹配，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：PolSAR图像分类（land-cover语义分类）；传感器类型：极化合成孔径雷达（PolSAR），涉及L波段和P波段；目标类别：地物覆盖类型（land-cover semantics）；应用场景：GeoAI、物理感知遥感图像理解。**不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测。**

Model architecture: CV-SSMNet：复值状态空间网络，包含复值状态空间模型（CV-SSM）、多尺度复值卷积、分支式CV-SSM编码、先验引导的特征重标定（FiLM-style调制）、轻量级全局上下文聚合模块。核心创新是在复值域中构建状态空间模型以捕获长程空间依赖，同时保留极化幅度-相位耦合信息。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文中提到"lightweight global context aggregation"，但这里的"lightweight"是指模块设计上的轻量，并非模型压缩/加速意义上的轻量化（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。不涉及任何面向在轨部署或边缘部署的优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。论文完全不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：三个L波段基准数据集 + 一个P波段BIOMASS评估数据集；评价指标：分类精度（accuracy）、区域一致性（regional consistency）、边界保持（boundary preservation）。摘要未说明具体指标数值。

Experiments: 实验结果表明CV-SSMNet在分类精度、区域一致性和边界保持方面具有竞争力，验证了将极化散射机制嵌入复值长程GeoAI表示学习的有效性。摘要未提供与SOTA方法的详细对比数据。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及目标检测任务，与在轨目标检测方向无关；2. 未讨论模型效率、参数量、计算开销，无法评估部署可行性；3. 仅针对PolSAR分类，不适用于光学遥感、SAR目标检测等更广泛的遥感任务；4. 复值网络的计算复杂度通常较高，可能不利于轻量化部署。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. 状态空间模型（SSM）在遥感长程依赖建模中的潜力，可探索将SSM用于遥感目标检测中的上下文增强；2. FiLM-style物理先验调制机制，可思考如何将遥感物理知识（如目标几何先验、散射特性）融入检测模型；3. 复值网络对幅度-相位信息的保留思路，对SAR目标检测可能有参考价值。但以上均需额外设计轻量化方案才能适配在轨场景。

Abstract: Polarimetric synthetic aperture radar (PolSAR) image classification is a representative task for physics-aware GeoAI, where land-cover semantics are closely coupled with electromagnetic scattering mechanisms. Many existing complex-valued networks can preserve amplitude-phase information, but they are often limited in long-range spatial dependency modeling and usually incorporate polarimetric priors only as input-level or shallow auxiliary features. As a result, physical knowledge is insufficiently used to guide deep feature evolution. To address this issue, this paper proposes CV-SSMNet, a physics-aware complex-valued state-space network with scattering-aware feature modulation for PolSAR image classification. The proposed method builds a complex-valued state-space model (CV-SSM) in the original complex domain to capture long-range spatial dependencies while preserving polarimetric amplitude-phase coupling. Meanwhile, seven physically meaningful scattering priors, are encoded as FiLM-style modulation signals to adaptively recalibrate complex-valued representations during feature evolution. CV-SSMNet further integrates multi-scale complex convolutions, branch-wise CV-SSM encoding, prior-guided recalibration, and lightweight global context aggregation, enabling physically guided representation learning from local scattering structures to global spatial context. Experiments on three L-band benchmark datasets and an additional P-band BIOMASS evaluation demonstrate that CV-SSMNet achieves competitive accuracy, improved regional consistency, and better boundary preservation, supporting the effectiveness of embedding polarimetric scattering mechanisms into complex-valued long-range GeoAI representation learning.

</details>

*Md Tanvirul Alam*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多领域视觉推理（visual reasoning）；应用场景：通用视觉语言模型推理能力提升；传感器类型：不适用（合成图像）；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明遥感相关任务。
