Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Trace环境（1,000个任务，277个场景语法，11个视觉领域，64,000个实例）；外部基准：24个外部基准测试；评价指标：macro-average准确率。

Experiments: RLVR在64,000个Trace实例上训练后，Qwen2.5-VL-3B在24个外部基准上的macro-average提升3.51个百分点，Qwen2.5-VL-7B提升4.06个百分点。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。未涉及遥感数据、目标检测任务、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。可借鉴点有限：1) 可验证奖励的强化学习方法（RLVR）理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测模型的训练优化中；2) 程序化生成训练数据的思路（scene grammar + executable task program）可用于遥感数据增强或合成。但整体与课题核心方向关联度低。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved language-model reasoning, yet its extension to vision-language models remains constrained by the lack of training data that are simultaneously broad, exactly verifiable, and reproducible. We introduce Trace, a taxonomy-guided environment for multidomain visual reasoning. Trace factorizes task construction into a scene grammar and an executable task program, separating visual realization from answer computation. A shared semantic state determines the rendered image, prompt, typed answer, verifier state, and replayable instance trace. The resulting environment comprises 1,000 tasks over 277 scene grammars and 11 visual domains, with controlled semantic and visual variation. RLVR on 64,000 Trace instances improves the macro-average across 24 external benchmarks by 3.51 percentage points for Qwen2.5-VL-3B and 4.06 points for Qwen2.5-VL-7B, providing evidence that broad procedural training can transfer beyond the generated task distributions. Project page: https://maveryn.github.io/trace/.

</details>


### [27] [Lean-SAM2: Target-Anchored Memory and Encoder Acceleration for SAM2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19811)
*Xudong Ouyang,Wenlun Zhang,Yimin Xu,Huazhong Liu,Yunshan Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lean-SAM2 通过目标锚定记忆剪枝、时序压缩保险记忆和目标锚定风险感知路由三种机制，在视频分割任务上实现了 SAM2 的轻量化加速，同时保持甚至提升了分割精度。

Research relevance: 2 分 — 弱相关。论文核心是 SAM2 视频分割模型的轻量化加速，属于通用视觉分割领域，不涉及遥感/卫星场景、目标检测任务，也未提及遥感图像、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感相关概念。虽然其轻量化方法（记忆剪枝、稀疏路由）在方法论上对模型压缩有参考价值，但与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"这一特定方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 2 分 — 阅读优先级低。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但其记忆剪枝和条件路由的轻量化思路有一定方法论参考价值。如果时间充裕，可快速浏览其轻量化机制设计思路，但不建议深入精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频分割（temporal promptable segmentation）；应用场景：通用视频分割，含遮挡和干扰物的复杂分割场景；传感器类型：摘要未说明（RGB 视频帧）；目标类别：摘要未说明（通用分割目标）。

Model architecture: 基于 SAM2（Segment Anything Model 2）的轻量化改进框架。包含三个核心模块：(1) Target-Anchored Memory Pruning (TAMP) — 目标锚定记忆剪枝；(2) Temporal Condensation with Insurance Memory (TCIM) — 时序压缩保险记忆；(3) Target-Anchored Risk-Aware Routing (TARR) — 目标锚定风险感知路由。基础骨干为 SAM2.1-Large 和 SAM2.1-Base+。

Lightweight method: 涉及轻量化方法：(1) 记忆剪枝（Memory Pruning）— TAMP 机制通过语义一致性筛选目标 token，减少冗余记忆；(2) 稀疏路由（Sparse Routing）— TARR 机制选择性激活图像编码器，仅对目标相关窗口进行全量特征提取；(3) 时序压缩（Temporal Condensation）— TCIM 通过可见性门控融合压缩历史上下文。未涉及剪枝（weight pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS 等经典轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署关键指标。仅报告了推理加速比：在 LVOSv2 验证集上，SAM2.1-Large 加速 1.412×，SAM2.1-Base+ 加速 1.417×。未提及 FPGA/NPU/TensorRT 等边缘部署硬件适配，未讨论星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LVOSv2 validation dataset（视频分割基准）；评价指标：𝒥&ℱ score（视频分割标准指标，结合区域相似度 𝒥 和轮廓精度 ℱ）；对比方法：Efficient-SAM2。

Experiments: 在 LVOSv2 上，Lean-SAM2 相比 Efficient-SAM2：SAM2.1-Large 加速 1.412× 且 𝒥&ℱ 提升 5.0%；SAM2.1-Base+ 加速 1.417× 且 𝒥&ℱ 提升 3.6%。摘要未报告其他数据集结果、消融实验细节或与更多基线方法的对比。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关；(2) 仅报告了推理加速比，未提供参数量、FLOPs、模型大小等关键轻量化指标；(3) 仅在 LVOSv2 一个数据集上报告结果，泛化性存疑；(4) 方法针对视频分割设计，无法直接迁移到目标检测任务；(5) 未讨论实时性（FPS）和硬件部署约束。

Ideas for my research: (1) TAMP 中的"目标锚定"思想可借鉴：在遥感目标检测中，可利用先验知识（如地物类别、空间位置）引导注意力剪枝，减少冗余特征计算；(2) TARR 的风险感知路由策略有参考价值：在星上资源受限场景下，可设计"轻量级检测 + 条件触发全量检测"的级联机制，在保证关键目标检测精度的同时降低平均计算开销；(3) TCIM 的时序压缩思路可用于遥感视频流（如卫星视频目标检测）中的历史帧信息管理，减少存储和计算冗余。但需注意这些方法需重新适配到目标检测任务和遥感图像特性。

Abstract: The Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) has advanced temporal promptable segmentation, yet its deployment remains hindered by heavy memory cross-attention overhead and redundant full-frame visual feature extraction. While recent methods explore efficiency via heuristic memory pruning and window-based sparse routing, they typically suffer from catastrophic performance degradation in complex segmentation scenarios replete with occlusions and distractors. To resolve these limitations, we propose \textbf{Lean-SAM2}, a holistic lightweight framework designed to address the above vulnerabilities while systematically eliminating computational redundancies. Specifically, Lean-SAM2 integrates three collaborative mechanisms: (1) Target-Anchored Memory Pruning (TAMP) safeguards target tokens against deceptive attention by modulating raw attention significance with semantic consistency against prompt-derived foreground anchors; (2) Temporal Condensation with Insurance Memory (TCIM) condenses historical context via a visibility-gated fusion while conditionally archiving high-confidence entries in a parallel insurance bank; and (3) Target-Anchored Risk-Aware Routing (TARR) selectively activates the heavy image encoder for target-related windows based on anchor similarity, utilizing a risk-aware fallback policy to trigger full-frame refreshes during volatile transitions. Extensive evaluations across multiple challenging benchmarks demonstrate that Lean-SAM2 establishes a superior balance between accuracy and efficiency. For example, on the LVOSv2 validation dataset, Lean-SAM2 achieves overall inference speedups of $1.412\times$ and $1.417\times$ on the SAM2.1-Large and SAM2.1-Base+, respectively, significantly outperforming Efficient-SAM2 while boosting the corresponding $\mathcal{J}\&\mathcal{F}$ scores by $5.0\%$ and $3.6\%$. Code is available at https://github.com/DeawhaleQwQ/Lean-SAM2.

</details>


### [28] [MoAKE: Toward Unified All-in-One Action Quality Assessment via Mixture of Action Knowledge Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19826)
*Huangbiao Xu,Huanqi Wu,Xiao Ke,Jiaxin Cai,Junyi Wu,Jinglin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoAKE框架，用于多动作类型的统一动作质量评估（AQA），通过混合专家知识缓解语义差异导致的负迁移问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是动作质量评估（AQA），属于视频理解/人体动作分析领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感内容；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏等模型压缩方法。唯一可能的弱关联是"部署"一词出现在摘要中讨论现有方法的局限性，但论文本身并未提出任何轻量化或边缘部署方案。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：动作质量评估（AQA）；场景：视频动作分析；传感器类型：RGB视频；目标类别：人体动作（如跳水、体操等）。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: MoAKE（Mixture of Action Knowledge Experts）框架，包含多个互补专家模块，每个专家配备segment-aware prototypes和AIISRM（Adaptive Intra- and Inter-Segment Relationship Modeling）模块，用于多粒度时序动态建模。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然摘要提到"real-world deployment"一词，但仅用于批评现有方法需要为每个动作类型单独训练模型的问题，并未提出任何面向边缘/星上部署的轻量化方案。

Datasets and metrics: 三个长时数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）用于all-in-one AQA评估；三个短时数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）用于zero/few-shot评估。评价指标摘要未说明具体名称。

Experiments: 在all-in-one设置下显著优于现有方法；在zero/few-shot评估下也展现出泛化能力。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化或模型压缩技术；3. 未提供计算效率指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；4. 仅在视频动作数据集上验证，未在遥感图像上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但MoAKE中的"混合专家"（Mixture of Experts）思想在理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测中处理多尺度/多类别目标，例如为不同尺度或不同传感器模态（光学、SAR）设计专用专家分支。不过论文本身未涉及轻量化，直接迁移会带来模型参数量增加的问题，与轻量化目标相悖。

Abstract: Action Quality Assessment (AQA) aims to objectively evaluate performance quality from action videos. Most existing methods follow a ``one-by-one'' paradigm, training a separate model for each action type. This setting limits real-world deployment, as it requires prior action-type knowledge to select the corresponding model and suffers from poor generalization across diverse actions. To address these limitations, we study the challenging task of all-in-one AQA, which aims to assess heterogeneous actions within a single unified model. We propose a novel Mixture of Action Knowledge Experts (MoAKE) framework, designed to mitigate negative knowledge transfer caused by large semantic discrepancies among actions. MoAKE learns complementary experts that capture diverse action patterns within a shared semantic space and dynamically aggregates their knowledge to adapt the assessment to the input action. Each expert is tailored with segment-aware prototypes to handle varying temporal lengths, together with an Adaptive Intra- and Inter-Segment Relationship Modeling (AIISRM) module to model multi-granularity temporal dynamics. Furthermore, we establish comprehensive benchmarks for all-in-one as well as zero/few-shot AQA. Extensive experiments on three long-term datasets demonstrate that MoAKE significantly outperforms existing methods in the all-in-one setting, while also achieving consistent generalization on three short-term datasets under zero/few-shot evaluation. Code is available at https://github.com/XuHuangbiao/MoAKE.

</details>


### [29] [Memory-Augmented Multimodal Large Language Models for Small Object Understanding in Streaming Aerial Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19857)
*Penglei Sun,Yehua Huang,Zhuoli Tao,Xiang Li,Runwei Guan,Yaoxian Song,Kaiyong Zhao,Henghui Ding,Bo Han,Yang Yang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个面向空中微小目标的开放词汇指代分割数据集DroneEyes，以及面向流式在线感知的MLLM模型SkyAnchor，通过语义感知令牌路由和分层记忆库解决小目标细节丢失和流式上下文漂移问题。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及无人机航拍场景（aerial/UAV）和小目标检测，属于遥感/航空图像相关方向；但核心任务是"开放词汇指代分割（open-vocabulary referring segmentation）"而非目标检测，且方法基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM），与轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS/边缘部署）无直接关联。虽然提到了"resource-constrained onboard hardware"，但未给出任何轻量化方法或部署指标。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关，阅读优先级低。理由：论文虽涉及航拍小目标和资源受限部署，但核心任务（指代分割）和基础方法（MLLM）与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向差异较大，且未提供任何轻量化技术细节或部署指标。仅建议快速浏览其令牌压缩和记忆机制的设计思路。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：开放词汇指代分割（open-vocabulary referring segmentation）、流式在线感知（online streaming perception）；场景：无人机航拍（UAV aerial scenes）；传感器类型：高清视频（HD videos）；目标类别：微小空中目标（tiny aerial targets），具体类别摘要未说明。

Model architecture: SkyAnchor——基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM），包含两个核心设计：(1) Semantics-Aware Token Router（语义感知令牌路由器），在减少视觉令牌预算的同时保留小目标细节；(2) Hierarchical Memory Bank（分层记忆库），在流式处理中保持目标理解的连续性。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。仅提到"Semantics-Aware Token Router"通过减少视觉令牌预算（reduced visual-token budget）来压缩视觉表示，但这属于MLLM的令牌压缩策略，并非传统意义上的模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到"resource-constrained onboard hardware"和"online streaming manner"表明考虑了部署约束，但未给出任何具体部署指标：参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、帧率等均未说明。仅提及无法保留完整历史帧（retaining the entire history is infeasible），但未给出量化分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DroneEyes——首个面向空中微小目标的像素级开放词汇指代分割数据集，包含2,140个高清视频、176,623对样本（Object Description和Referring Expression任务），密集逐帧掩码标注。评价指标：摘要未说明。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 核心任务（指代分割）而非目标检测，与课题方向不完全对齐；2. 基于MLLM的方法计算开销大，未给出任何轻量化手段或部署指标；3. 未与现有轻量目标检测方法（如YOLO系列轻量变体）进行对比；4. 数据集仅针对无人机航拍场景，未涉及卫星遥感（satellite remote sensing）或SAR/多光谱等传感器；5. 未说明模型参数量、推理速度等关键部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 1. "Semantics-Aware Token Router"的思路——在视觉令牌压缩时保留小目标区域细节，这一思想可迁移到遥感目标检测的轻量化特征压缩中，例如在剪枝或量化时对小目标敏感通道给予保护；2. "Hierarchical Memory Bank"的分层记忆机制可用于卫星视频流（satellite video）中的连续帧目标跟踪与检测，减少帧间冗余计算；3. DroneEyes数据集虽非卫星遥感，但其小目标标注方式（密集逐帧掩码）对构建遥感小目标检测数据集有参考价值；4. 论文关注"resource-constrained onboard hardware"场景，但其方法本身不轻量，可作为"需要进一步轻量化"的基线参考。

Abstract: Language-guided aerial perception aims to understand user-specified tiny targets in complex unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) scenes. In real UAV deployment, the UAV must respond while it flies, so such perception runs in an online streaming manner, where frames arrive sequentially and the model responds to each one without access to future frames. However, applying current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to this setting raises two challenges. First, targets viewed from the air are often tiny, yet the visual compression in existing MLLMs treats all regions equally and discards their fine-grained details. Second, understanding a continuous stream requires past-frame context, yet retaining the entire history is infeasible on resource-constrained onboard hardware, whereas discarding it causes the target to drift or disappear. We address the tiny object and streaming challenges from both data and method perspectives. From the data perspective, we present \textbf{DroneEyes}, the \textbf{first} pixel-level and open-vocabulary referring-segmentation dataset for tiny aerial targets, comprising $2,140$ high-definition videos and $176,623$ pairs across Object Description and Referring Expression tasks, with dense per-frame masks. From the method perspective, we propose \textbf{SkyAnchor}, an MLLM with two designs to the above challenges: a Semantics-Aware Token Router that preserves small-target under a reduced visual-token budget, and a Hierarchical Memory Bank that keeps the target consistently understood on streams.

</details>


### [30] [Robust Activation Map Rectification for Weakly Supervised Volumetric Segmentation: Temporal Coherence as a Free Lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19877)
*Renshu Gu,Jialiang Chen,Fei Gao,Hang Su,Jun Qi,Jiamin Xu,Yicheng Shen,Jiayu Zhang,Jiaxi Pan,Caiming Zhang,Gang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练、无需原型学习的弱监督分割框架，利用体数据中的时序和结构一致性来修正类激活图（CAM），提升分割性能并大幅降低推理时间。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学/体数据（volumetric data）的弱监督分割，不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。虽然方法本身是"training-free"且能降低推理时间，但场景、任务、数据类型均与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对弱监督分割或体数据处理有交叉兴趣，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：弱监督语义分割（weakly supervised segmentation）；场景：体数据分割（volumetric segmentation），如医学影像；传感器类型：摘要未说明，推测为CT/MRI等医学成像；目标类别：摘要未说明。

Model architecture: 基于CAM（Class Activation Map）的弱监督分割框架，包含两个核心组件：VRAA（Variance-Reduced Activation Aggregation）和BER（Bidirectional Extremity Rectification）。模型无关（model-agnostic），可集成到现有pipeline中。

Lightweight method: 方法本身是training-free（无需训练）和prototype-free（无需原型学习），不引入额外训练阶段或参数，因此计算开销低。但这不是传统意义上的轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等），而是通过算法设计减少训练和推理成本。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到推理时间减少5倍以上（reducing inference time by more than 5 times），但未给出具体硬件平台和延迟数值。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 多个公开体数据分割基准（multiple public benchmarks）；评价指标：Dice（提升最高20%）、mIoU（提升最高40%）。具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在多个公开基准上超越现有弱监督方法，Dice提升最高20%，mIoU提升最高40%，推理时间减少5倍以上。消融实验和理论分析（CAM作为高维随机向量的方差缩减证明）摘要提及但未给出细节。

Limitations: 1. 仅适用于体数据（volumetric data），依赖时序/结构一致性先验，不适用于遥感单帧图像；2. 弱监督分割任务，非目标检测；3. 未在遥感场景下验证；4. 无参数量、FLOPs等轻量化关键指标；5. 未讨论星上/边缘部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：1. "training-free"思路——在遥感在轨场景中，可探索无需额外训练即可提升检测性能的轻量化后处理方法；2. "利用数据内在一致性作为隐式先验"——遥感时序影像（如视频卫星、多时相数据）也可利用帧间一致性来增强检测稳定性；3. 方差缩减的聚合策略（VRAA）可借鉴用于遥感多帧检测结果的融合去噪。但整体而言，该论文与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化方向距离较远。

Abstract: Weakly supervised segmentation relies heavily on class activation maps (CAMs) to initially localize target regions. However, CAMs are often noisy and prone to catastrophic failures. Existing remedies typically introduce additional training stages or prototype learning, increasing computational cost and reducing robustness. In this paper, we propose a training-free prototype-free framework that rectifies unreliable CAMs by exploiting temporal and structural coherence in volumetric data as a free lunch. Our approach is built on two key components. First, we introduce Variance-Reduced Activation Aggregation (VRAA) which suppresses noise and amplify coherent semantic signals. We provide a theoretical justification by modeling CAMs as high-dimensional random vectors and show that aggregation yields provable variance reduction. Second, we design a Bidirectional Extremity Rectification (BER) mechanism that detects and rectifies implausible activations through bidirectional extremity checks, effectively mitigating extreme-value failures without learning additional parameters. Our method is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated with existing pipelines. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks demonstrate substantial improvements over state-of-the-art weakly supervised methods, achieving up to 20% Dice and 40% mIoU gains while reducing inference time by more than 5 times. These results indicate that leveraging coherence as an implicit inductive bias yields a principled and efficient approach to stabilizing weakly supervised volumetric segmentation. Our code will be available.

</details>


### [31] [Current Injection Spiking Neural Network for Infrared and Visible Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19879)
*Rui Zhao,Zhuoyuan Li,Wenrui Li,Yanchen Dong,Yajing Zheng,Giuseppe Valenzise,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于脉冲神经网络(SNN)的跨模态膜电位级融合方法CIS-Fuse，用于红外与可见光图像融合，在保持融合质量的同时显著降低推理能耗。

Research relevance: 2分 — 弱相关。论文涉及红外与可见光图像融合(IVIF)，属于多模态图像处理，但**不涉及遥感/卫星场景**，**不涉及目标检测模型本身**，**不涉及模型轻量化**（虽然SNN本身具有低能耗特性，但论文核心是融合任务而非检测模型压缩或加速）。SNN的低能耗特性对星上部署有间接启发，但论文未讨论遥感、卫星、在轨部署等场景。

Reading priority: 2分 — 与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题弱相关。SNN的低能耗思路有启发价值，但论文聚焦于融合任务而非检测任务，且未涉及遥感场景。可作为SNN在视觉任务中应用的背景阅读，不建议作为核心参考文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：红外与可见光图像融合(IVIF)；应用场景：通用多模态融合（非遥感特定）；传感器类型：红外相机+可见光相机；目标类别：摘要未说明；下游任务：目标检测(detection)和语义分割(segmentation)。

Model architecture: CIS-Fuse：基于脉冲神经网络(SNN)的双分支非对称深度架构；核心模块：电流注入脉冲(CIS)算子、双向跨模态融合(BCMF)模块；关键设计：在膜电位(membrane potential)层面进行跨模态融合，而非在脉冲(spike)层面。

Lightweight method: 论文本身不是轻量化方法研究，但SNN架构天然具有低能耗优势：通过稀疏二进制脉冲(sparse binary spikes)通信，仅在脉冲发生时进行计算，推理能耗约为同等规模ANN方法的1/10。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟等具体部署指标；仅报告了推理能耗：比同等规模的ANN方法DCEvo低约一个数量级(order of magnitude)。未讨论星上/边缘部署可行性、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件适配。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：4个IVIF基准数据集；下游任务在检测和分割数据集上评估；评价指标：融合质量指标（摘要未具体列出）+ 下游检测/分割性能指标；具体数据集名称和指标数值摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在4个IVIF基准数据集上，CIS-Fuse的融合质量与SOTA的ANN方法相当；下游检测和分割任务表现良好；推理能耗比同等规模的ANN方法DCEvo低约一个数量级。消融实验和详细对比结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 非遥感场景，未在遥感数据集（如卫星多光谱/高光谱/SAR）上验证；2. 未讨论目标检测模型的轻量化，而是融合模型的低能耗；3. 未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟等关键部署指标；4. SNN在现有GPU/NPU硬件上的实际加速效果可能不如理论能耗优势明显；5. 未讨论在轨部署的约束条件（如辐射环境、实时性要求）。

Ideas for my research: 1. **SNN用于遥感目标检测的启发**：SNN的低能耗特性对星上部署有吸引力，可探索将遥感目标检测模型（如YOLO系列）替换为SNN变体；2. **膜电位级融合思想**：在遥感多模态融合（如可见光+SAR、多光谱+高光谱）中，可借鉴在膜电位层面而非特征层面进行融合的思路；3. **非对称双分支架构**：不同模态采用不同深度的分支，这种设计可迁移到遥感多模态检测中；4. **可学习注入强度**：per-channel可学习的调制权重，可用于自适应融合不同遥感传感器信息。但需注意：本文是融合任务而非检测任务，直接借鉴需改造。

Abstract: Infrared and visible image fusion (IVIF) integrates the complementary information of two modalities into a single image with richer scene content. While existing methods are largely built on artificial neural networks (ANNs), which densely compute over all activations, spiking neural networks (SNNs) communicate through sparse binary spikes and compute only where and when a spike occurs, offering a route to more energy-efficient fusion. However, directly applying SNNs to IVIF creates a fundamental tension: cross-modal fusion relies on fine-grained responses from both modalities, whereas binary spikes can discard complementary cues that remain below the firing threshold. The membrane potential retains these subthreshold responses before firing, letting both modalities jointly shape the output when integrated at this stage. Building on this, we propose CIS-Fuse, a spiking network that performs cross-modal fusion directly at the membrane-potential level. At its core is the current injection spiking (CIS) operator, which injects one modality as a gated auxiliary current into the driving neuron of the other, so the two integrate before spike firing, with a per-channel learnable injection strength that adaptively regulates the modulation magnitude. Building on CIS, we construct a bidirectional cross-modal fusion (BCMF) module and deploy it on a dual-branch architecture with asymmetric stacking depths, where the two branches develop a clear functional specialization. Extensive experiments on four IVIF benchmarks and on downstream detection and segmentation show that CIS-Fuse achieves fusion quality on par with state-of-the-art ANN-based methods while inheriting the energy efficiency of spike-based computation, with roughly an order of magnitude lower inference energy than the similarly-sized ANN-based DCEvo. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [32] [MTVDiff: Multimodal Conditional Latent Diffusion for Enhanced Thermal-to-Visible Face Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19886)
*Zhiyuan Xia,Haojie Li,Jingyu Lin,Yiguo Qiao,Cunjian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MTVDiff多模态潜在扩散框架，利用深度和文本信息实现热红外到可见光人脸翻译，提升跨光谱人脸识别性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦热红外到可见光人脸翻译（thermal-to-visible face translation），属于跨光谱人脸识别/生成任务，不涉及遥感图像、卫星平台、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及热红外（thermal）模态，但应用场景是人脸识别而非遥感对地观测，且无任何轻量化或星上部署讨论。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：跨光谱人脸图像翻译（thermal-to-visible face translation）；场景：人脸识别系统在变化光照条件下的鲁棒性提升；传感器类型：热红外相机+可见光相机；目标类别：人脸。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: MTVDiff：多模态潜在扩散框架（multimodal latent diffusion framework），包含：(1) Dual-Branch Cross-Attention Fusion (DBCAF) 双分支交叉注意力融合模块；(2) Gated Text-to-Visual Feature Alignment 门控文本到视觉特征对齐；(3) Spatial Feature Transformations (SFT) 空间特征变换。摘要未说明参数量和计算量。

Lightweight method: 未涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。扩散模型通常计算开销极大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MCXFace, SpeakingFaces；评价指标：FID（Fréchet Inception Distance，降低48.3%）、Rank-1 accuracy（提升8.9%）。不涉及遥感数据集或遥感评价指标。

Experiments: 在MCXFace和SpeakingFaces数据集上，MTVDiff优于现有GAN-based和diffusion-based方法，FID降低48.3%，Rank-1准确率提升8.9%。摘要未说明消融实验细节。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；(2) 扩散模型推理速度慢、参数量大，不适合边缘/星上部署；(3) 未讨论模型轻量化或实时性；(4) 仅针对人脸翻译任务，无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的相关点是：论文使用了热红外（thermal）模态，遥感中也有热红外/SAR等多模态数据，但本文的多模态融合方法（DBCAF、SFT）是针对人脸生成设计的，且扩散模型计算量过大，不适合在轨场景。不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Thermal-to-visible face translation presents fundamental challenges including geometric discontinuities, semantic attribute mismatches, and identity degradation. We propose MTVDiff, a novel multimodal latent diffusion framework that synergistically integrates depth and textual information to address these limitations while preserving identity characteristics. The MTVDiff framework presents three core technical contributions: (1) a Dual-Branch Cross-Attention Fusion (DBCAF) module for multi-scale thermal-depth feature extraction and fusion; (2) a Gated Text-to-Visual Feature Alignment mechanism for semantically-guided generation; and (3) Spatial Feature Transformations (SFT) for adaptive multimodal prior integration. Extensive experiments on the MCXFace and SpeakingFaces datasets demonstrate that our multimodal approach significantly outperforms existing GAN-based and diffusion-based approaches across multiple metrics, achieving substantial improvements in both image quality and face verification performance, with FID reductions of up to 48.3% and Rank-1 accuracy improvements of up to 8.9\%. Our work provides a robust solution for face recognition systems operating under varying illumination conditions and advances the state-of-the-art in cross-spectral facial image translation through effective multimodal integration.

</details>

*Jiajun Cheng,Subarna Tripathi,Sainan Liu,Xiaofan Yu,Shan Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LAViFiT框架，通过逆动力学模型和前向世界模型引导视觉-语言模型在手术器械-组织交互场景下的细粒度微调，提升特征空间定位能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是手术机器人场景下的器械-组织交互理解，属于医学图像/手术AI领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等完全无关。不涉及目标检测，不涉及轻量化/部署。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：手术器械-组织交互识别（instrument-tissue interaction recognition）；应用场景：context-aware surgical AI, autonomous robotic surgery；传感器类型：内窥镜/手术视频（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：手术器械与组织交互动作。摘要未说明具体数据集。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Experiments: 实验在多个编码器和数据集上进行，提升了识别（recognition）和图像-文本对齐（image-text alignment）性能；表征分析显示更强的空间定位和更空间一致的特征。未提供具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨部署完全无关；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何轻量化或边缘部署方法；4. 摘要未报告计算开销、推理速度等关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可抽象参考：(1) 逆动力学模型+前向世界模型引导视觉编码器关注"动作相关区域"的思路，可类比到遥感目标检测中引导模型关注目标区域而非背景；(2) patch-level正则化防止局部特征坍塌的思路，可用于遥感小目标检测中的特征增强。但这些均为间接借鉴，且需要大量改造。

Abstract: Understanding instrument-tissue interactions is essential for context-aware surgical AI and autonomous robotic surgery. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) and vision encoders offer an alternative to conventional interaction classifiers by transferring broad visual and semantic knowledge. However, adapting them to fine-grained surgical interactions remains challenging: (1) freezing the vision encoder depends entirely on pretrained representations that may retain noise and provide weak spatial localization, while (2) full fine-tuning can improve global semantic alignment without ensuring that the encoder learns meaningful features in the correct action region. We address these limitations by introducing LAViFiT, an end-to-end latent-action-guided framework for vision-language fine-tuning. An inverse dynamics model captures the visual changes induced by each action, while a forward world model drives the encoder to represent action-relevant regions. A patch-level SIG Regularizer further prevents local feature collapse without additional supervision, such as bounding boxes or pseudo-labels. Experiments across multiple encoders and datasets improve recognition and image-text alignment, while representation analyses show stronger grounding over the complete instrument-tissue interaction region and more spatially coherent features.

</details>

*Habin Lim,Gyeong-Moon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OSVE框架，首次将一步式文生图扩散模型适配到视频编辑任务，通过可学习编码器替代迭代反演，实现155-171倍加速。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于text-guided video editing（文本引导视频编辑），属于AIGC/视频生成领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然文中涉及一步式推理加速（one-step generation），但应用场景和任务目标与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：text-guided video editing（文本引导视频编辑）；应用场景：视频编辑/生成；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于一步式Text-to-Image (T2I)扩散模型；可学习编码器（learnable encoder）用于预测每帧初始噪声；Unified-Frame Editing (UFE) 通过拼接帧隐变量实现跨帧注意力；滑动窗口策略维持长视频全局一致性。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确提及传统轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。核心加速手段是：①用可学习编码器替代迭代反演（inversion），将多步采样变为一步前向；②一步式生成替代多步扩散采样。整体属于算法级加速而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及推理速度提升155-171倍（相比多步方法），但未给出绝对延迟数值，也未涉及星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及在"structurally-aligned image pairs"数据集上训练SAE损失，以及进行了"extensive experiments"。

Experiments: 摘要未给出具体数值对比结果，仅声称编辑质量与SOTA多步方法相当或更优，速度提升约155-171倍。

Limitations: ①与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；②摘要未说明模型参数量、计算量等关键轻量化指标；③视频编辑任务与目标检测任务差异巨大，方法迁移性存疑；④未讨论在资源受限设备上的部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能值得注意的点是：①"一步式推理替代多步迭代"的思路在扩散模型加速中有效，如果未来遥感目标检测也采用扩散模型范式（如diffusion-based detection），可参考此加速策略；②可学习编码器替代迭代过程的思想，与知识蒸馏中"学生网络一步模仿教师多步输出"有相似之处。但整体而言，该论文对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化的直接参考价值很低。

Abstract: Text-guided video editing with diffusion models is impractically slow, hindered by costly multi-step sampling and inversion. We present OSVE, the first framework to successfully adapt one-step Text-to-Image (T2I) models for high-quality video editing, addressing the core challenges of inversion, editability, and temporal consistency. To bypass slow iterative inversion, we train a learnable encoder that predicts the initial noise for each frame in a single forward pass. This encoder is trained with a novel Structure-Aware Editing (SAE) loss on a curated dataset of structurally-aligned image pairs, teaching it to preserve the source video's geometry during edits. For temporal coherence, we introduce Unified-Frame Editing (UFE), a technique that concatenates frame latents to facilitate cross-frame attention in a single generation step. Furthermore, for long videos, a sliding-window strategy with an anchor frame maintains global consistency. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that OSVE achieves editing quality comparable or superior to state-of-the-art multi-step methods, while operating approximately 155--171 times faster. This breakthrough paves the way for practical, real-time video editing applications. Code is available at https://github.com/KU-VGI/OSVE.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：3D脑卒中病灶分割（brain lesion segmentation）；场景：临床工作站部署；传感器类型：医学影像（MRI/CT等）；目标类别：脑卒中病灶。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 教师模型：nnU-Net（102.3M参数）；学生模型：0.84M参数（具体结构摘要未说明）；框架：C++/Qt跨平台架构；推理优化：ONNX Runtime + Float16量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未涉及星上/边缘部署。参数量0.84M，磁盘占用2.1 MB，能耗降低>90%，推理时间平均减少84%。但硬件环境为CPU/iGPU/dedicated GPU（临床工作站），非FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等星上平台。延迟具体数值摘要未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标（仅提及"robust lesion localisation and competitive segmentation performance"）。

Experiments: 对比了不同硬件配置（CPU、集成GPU、独立GPU）下的性能；消融实验表明知识蒸馏是效率提升的主要贡献者（>90%能耗降低，84%推理时间减少）；0.84M参数学生模型被认为是最优权衡点。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 未说明具体数据集和分割精度指标（如Dice系数）；3. 未涉及旋转框检测、小目标检测等遥感特有挑战；4. 部署平台为临床工作站，非星上资源受限环境；5. 未讨论FPGA/NPU等硬件加速方案。

Ideas for my research: 1. 知识蒸馏+量化联合优化的思路可借鉴，尤其是蒸馏对能耗和推理时间的显著贡献；2. 0.84M参数/2.1 MB的极轻量模型尺寸可作为遥感在轨检测模型的参考量级；3. C++/Qt跨平台部署思路对星上软件栈设计有参考价值；4. 但需注意：医学分割与遥感检测任务差异大，蒸馏策略需重新设计以适应遥感目标检测（尤其是小目标、旋转框）。

Abstract: Deep learning frameworks like nnU-Net achieve state-of-theart brain lesion segmentation performance but remain difficult to deploy in clinical research environments due to, among other reasons, software dependencies and computational requirements. We introduce StrokeSeg2, a lightweight, modular, cross-platform C++/Qt framework designed to adapt resource-intensive 3D stroke segmentation pipelines into portable and reproducible applications. To improve compatibility with standard clinical workstations, we investigate the combined effect of architectural compression through knowledge distillation and inference optimisation using ONNX Runtime with Float16 quantisation. Across heterogeneous hardware configurations (CPU, integrated GPU, and dedicated GPU) architectural distillation emerged as the primary contributor to efficiency gains, contributing to over 90% reduction in energy consumption and an average 84% reduction in inference time. Specifically, we identify a 0.84M-parameter student model as the most favourable trade-off, reducing the original 102.3M-parameter teacher architecture to a 2.1 MB disk footprint while preserving robust lesion localisation and competitive segmentation performance. This small footprint supports the development of a self-contained installer for clinical workstation targets. Finally, StrokeSeg2 packages these optimisations into standalone installers for Windows, macOS, and Linux. By providing both graphical and commandline interfaces without Docker or external environment dependencies, StrokeSeg2 facilitates deployment of high-performance segmentation workflows for routine clinical research pipelines.

</details>

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected to automate visualization development by generating code directly from visual designs. However, existing evaluations mainly focus on single-chart generation and overlook coordinated multi-view interface construction, which requires joint reasoning about data semantics, view coordination, and interaction logic. Consequently, MLLM capabilities in this setting remain underexplored, and the field lacks a dedicated benchmark for systematic assessment. We introduce MV-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on coordinated multi-view interface construction. Instead of relying on incomplete or inconsistent open-source implementations, we use Tableau workbook files as ground truth because they explicitly encode data bindings, visual mappings, and interactions. We develop a multi-stage pipeline that converts these specifications into executable web interfaces through structured intermediate representations. The benchmark contains 92 base interfaces and 1,048 verified instances created by recombining chart types, datasets, and interaction patterns. Each instance includes executable code, a rendered interface, a dataset, and interaction annotations. We evaluate five state-of-the-art MLLMs in a single-pass setting using metrics for visual fidelity, data binding correctness, and interaction completeness. The strongest model achieves 75.45 percent accuracy in visual layout reproduction, but only 21.71 percent in data binding and 11.68 percent in interaction completeness. These results show that current MLLMs can reproduce visual appearance but remain limited in generating the data semantics and interactive logic required by coordinated multi-view interfaces. Iterative refinement improves code executability but does not substantially reduce the gap in data binding and interaction generation.

</details>

*Yufan Zhu,Kefu Yi,Xueju Zhang,Yunyang Tian,Long Chen,Zixuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：远距离车辆轨迹重建（long-range vehicle trajectory reconstruction）；场景：固定高速公路摄像头（fixed highway cameras）；传感器：普通监控摄像头；目标类别：车辆。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: Map-RSTNet：地图感知残差序列到序列模型（map-aware residual sequence-to-sequence model），在道路几何对齐的状态空间（road-geometry-aligned state space）中重建远距离轨迹，解码时动态刷新局部道路几何信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LoRFT（22个高速公路监控场景，366,109帧，6,601条人工验证轨迹，2,694,889个边界框，含道路几何标注）；评价指标：ADE（平均位移误差）、FDE（最终位移误差）、5秒RMSE（均方根误差）。

Experiments: Map-RSTNet在LoRFT上相比最强基线，ADE降低11.0%，FDE降低15.4%，5秒RMSE降低10.5%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；3. 仅针对地面固定摄像头场景，不涉及遥感图像中的目标检测任务；4. 未报告模型复杂度、推理速度等效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接参考的点是：论文利用道路几何先验（road geometry）辅助轨迹重建，类似思路在遥感目标检测中可利用地理空间先验（如道路网络、建筑物布局）辅助检测，但与本课题核心方向（在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化）关联微弱。

Abstract: Long-range vehicle trajectories provide important spatio-temporal evidence for traffic safety analysis, autonomous driving evaluation, and data-driven traffic management, yet continuously recovering them from fixed highway cameras remains difficult. As vehicles recede into distant road regions, perspective compression and scale decay often fragment or prematurely terminate automatic tracklets, even when their continuation remains identifiable from motion consistency across neighboring frames. We formulate this problem as recovering the far-range continuation of a vehicle trajectory from a reliable near-field tracklet. We introduce LoRFT, to our knowledge the first open benchmark dedicated to long-range vehicle trajectory reconstruction from fixed highway cameras. LoRFT comprises 22 expressway surveillance scenes, 366,109 video frames, 6,601 manually verified trajectories, 2,694,889 bounding boxes, road-geometry annotations, scene-level splits, and evaluation scripts. We further propose Map-RSTNet, a map-aware residual sequence-to-sequence model that reconstructs distant trajectories in a road-geometry-aligned state space and dynamically refreshes local road geometry during decoding. On LoRFT, Map-RSTNet reduces ADE, FDE, and 5-second RMSE by 11.0%, 15.4%, and 10.5%, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline. These results demonstrate that road-geometry-aware reconstruction can extend usable trajectory records from existing fixed-camera infrastructure. LoRFT provides a reproducible testbed for long-range vehicle trajectory reconstruction.

</details>

*Xujie Zhang,Runyan Du,Song Chang,Jiang Li,Dongliang Shao,Liping Wu,Wei Luo,Xiaochao Qu,Luoqi Liu,Xiaodan Liang*

Research relevance: 1 分（基本无关）。该论文研究的是数字时尚领域的虚拟试穿（virtual try-on），属于计算机视觉与图形学的交叉方向，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等概念完全无关。不涉及任何遥感目标检测或轻量化部署内容。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：虚拟试穿（virtual try-on）；应用场景：数字时尚/电商；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：服装、人体。与遥感目标检测（舰船/飞机/车辆/小目标/旋转框）完全无关。

Model architecture: 端到端无掩码生成框架（mask-free generative framework），核心模块包括 Adaptive 2D Token Packing (ATP) 和 Multi-dimensional Try-on Reward (MTR) 系统。ATP 利用服装稀疏性将异构物品打包到统一 2D 画布并剪枝无信息背景 token；MTR 包含 Semantic Guidance Reward 和 Cloth Distribution Reward。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"prune uninformative background tokens"（剪枝无信息背景 token）是一种 token 级别的剪枝策略，但其目的是缓解高分辨率下的内存爆炸问题，而非面向边缘/星上部署的模型轻量化。不涉及剪枝（结构化/非结构化）、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT 等典型轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然 ATP 方法降低了内存开销，但面向的是 2K 分辨率生成任务，与星上/边缘部署场景无关。

Datasets and metrics: 自建数据集 WearWow-2K，包含原生 2K 分辨率的三元组数据。评价指标和实验设置摘要未详细说明。

Experiments: 实验表明 WearWow 在原生 2K 多服装合成任务上超越了现有商业基线，达到 SOTA。但未提供与遥感目标检测相关的任何实验对比或消融结果。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未讨论遥感场景下的任何问题（如小目标、旋转框、星上资源约束等）。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点有限：（1）ATP 中的 token 剪枝思路（利用稀疏性剪枝无信息 token）在概念上类似于遥感图像中背景区域剪枝，可思考是否适用于遥感大图的稀疏目标检测；（2）但该方法面向生成任务而非检测任务，且未涉及量化/蒸馏等轻量化核心技术，借鉴价值较低。

Abstract: Synthesizing native 2K multi-garment virtual try-on is a formidable frontier in digital fashion, critically bottlenecked by two fundamental limitations: the O(N^2) memory explosion induced by 2k conditions, and the spectral bias of diffusion models that over-smooths high-frequency fabric details. We present WearWow, an end-to-end, mask-free generative framework that pioneers ultra-high-resolution multi-garment synthesis. To mitigate the memory explosion , we propose Adaptive 2D Token Packing (ATP). ATP leverages inherent garment sparsity to algorithmically pack heterogeneous items onto a unified 2D canvas and prune uninformative background tokens, minimizing the effective sequence length and subsequent memory overhead while rigorously preserving 2D spatial priors. To rectify texture degradation, we introduce the Multi-dimensional Try-on Reward (MTR) system. MTR synergizes a Semantic Guidance Reward to explicitly drive tactile restoration with a Cloth Distribution Reward to implicitly anchor the physical distribution, a joint formulation that effectively mitigates the severe reward hacking. Furthermore, we curate WearWow-2K, an extreme-quality dataset comprising native 2K triplets, providing physically correct spatial interactions that naturally empower the model's mask-free generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WearWow establishes a new state-of-the-art, exceeding existing commercial baselines in native 2K multi-garment synthesis.

</details>

Abstract: This work introduces G-MAD, an open-source framework that uses Arma3 to generate synchronized multi-view RGB-T data for aerial object detection. G-MAD addresses key limitations of real-world aerial dataset construction, including limited viewpoint control, imperfect RGB-T alignment and high annotation cost. The framework supports structured scenario specification, controllable multi-view camera placement, simultaneous visible/thermal capture, and automatic bounding box annotation using engine-level geometric metadata. These capabilities enable controlled studies of viewpoint variation, multi-modal fusion, and synthetic-to-real transfer in aerial object detection. Besides, using G-MAD, we construct and release AMOD, a new large-scale multi-view aerial RGB-T object detection benchmark. The source code and the dataset are available at https://unique-chan.github.io/G-MAD-Project.

Abstract: Diffusion Magnetic Resonance Imaging (dMRI) is a powerful tool for probing brain microstructure, but clinical acquisitions are often limited by low out-of-plane resolution, resulting in degraded structural information and reduced utility for advanced analysis. We introduce SIINR (Structurally Informed Implicit Neural Representations), a general framework for super-resoltion of clinical dMRI datasets while quantifying uncertainty in the reconstructed outputs. SIINR utilizes a supervised 3D U-net as a prior and combines it with a self-supervised implicit neural representation (INR) that fuses the high-resolution prior and the original low-resolution data. The INR enables joint modeling across spatial and angular domains, enforces data consistency, and provides analytic approximate posterior distributions for downstream uncertainty quantification. We validate the framework on a diverse set of open-access dMRI datasets, demonstrating that SIINR outperforms standard interpolation methods in both quantitative error metrics and qualitative anatomical fidelity. Experiments on clinical cases, including subjects with multiple sclerosis and brain lesions, illustrate the framework its ability to propagate intensity changes and flag uncertain regions in challenging scenarios. SIINR is flexible, modular, and can be adapted to different upsampling ratios and downstream tasks, providing a principled approach for enhancing clinical dMRI and supporting robust interpretation of derived neuroimaging metrics.

</details>

Abstract: Electronic Theater Programs (ETPs) serve as critical promotional media in the performing arts, comprising a multi-page collection of heterogeneous visual assets such as theatrical posters, performance details, and character portraits. However, existing text-to-image paradigms struggle with such complex design tasks due to their inability to comprehend long-context narratives and maintain visual consistency across multiple distinct pages. To address this, we introduce ETPDesigner, a collaborative Multi-Agent framework that directly synthesizes high-quality ETPs from raw dramatic scripts. Emulating a professional design pipeline, our framework orchestrates specialized agents for semantic script analysis, core poster synthesis, functional background generation, and the stratified composition of character assets. Central to ETPDesigner is a global style anchor mechanism that extracts visual priors from the core poster to enforce strict aesthetic uniformity across all generated components. Furthermore, we elevate the ETP from a static publication to an immersive interactive companion. By integrating portrait animation, customized speech synthesis, and persona-grounded Large Language Models (LLMs), our system enables users to engage in real-time, voice-enabled conversations with the generated virtual characters. To rigorously benchmark this task, we construct ETP-Pro, a domain-specific benchmark of professional theater posters and high-quality character portraits. Extensive evaluations demonstrate our method's superiority in producing semantically faithful, aesthetically consistent, and highly interactive program sets.

</details>


### [42] [OffNadirLoc: Benchmark and Framework for Challenging UAV-to-Satellite Geo-Localization under Large Off-Nadir Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19951)
*Qian Qiao,Wenye Liu,Ting Liu,Jiuhe Shu,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向无人机与卫星大离轴视角交叉视角地理定位的OffNadirLoc基准和ONLoc框架，通过结构感知上下文加权和多视图一致性学习提升跨视角匹配性能。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及卫星遥感（satellite imagery）和无人机（UAV）的交叉视角地理定位（cross-view geo-localization），属于遥感场景理解范畴。但核心任务是**地理定位/图像检索**，而非**目标检测**；且论文**完全不涉及**模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、星上部署等任何轻量化或部署相关技术。因此与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关。论文聚焦交叉视角地理定位而非目标检测，且完全不涉及轻量化部署，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接参考价值有限。但如果课题涉及遥感场景理解或多视角特征学习，可选择性阅读其结构感知加权机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：交叉视角地理定位（cross-view geo-localization），即无人机图像与卫星图像之间的位置匹配。应用场景：大离轴视角（large off-nadir views）下的UAV-to-satellite定位。传感器类型：可见光光学图像（UAV和satellite imagery）。目标类别：场景级匹配，非特定目标检测。

Model architecture: ONLoc框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 结构感知上下文加权机制（structure-aware contextual weighting mechanism），动态强调可靠局部特征、抑制模糊或重复区域；(2) 视图一致性学习策略（view-coherent learning strategy），将一张卫星图与多视角UAV图视为语义组，进行集合级监督学习，替代传统的成对对比学习。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。无法判断星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 提出新基准OffNadirLoc（大离轴视角UAV-to-satellite地理定位数据集）。在OffNadirLoc和4个近天底（near-nadir）数据集上进行实验。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称（推测为Recall@K等地理定位常用指标）。

Experiments: 在OffNadirLoc基准和4个近天底数据集上，ONLoc方法一致优于现有最先进方法（state-of-the-art），并展现出对未见数据集的强零样本泛化能力（zero-shot generalization）。

Limitations: (1) 与目标检测任务无关，无法直接用于遥感目标检测；(2) 未提供任何模型复杂度或推理效率数据；(3) 未讨论星上部署或实时性需求；(4) 结构感知加权机制可能引入额外计算开销，但未量化分析。

Ideas for my research: (1) 结构感知上下文加权机制的思想可借鉴到遥感目标检测中，用于在复杂背景中增强目标区域特征、抑制背景噪声；(2) 多视图一致性学习策略可启发遥感卫星多视角目标检测中的特征融合方法；(3) 但该论文本身不提供轻量化方法，需自行设计轻量化方案。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization between UAV and satellite imagery remains a fundamental yet highly challenging task, especially under large off-nadir views where drastic perspective distortions, occlusions, and appearance gaps occur. Existing benchmarks and methods primarily focus on near-nadir scenarios and often overlook the importance of structural scene understanding and intra-domain relational constraints, limiting their performance in real-world deployments. In this work, we introduce OffNadirLoc, a new benchmark for large off-nadir UAV-to-satellite geo-localization. To tackle the unique challenges posed by off-nadir perspectives, we further propose ONLoc, a framework that incorporates a structure-aware contextual weighting mechanism to dynamically emphasize reliable local features while suppressing ambiguous or repetitive regions. Additionally, we design a view-coherent learning strategy, which treats one satellite image and the corresponding UAV images from multiple views as a cohesive semantic group. This set-level supervision enables the model to learn viewpoint-invariant and discriminative features, making it more effective at capturing multi-view consistency than conventional pairwise contrastive learning. Extensive experiments on the OffNadirLoc benchmark and four near-nadir datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches while exhibiting strong zero-shot generalization to unseen datasets without additional training. The code will be released at https://montalario.github.io/offnadirloc/.

</details>

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；3. 数据集为近景温室图像，非遥感影像；4. 模型复杂度、推理速度等关键部署指标缺失。

Abstract: Accurate yield forecasting at the individual-plant level is critical for precision agriculture and supply-chain planning, yet public datasets capturing both visual growth dynamics and per-plant measurement labels are scarce. In this paper, we introduce a novel, annotated image time-series dataset of 691 sweet pepper plants monitored over two growing seasons, comprising 4837 images with per-plant fruit counts categorized by maturity. We propose a multimodal deep learning framework that fuses high-dimensional image features, extracted using the DinoV3 encoder, with numerical count measurements. Our architecture utilizes a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network to model temporal dependencies and handles irregular sampling intervals common in greenhouse monitoring. Through quantitative experiments, we demonstrate that this multimodal approach reduces RMSE over a persistence baseline by 33% and 38% in the 2022 and 2023 seasons, respectively, with a further 1.2% average gain over a measurement-only model. Furthermore, we employ Deep Ensembles and Gaussian Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) to provide calibrated uncertainty estimates, with an Uncertainty Calibration Error (UCE) ranging from 0.39 to 0.89 depending on the cross-season evaluation direction, offering a principled confidence signal for real-world agricultural decision-making. We release the dataset and code to support reproducible research and to accelerate development of data-driven yield forecasting methods for horticultural crops.

</details>

<details>

Abstract: Stereo matching is a fundamental task in 3D reconstruction. Despite remarkable advances, the prevailing paradigms formulate stereo matching as a deterministic regression problem, collapsing the multimodal distribution modeling into a single-point estimation. This formulation suffers from a regression-to-mean bias, frequently struggling with ambiguous regions. In contrast, we introduce a prior-guided generative framework that integrates deterministic matching regression and generative distribution modeling within a complementary formulation. Built upon this formulation, we introduce StereoFlow through three key components: (i) a two-stage progressive cascade matching network that progressively produces multi-resolution stereo conditions with complementary matching cues; (ii) a pixel diffusion transformer (termed StereoDiT) with a frequency-decoupled architecture for modeling correspondence ambiguity; (iii) a few-step flow matching objective (termed Transition Flow Matching) for efficient optimization. In summary, \textsc{\textbf{StereoFlow}} achieves strong geometric consistency and rich fine-grained details in ill-posed, discontinuous regions and under zero-shot generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed StereoFlow establishes multiple state-of-the-art results across benchmarks, including Scene Flow, KITTI, ETH3D, and Middlebury.

</details>

Ideas for my research: 1. 多时相分析思路可借鉴：在轨遥感目标检测中，不同季节/光照条件对模型性能的影响值得系统评估；2. 跨时相迁移实验设计方法可用于评估轻量化模型在不同成像条件下的鲁棒性；3. 但本文与轻量化目标检测核心方向偏离较大，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Sentinel-2 imagery offers open access, global coverage, and frequent revisit times, making it attractive for practical building mapping at scale; however, its native 10m resolution makes building vs non-building classification challenging, particularly for small or sub-pixel buildings, and performance can vary with both seasonality and the heterogeneity of built-up environments. This paper introduces a Sentinel-2 building-detection framework designed to systematically quantify these effects and to support more formalised, practice-oriented model selection. We construct a dedicated multi-temporal Sentinel-2 dataset over the Warsaw region and derive binary ground-truth masks by rasterising official Polish topographic database (BDOT10k) building footprints onto the Sentinel-2 pixel grid. Using two established convolutional segmentation backbones (U-Net and DeepLabV3+), we first perform scene-specific fine-tuning to select a robust architecture and identify the best monthly models for L1C and L2A products separately. We then conduct cross-temporal inference by applying each best monthly model to all scenes, enabling an assessment of (i) which months provide favourable training and inference conditions, (ii) how performance transfers between seasons, (iii) the impact of processing level, and (iv) how these effects differ across built-up typologies. Based on these results, we provide practical guidance for routine Sentinel-2 building classification under varying acquisition periods and settlement characteristics.

</details>


### [46] [A Systematic Benchmark of Intensity Normalisation Methods for 3D Knee MRI Segmentation and Cross-Domain Generalisability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20028)
*Oliver Mills,Philip Conaghan,Samuel Relton*

Main category: cs.CV

Abstract: Robust out-of-the-box performance is essential for the clinical deployment of deep learning models in medical imaging. An important but underexplored factor affecting model generalisability is intensity normalisation, particularly for magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), where image intensities vary across scanners and protocols. In this study, we systematically compared seven normalisation methods and their impact on the performance of a 3D U-Net model for meniscus segmentation from knee MRI. The methods included standard scaling approaches, histogram-based techniques, and a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM)-based method. Models were trained on the IWOAI 2019 dataset and evaluated on both internal and external test sets (SKM-TEA) to assess generalisability. Performance was similar internally but differences were significant on external data, with Z-score, Nyúl histogram matching, and CLAHE showing greater robustness than other methods. However, these differences were small compared to the significant performance drop observed between datasets. Overall, while intensity normalisation had a measurable effect on model generalisability, its impact was limited relative to the effects of domain shift, highlighting the need for complementary strategies for robust deployment.

Abstract: We propose importance-aware pruning for diffusion models, a training-free framework that prioritizes preserving parameters critical to semantically salient image regions. To do so, we incorporate spatial importance maps -- derived from conditioning signals or model attention -- into the pruning objective. This produces parameter rankings aligned with perceptual relevance rather than uniform reconstruction error. On MS-COCO dataset, our proposed approach consistently retains subject fidelity and structural correctness at high compression ratios where conventional pruning causes visible degradation. These results demonstrate that content-aware objectives are key to perceptually faithful compression of generative models.

</details>

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。摘要未说明模型参数量、FLOPs、是否可迁移至遥感场景、是否支持遥感目标检测任务。层次化高斯表示在遥感大场景下的泛化性未知。

Abstract: Vision-centric 3D occupancy prediction provides dense scene representations essential for autonomous driving and robotic navigation, yet existing methods struggle to scale to high voxel resolutions due to prohibitive computational costs. To address this, we introduce GaussianSeed, a progressive multi-scale Gaussian occupancy prediction framework that organizes primitives into a coarse-to-fine hierarchy. Benefiting from this hierarchical design, GaussianSeed effectively circumvents the memory bottlenecks inherent in dense representations, successfully scaling to a $0.1\text{m}$ spatial resolution while maintaining real-time inference capabilities. To comprehensively evaluate high-resolution geometric perception, we further construct TJScenes, a panoramic six-camera occupancy dataset with highly detailed $0.1\text{m}$ annotations. Extensive experiments on Occ3D-nuScenes and TJScenes demonstrate that GaussianSeed delivers the lowest latency among all evaluated methods while maintaining highly competitive accuracy, advancing the efficiency-quality frontier of high-resolution 3D occupancy prediction.

</details>

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Factor-Informed Uncertainty Distillation (FIUD)，一种利用可解释图像质量因素（光照、清晰度、眼部可见性等）通过教师-学生框架蒸馏不确定性信号的方法，用于无约束场景下的深度视线估计。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：gaze estimation（视线估计）；应用场景：无约束环境下的视线预测与不确定性估计；传感器类型：RGB摄像头（人脸图像）；目标类别：人眼/视线方向。与遥感场景无关。

Model architecture: 教师-学生框架（teacher-student framework）：梯度提升树（gradient-boosting）作为教师模型，预测基于可解释因素（光照、清晰度、眼部可见性、对称性）的视线误差；神经网络学生模型通过课程学习（curriculum learning）和排序监督（ranking supervision）蒸馏不确定性信号到轻量级单次不确定性头（lightweight single-pass uncertainty head）。

Lightweight method: 使用了知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）和轻量级单次不确定性头设计。但蒸馏目标是不确定性信号而非模型压缩，轻量化不是核心贡献。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到采样方法（sampling-based methods）对实时使用成本过高，而FIUD是单次前向（single-pass），但未给出具体速度指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ETH-XGaze, Gaze360, MPIIFaceGaze（>300k样本）；评价指标：不确定性质量（uncertainty quality）、误差秩相关性（error rank correlation）、选择性预测性能（selective prediction）。

Experiments: 与确定性方法（deterministic）和基于采样的基线（sampling-based baselines）对比，FIUD在不确定性、误差秩相关性和选择性预测方面均有提升，在无约束场景下增益最大。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 未提供模型参数量、FLOPs、推理延迟等轻量化关键指标；3. 教师模型使用梯度提升树，在星上部署场景中可能不够高效；4. 仅针对视线估计任务，未验证在目标检测任务上的泛化性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可间接参考：1. 教师-学生框架中利用可解释因素（如遥感图像中的云雾遮挡、光照条件、分辨率退化等）作为不确定性信号来源，用于遥感目标检测的置信度校准；2. 课程学习（curriculum learning）和排序监督（ranking supervision）策略可迁移到遥感检测模型的训练中；3. 轻量级单次不确定性头（lightweight single-pass uncertainty head）的设计思路可用于在轨检测模型的不确定性估计模块。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Deep gaze estimation works well in controlled capture but degrades in unconstrained settings, where systems must reject unreliable predictions. Single-pass uncertainty (e.g., heteroscedastic regression) infers uncertainty from pixels without explicit input-validity cues, while sampling based methods are often too costly for real time use. We propose Factor-Informed Uncertainty Distillation (FIUD), a teacher-student framework that aligns uncertainty with interpretable image-quality failure modes. A gradient-boosting teacher predicts expected gaze error from factors such as illumination, sharpness, eye visibility and symmetry; a neural student distills these signals via curriculum learning and ranking supervision into a lightweight single-pass uncertainty head. Across ETH-XGaze, Gaze360, and MPIIFaceGaze (>300k samples), FIUD improves uncertainty, error rank correlation and selective prediction versus deterministic and sampling-based baselines, with the largest gains in unconstrained settings.

</details>

*Sina Amirrajab,Volker Vehof,Michael Bietenbeck,Nuriye Akyol,Redouane Bouras,Khuraman Isgandarova,Alexandru Zlibut,Philipp Stalling,Ali Yilmaz*

Experiments: 单模型最高AUC：HCM 0.937，CA 0.945；集成策略进一步提升：HCM AUC=0.959，CA AUC=0.966，NOR AUC=0.872，DCM AUC=0.848，ICM AUC=0.840。消融实验摘要未说明。

Abstract: Aims: Cardiovascular magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging enables non-invasive assessment of myocardial structure, function, and pathology, but requires substantial experience in interpretation of CMR images that could be supported by artificial intelligence (AI)-based models. However, use of AI models for enhanced CMR reading is limited by labor-intensive data curation, suboptimal model performance, and unclear implementation pathways. Methods and results: We developed an automated data curation pipeline for CMR-based cardiovascular disease (CVD) diagnosis, integrating open-source locally-run large language models (LLMs) to extract diagnostic labels from narrative CMR reports and preprocessing multimodal imaging data, including cine and late-gadolinium-enhancement (LGE) CMR sequences. Three vision foundation models (DINO, VST, UMedPT) were fine-tuned across these modalities in a two-stage approach. The dataset comprised hypertrophic cardiomyopathy (HCM), dilated cardiomyopathy (DCM), ischemic cardiomyopathy (ICM), cardiac amyloidosis (CA), and normal controls (NOR). A total of 988 curated cases were randomly divided into 742 for training and 246 for validation. Fine-tuned AI-models achieved high discriminative diagnostic performance on an independent test set comprising 1067 patients , with individual AUC-ROC values of up to 0.937 for the correct diagnosis of HCM and 0.945 for cardiac amyloidosis. Ensemble strategies combining multiple models and modalities further improved AI-based diagnostic accuracy and robustness, achieving the highest overall diagnostic performance for HCM (AUC=0.959, CI [0.936-0.978]), CA (AUC=0.966, CI [0.939-0.986]), NOR (AUC=0.872, CI [0.852-0.894]), DCM (AUC=0.848, CI [0.808-0.885]) and ICM (AUC=0.840, CI [0.809-0.868]). All training and inference code, along with the trained model weights, are publicly available on https://github.com/sinaamirrajab/CMR_CVD.

</details>

Abstract: Contextual entrainment is the tendency of a model to let auxiliary context in its input pull its output, independently of whether that context is relevant, true, or even meaningful. Recently, it has been identified and given a mechanistic account in unimodal language models. Whether and how it manifests in vision-language models (VLMs) is, by contrast, largely unexamined, and the field lacks a purpose-built instrument with which to investigate it. We take the position that studying contextual entrainment in VLMs requires more than porting an existing text-only benchmark to the multimodal setting: it requires a taxonomically structured, dual-modality instrument whose conditions are constructed around the item at hand (the depicted image in the textual stream, the textual query in the visual stream). We argue that the move to VLMs is substantive rather than incremental. It makes entrainment a dual phenomenon, drivable independently by textual and by visual context, and it opens a veracity distinction (context that is false of the depicted scene yet possible in the world) that has no counterpart in the unimodal, world-knowledge-only formulation of prior work. To make this position concrete and actionable, we introduce ENTRAP-VL (ENTRainment Assessment Probe for Vision and Language), a manually curated dataset of 1,500 items across eight categories, organized by a taxonomy that spans two axes, i.e., the association of context with the item and its relationship to truth, and split into a textual-entrainment stream (eight context conditions) and a visual-entrainment stream (three context conditions). We do not claim to measure entrainment in any particular model; we provide the instrument, the taxonomy that motivates it, and the evaluation protocols it enables, so that the community can investigate the phenomenon rigorously. We will release the dataset and its documentation publicly.

</details>


### [52] [RIM: A Retrieval-In-Matching Framework for Cross-Domain Global Visual Localization of UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20116)
*Xin Li,Siyuan Duan,Shang Wang,Zhimin Mao,Bingliang Hu,Geng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RIM方法，通过冻结DINOv2-B检索器并蒸馏局部描述子解码器，实现无人机全局视觉定位中检索与匹配的共享骨干，在保持精度的同时加速端到端定位流程。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及遥感参考地图和无人机视觉定位，但核心任务是6-DoF位姿估计（视觉定位/重定位），而非目标检测。轻量化方面仅涉及蒸馏一个局部描述子解码器以复用检索骨干，并非面向遥感目标检测的模型轻量化。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向相关性较弱。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关。论文核心是无人机视觉定位而非遥感目标检测，轻量化仅涉及蒸馏且未面向在轨部署。除非课题涉及无人机定位与遥感地图匹配的交叉方向，否则阅读优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：无人机全局视觉定位（6-DoF位姿估计）；场景：GNSS拒止环境下的无人机定位；传感器类型：无人机可见光图像 + Google 3D Tiles遥感参考地图；目标类别：无目标检测，关注场景级匹配。

Model architecture: DINOv2-B作为检索骨干（冻结）；蒸馏出的局部描述子解码器（复用DINOv2的token field）；浅层VGG19细节流（detail stream）；SALAD作为检索基线；RIM框架将检索与局部描述子共享同一骨干。

Lightweight method: 知识蒸馏（Knowledge Distillation）：从冻结的DINOv2-B检索器蒸馏出一个局部描述子解码器，避免额外部署第二个大模型骨干。但未涉及剪枝、量化、NAS等典型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 端到端定位查询（含检索、候选匹配、几何验证）共67.9 ms，比最强分离稀疏匹配基线快1.8倍，比RoMa快40倍以上。参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等摘要未说明。未提及星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 训练数据：从Google 3D Tiles采样多视角参考视图；测试集：EPFL Urbanscape（重建数据集）、自采集Chang'an Park数据集（与训练数据地理不重叠）；指标：Recall@1（25m/50m下全3D距离度量）、端到端延迟（ms）。

Experiments: RIM在EPFL上Recall@1比SALAD提升8.55/13.77个百分点（25/50m），在Park上提升4.45/8.94个百分点；Top-K=5时端到端67.9 ms；与10种检索基线对比均取得优势。

Limitations: 1. 任务为视觉定位而非目标检测，与课题核心方向不一致；2. 轻量化仅涉及蒸馏，未涉及剪枝/量化/NAS等；3. 未报告参数量、FLOPs、模型大小等部署关键指标；4. 未在卫星在轨场景验证；5. 依赖DINOv2-B这一大模型，虽共享骨干但基础模型仍较大。

Ideas for my research: 1. 共享骨干的蒸馏思路可借鉴：在遥感目标检测中，可考虑让检测头和辅助任务（如分割/旋转框回归）共享同一轻量骨干，减少冗余计算；2. 跨域微调策略（pose-near positives + hard negatives）可用于遥感域适应；3. 端到端延迟测量方法可参考，但需补充参数量和FLOPs报告。

Abstract: Global visual localization of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) using remote-sensing reference maps has attracted increasing attention. However, acquisition-time and imaging-platform differences between UAV and reference imagery induce substantial cross-domain appearance and viewpoint shifts, challenging robust six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) pose estimation. We address these shifts by sampling UAV-viewpoint reference views from Google 3D Tiles across locations, altitudes, and orientations. A two-stage cross-domain fine-tuning recipe adapts SALAD using pose-near positives and geographically distant hard negatives, while local geometric consistency re-ranks the Top-K candidates. We further propose Retrieval-In-Matching (RIM), which freezes the adapted DINOv2-B retriever and distils a local-descriptor decoder that reuses its token field alongside a shallow VGG19 detail stream. One query-side DINOv2-B forward thus serves both SALAD retrieval and local description, eliminating a second foundation-model backbone while preserving retrieval descriptors by construction. We evaluate RIM zero-shot on the reconstructed EPFL Urbanscape and self-collected Chang'an Park datasets, both geographically disjoint from the training data. RIM outperforms ten recent retrieval baseline families. At 25/50 m under the full 3D distance metric, it improves Recall@1 over SALAD by 8.55/13.77 percentage points on EPFL and 4.45/8.94 points on Park. At Top-K=5, the complete measured localization query, including retrieval, candidate matching, and robust geometric verification, takes 67.9 ms end-to-end: 1.8 times faster than the strongest separate sparse-matching baseline and over 40 times faster than RoMa, while achieving comparable re-ranking accuracy. These results establish an efficient and deployable pipeline for UAV global visual localization in GNSS-challenged environments.

</details>


### [53] [HeadCast: Casting Attention Heads for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20125)
*Jinliang Shen,Lianghao Su,Zheming Li,Kang He,ZiLiang Lai,Yanbing Jiang,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeadCast 是一种无需训练、即插即用的视频扩散模型加速框架，通过对自回归模型中注意力头的分类（Sink/Dummy/Spatial/Global）实现 KV cache 的分路径管理，在保持视频质量的同时加速推理。

Research relevance: 1 分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自回归视频扩散模型的推理加速，属于视频生成/视频合成领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1 分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议优先阅读。仅当对注意力头冗余分析或 training-free 加速策略感兴趣时可作为旁路参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频扩散模型推理加速；应用场景：长视频/流式视频合成；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于自回归（AR）视频扩散模型，核心创新是对注意力头进行分类（Sink, Dummy, Spatial, Global），将统一的 KV cache 重构为头特定的路径（head-specific pathways）。

Lightweight method: 方法属于推理加速（inference acceleration）范畴，具体手段为 KV cache 管理优化（基于注意力头分类的 cache 路径重构），无需训练（training-free），即插即用（plug-and-play）。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS 等经典轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅报告了加速比：720P 下最高 1.62x，1080P 下最高 1.95x。未提及星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 评估指标：VBench（视频质量评估指标）。摘要未说明具体使用的视频数据集名称。

Experiments: 在多种 state-of-the-art AR 模型上验证，加速比 720P 下最高 1.62x，1080P 下最高 1.95x，VBench 质量与 full attention 持平，且 flicker-free。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 仅针对视频扩散模型的注意力机制加速，不适用于 CNN 或纯 Transformer 检测器；3. 加速比有限（<2x），对于星上实时推理需求可能不足；4. 未报告参数量、FLOPs、能耗等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可间接借鉴：1. 注意力头分类的思想（Sink/Dummy/Spatial/Global）可启发遥感检测模型中多头注意力的冗余分析，识别哪些头对遥感目标检测贡献小从而可跳过；2. "training-free + plug-and-play" 的加速范式对星上部署有吸引力，因为星上难以进行重训练；3. 对 Global 头的保留策略（保持长程一致性）可类比遥感大场景中保持全局上下文的需求。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models have become a promising paradigm for long and streaming video synthesis, but the continuously growing Key-Value (KV) cache makes attention the dominant inference cost, especially at high resolution where each frame contributes many tokens. Existing remedies either evict the cache with coarse heuristics that cause inter-frame flickering, or require model re-training. We propose HeadCast, a training-free, plug-and-play acceleration framework built on the observation that a pre-trained AR model's attention heads exhibit stable, heterogeneous behaviors. After a short warm-up, HeadCast performs a one-time classification at the maximum-noise step that sorts every head into one of four archetypes: Sink, Dummy, Spatial, and Global, and restructures the monolithic KV cache into head-specific pathways. Crucially, it retains the Global heads that preserve the long-range temporal consistency aggressive eviction destroys. Because the Spatial pathway operates on a fixed-size grid, its savings grow with resolution: across state-of-the-art AR models, HeadCast accelerates inference by up to 1.62x at 720P and 1.95x at 1080P, while keeping VBench quality on par with full attention and largely flicker-free. Code is available at https://github.com/sjlgaga/HeadCast .

</details>

*Sriprabha Ramanarayanan,Rahul G. S.,Mohammad Al Fahim,Keerthi Ram,Ramesh Venkatesan,Mohanasankar Sivaprakasam*

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学影像（MRI）重建任务，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱遥感、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关场景；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：加速MRI重建（医学影像重建）；应用场景：多模态MRI数据重建；传感器类型：MRI（磁共振成像）；目标类别：无（非检测任务）。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 提出SHFormer，包含两个核心模块：(i) 光谱滤波CNN（spectral filtering CNN）用于捕获模态特定的可迁移特征；(ii) 动态高通核生成Transformer（dynamic high-pass kernel generation transformer）专注于高频细节。整体基于神经调制的判别式多光谱注意力机制（neuromodulation-based discriminative multi-spectral AM）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。论文未讨论星上或边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多模态MRI数据（具体数据集名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：PSNR（~1 dB提升）、SSIM（~0.01提升）；实验设置：监督学习、自监督学习、扩散模型训练、闭集和开集泛化实验、可解释性分析。

Experiments: 在监督/自监督学习、扩散模型训练、闭集/开集泛化场景下进行对比实验，最佳提升约1 dB PSNR和0.01 SSIM。代码已开源。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 论文未讨论模型效率（参数量、计算量、推理速度）；3. 仅在MRI数据上验证，领域迁移到遥感场景需大量重新设计。

Ideas for my research: 1. 论文中的"动态高通核生成"思路（关注高频细节）可抽象借鉴到遥感小目标检测中，小目标往往对应高频信息，但需重新设计为轻量版本；2. "多光谱注意力"概念在遥感多光谱/高光谱图像中可能有启发，但本文的"多光谱"指MRI多模态频率域，与遥感多光谱成像含义不同，需注意区分；3. 整体而言，本文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的借鉴价值非常有限。

Abstract: Attention Mechanism (AM) selectively focuses on essential information for imaging tasks and captures relationships between distant pixel neighborhoods to compute feature representations. Accelerated MRI reconstruction benefits from AM, as the imaging process involves Fourier domain measurements that influence image representation non-locally. However, AM-based models are more adept at capturing low-frequency information with limited capacity for high-frequency representations, restricting models to smooth reconstruction. Additionally, AM-based models need mode-specific retraining for multimodal MRI data, as their knowledge is restricted to local contextual variations that may be inadequate to capture transferable features across heterogeneous domains. To address these challenges, we propose a neuromodulation-based discriminative multi-spectral AM for scalable MRI reconstruction that can (i) propagate context-aware high-frequency details for high-quality reconstruction, and (ii) capture features reusable across deviated unseen domains in multimodal MRI. The proposed network consists of a spectral filtering CNN to capture mode-specific transferable features and a dynamic high-pass kernel generation transformer focusing on high-frequency details. We evaluate our model on comparative studies in supervised and self-supervised learning, diffusion model-based training, closed-set and open-set generalization under heterogeneous MRI data, and interpretation-based analysis. Our method offers scalable, high-quality reconstruction with best improvement margins of ~1 dB in PSNR and ~0.01 in SSIM under unseen scenarios. Code: https://github.com/sriprabhar/SHFormer

</details>


### [55] [StreamHOI: Interaction-aware Temporal Memory Adaptation for Streaming HOI Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20174)
*Zejing Rao,Haoxian Zhang,Xiaoqiang Liu,Yiping Meng,Guoxin Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Fan Tang,Tong-Yee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamHOI提出了一种低延迟流式框架，用于长时程人-物交互（HOI）视频生成，通过HOI感知的块级内存偏好分析和偏置引导训练，在0.75s首块延迟下达到17.6 FPS。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于**human-object interaction (HOI) video generation**（人-物交互视频生成），属于视频生成/交互生成领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对流式推理的memory管理机制有跨领域借鉴需求，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
