Limitations: 论文完全聚焦于machine unlearning的理论分析，与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化无任何直接或间接关联。无法为本课题提供可借鉴的技术方法。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于机器学习理论方向，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究目标、技术路线和应用场景均不匹配。

Abstract: We study machine unlearning: the removal of memorized training data from a trained model. Specifically, we investigate the algorithmic complexity of certified unlearning from an optimization perspective. We formalize the goal of an unlearning algorithm as simultaneously achieving certified unlearning and optimization accuracy. Utilizing the notion of uniformly convex regularizers, we prove new bounds on the distance between initial and unlearned models using a novel substitute for generalization error. Thus we theoretically demonstrate that if the removed data is well-predicted by the unlearned model, the corresponding optimization problem is simple. Furthermore, we develop a new second-order unlearning algorithm with an anisotropic Gaussian mechanism and state-of-the-art global convergence. We prove fast rates for our method in achieving certified unlearning for linear models with quasi-self-concordant losses. As a direct application, our theory covers unlearning for logistic and exponential regressions and shows a provable benefit of utilizing second-order information compared to first-order unlearning methods.

</details>


### [147] [OLEDLM: A Unified Language Model for OLED Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20194)
*Fukang Wen,Yuchong Tang,Jingyuan Li,Beichen Wang,Yixuan Jiang,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yaxuan Liu,Shunyu Wang,Zuoqiang Shi,Yi Zhu,Yanan Zhu,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于因果语言模型（LLaMA架构）的OLED分子逆设计框架，通过强化学习生成满足光电性质约束的SMILES序列。

Research relevance: 1分。论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。研究对象是OLED有机发光二极管材料，属于计算化学/材料科学领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或边缘部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：分子逆设计（inverse molecular design）；场景：OLED材料发现；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: LLaMA-style transformer（因果语言模型）+ BERT（属性预测器），用于分子SMILES序列生成。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 大规模OLED数据集（摘要未说明具体名称和规模）；评价指标：结构有效性（structural validity）、光电性质（激发能、振子强度）；DFT验证。

Experiments: 通过DFT验证证明框架能生成结构有效且光电性质优化的新型OLED候选分子。摘要未提供与基线方法的定量对比结果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或部署相关内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于材料科学领域的分子生成，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: The development of organic light-emitting diode (OLED) materials faces the compounded challenges of an astronomically large chemical space, stringent quantum-chemical constraints, and a scarcity of labeled data. Although the question of OLED generation is important, few models have been trained effectively for this specific domain. We propose an inverse molecular design framework based on causal language models: given target optoelectronic properties (e.g., excitation energy, oscillator strength), our model directly generates OLED SMILES sequences satisfying the specified constraints. We employ a multi-stage strategy: first, we establish a foundational chemical language model using a LLaMA-style transformer architecture. To the best of our knowledge, this represents the first successful adaptation of LLMs specifically for the OLED domain, bridging the gap between generic molecular generation and the stringent structural requirements of optoelectronic materials. Second, we fine-tune property predictors based on a BERT model pre-trained on our large-scale OLED dataset. Then, we perform Reinforcement Learning on our fine-tuned model, leveraging our property predictor, for better SMILES generation. Finally, through DFT verification, we demonstrate that our framework can efficiently navigate the OLED chemical space, generating novel candidates with high structural validity and optimized optoelectronic properties.

</details>


### [148] [The Quadrilateral Loss: Additivity as a Measurable Behavior of Dense Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20201)
*Antonio Di Cecco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出四边形损失（quadrilateral loss）作为可微惩罚项，将加性模型中的可加性约束从架构强制转变为可度量的行为约束，并系统比较了多种实现精确可加性的技术路线。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何关联。论文聚焦于可解释机器学习中的加性模型（additive models），属于泛机器学习/可解释AI方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：加性模型的可解释性研究；应用场景：可解释机器学习；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 涉及加性模型（additive models）的多种实现方式：structural masks（结构掩码）、behavioral penalties（行为惩罚）、weight decay（权重衰减）、backfitting（回拟合）、shared-section model（共享段模型）、bagged boosted stumps（袋装提升树桩）。核心贡献是四边形损失（quadrilateral loss）这一可微惩罚项。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是模型可解释性中的加性约束，与模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等均未提及）。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要提到在"small datasets"上进行了实验，但未给出具体数据集名称。评价指标涉及加性度量（additivity）和准确率（accuracy），以及per-feature surrender curves（逐特征投降曲线）等可解释性指标。未涉及目标检测相关指标（mAP、F1等）。

Experiments: 主要实验发现：(1) 大多数已学习的交互几乎可以无代价移除；(2) 在小数据集上适度的惩罚可同时提升准确率和可加性；(3) 预正则化的交互幅度几乎无法预测正则化模型保留的内容；(4) 行为约束先于结构约束优于权重空间约束；(5) 不同数据体制下排序会反转；(6) 不同收敛路线在形状函数上达成一致。还记录了三种静默失败模式。未涉及目标检测或遥感场景实验。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；(2) 论文讨论的是加性模型的可解释性，而非检测模型的精度或效率；(3) 未涉及任何遥感数据或目标检测任务；(4) 未提供任何部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接相关的点是：论文提出的"行为约束优于结构约束"的思想，在轻量化方法中知识蒸馏（behavioral mimicry）也属于行为约束，但论文讨论的是加性约束而非模型压缩，关联性非常弱。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Additive models buy interpretability by forbidding feature interactions, a constraint that neural instantiations enforce architecturally. We introduce the quadrilateral loss, a differentiable penalty that treats additivity as a measurable behavior instead: a second-order mixed difference on pairs of training points swapping one coordinate, which vanishes if and only if the coordinate carries no interaction, remains informative for piecewise-linear networks, and equals in expectation the per-coordinate interaction mass of the interventional Shapley-GAM. The loss turns additivity into a dial - most learned interactions prove removable almost for free, and on small datasets a moderate penalty improves accuracy and additivity simultaneously - and into an online observable: its per-feature surrender curves show, across seeds and datasets, that pre-regularization interaction magnitude barely predicts what a regularized model retains, undermining post-hoc interaction rankings. Against this instrument we compare routes to exact additivity, spanning structural masks, behavioral penalties (optionally crystallized into exact structure), weight decay, backfitting, the shared-section model, and bagged boosted stumps: constraining behavior before structure dominates weight-space constraints, rankings reverse between data regimes, and converging routes agree on the shape functions themselves. Three silent failure modes we document share one anatomy: guarantees imported into settings that quietly void their preconditions.

</details>


### [149] [ELSAA: Efficient Low-Rank and Sparse Attention Approximation for Training Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20214)
*Mahdi Heidari,Mohammad Mahdi Rahimi,Jaekyun Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ELSAA，一种低秩+稀疏混合注意力近似方法，避免显式构造N×N注意力矩阵，以支持Transformer处理更长序列。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、轻量化部署基本无关。它解决的是NLP/长序列Transformer中注意力计算效率问题，属于通用Transformer架构改进，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明具体任务和应用场景。从问题定义看，面向长序列Transformer的注意力机制加速，典型应用场景为NLP长文本或长序列建模，非遥感/卫星/目标检测场景。

Model architecture: ELSAA（Efficient Low-rank and Sparse Approximation of Attention）。在Q,K,V经密集投影得到后，将注意力算子分解为稀疏分支（捕获高相似度交互）和低秩分支（汇总全局扩散交互），并通过分母感知融合项（denominator-aware fusion）对两分支进行归一化融合。

Lightweight method: 属于注意力计算层面的效率优化（低秩+稀疏近似），但并非模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。不涉及参数量压缩或推理加速的硬件部署优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然方法避免了完整N×N注意力矩阵，但未给出具体计算效率指标，也未讨论边缘/星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明实验结果、对比方法和消融实验。

Limitations: 摘要仅给出方法设计，未讨论局限性。潜在问题：稀疏+低秩混合近似的精度损失边界未说明；分母感知融合项的计算开销未量化；该方法在视觉/遥感任务上的有效性未验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但可关注以下思路：(1) 低秩+稀疏混合近似思想可迁移到遥感大模型（如ViT-based检测器）的注意力计算加速；(2) 分母感知融合的归一化策略可能对遥感图像中多尺度目标（小目标vs大目标）的注意力分配有启发；(3) 但该方法本身不解决遥感目标检测的特定问题（小目标、旋转框、密集排列等），也不涉及模型轻量化的核心手段（剪枝/量化/蒸馏）。

Abstract: The quadratic $N\times N$ attention score matrix remains a central obstacle to extending Transformers to longer input lengths. Existing efficient attention methods usually reduce this bottleneck by either imposing sparsity, so that each query attends to only a small subset of keys, or by using low-rank/kernel sketches, so that global interactions are compressed into a lower-dimensional representation. We propose \emph{ELSAA}, an efficient low-rank and sparse approximation of attention. Importantly, ELSAA does \emph{not} decompose the learned projection or output matrices of the Transformer into sparse and low-rank factors. Instead, after dense projections produce $Q,K,V$, ELSAA approximates the induced attention score operator itself: a sparse branch captures selected high-similarity interactions, while a low-rank branch summarizes diffuse global interactions. Since the two branches can be normalized over supports with very different denominator mass, ELSAA introduces a denominator-aware fusion term that scales the sparse branch according to its estimated attention mass relative to the low-rank branch. This gives a practical framework for constructing low-rank and sparse attention outputs without materializing the full quadratic score matrix, aiming to enable longer-context training while preserving both sharp token-level interactions and broad contextual mixing.

</details>


### [150] [User-Centric Modeling of Transactional Sequences with Explainable State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20228)
*Ivan Palagin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoLES对比表示学习与Mamba状态空间模型混合的方法，用于用户交易事件序列建模，在年龄预测、产品获取和购买预测三个任务上验证了效果。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于用户行为序列建模（transactional event sequences），属于推荐系统/用户画像领域，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：用户交易事件序列建模（年龄预测、产品获取预测、购买预测）；场景：用户行为分析；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: CoLES（对比表示学习）+ Mamba（选择性状态空间模型），两种融合策略：(1)用CoLES嵌入初始化Mamba隐状态；(2)将CoLES嵌入作为前缀token拼接到输入序列。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。Mamba本身相比Transformer具有线性复杂度优势，但论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到混合模型收敛速度比纯SSM基线快2-3倍，但这是训练收敛速度，非推理速度。

Datasets and metrics: 三个公开数据集：Age（多分类年龄组预测）、MBD（多标签产品获取）、Taobao（二分类购买预测）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 混合模型在三个数据集上均优于纯Mamba和CoLES+线性分类器；收敛速度比纯SSM基线快2-3倍；通过离散化步长图和Integrated Gradients进行了可解释性分析。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关；未涉及任何视觉任务；未讨论轻量化部署；Mamba在遥感视觉任务上的适用性未验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但Mamba/SSM作为Transformer的替代方案在遥感目标检测中近期也有探索，可关注Mamba在遥感视觉任务中的表现；CoLES对比学习策略可迁移到遥感自监督预训练中。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid approach for user-centric modeling of transactional event sequences that combines contrastive representation learning (CoLES) with State Space Models (SSMs). While contrastive methods yield high-quality compressed user representations, existing encoders -- RNNs and Transformers -- suffer from vanishing gradients or quadratic complexity, respectively. Mamba, a selective SSM, efficiently handles long-range dependencies but remains underexplored for personalized user analysis. We investigate two integration strategies: (1)~initializing the Mamba hidden state with a CoLES embedding, and (2)~prepending the projected CoLES embedding as a prefix token to the input sequence. Both approaches supply the model with an informative user prior from the first step. Experiments on three public datasets -- Age (multiclass age-group prediction), MBD (multi-label product acquisition), and Taobao (binary purchase prediction) -- demonstrate consistent improvements over standalone Mamba and CoLES with a linear classifier, with the hybrid models converging 2--3$\times$ faster than the plain SSM baseline. Explainability analysis via discretization-step maps and Integrated Gradients reveals selective event filtering on behavior-rich datasets and identifies the most informative transaction features.

</details>


### [151] [PIER: Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval for Time-Series Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20230)
*Shiyuan Luo,Runlong Yu,Chonghao Qiu,Yue Qin,Rahul Ghosh,Robert Ladwig,Paul C. Hanson,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息增强的检索框架(PIER)，通过物理一致性评分和自适应权重机制改进环境系统建模中的知识迁移，用于湖泊水温和溶解氧预测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦环境系统建模（湖泊水温和溶解氧预测），完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：环境系统建模、知识迁移、水温和溶解氧预测；场景：湖泊环境监测；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: PIER框架包含两条检索流：标准embedding检索流 + 物理感知检索流（使用基于物理通量特征训练的局部验证器进行通量-响应一致性评分），以及自适应权重调整机制。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：美国中西部356个湖泊41年的观测数据；评价指标：水温和溶解氧预测性能（具体指标摘要未说明）。

Experiments: PIER在多个骨干网络上一致优于基线方法，但摘要未给出具体数值对比。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于环境科学/物理信息机器学习领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无交集。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of environmental systems is fundamental to scientific understanding and decision-making, yet remains challenging because observations are limited and physical dynamics vary across systems. Retrieval-augmented approaches offer a natural path to transfer knowledge across systems, but standard embedding-based retrieval does not guarantee consistency of underlying physical processes, since scenarios with similar embeddings may arise from different underlying mechanisms. We propose Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval (PIER), a model-agnostic framework that augments embedding-based retrieval with a physics-aware stream that scores candidates by flux-response consistency with the target, using local verifiers trained on physics-derived flux features. A weight adjustment mechanism then learns per-scenario weights that adaptively balance the two retrieval streams based on diagnostic features summarizing physics-stream reliability. Experiments on 356 lakes across the Midwestern United States spanning 41 years show that PIER consistently outperforms baselines for water temperature and dissolved oxygen prediction, and serves as a general augmentation strategy across diverse backbones.

</details>


### [152] [PhaseAware: Interpretable Human-in-the-Loop Rehabilitation Scoring with Boundary Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20237)
*Yankai Zheng,Yuhe Liu,Yuxin Ma,Tianci Xue,Jiayuan Tian,Yu Fu,Yuxuan Hu,Jianing Wang,Zichun Xiao,Junya Mu,Shaohui Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhaseAware，一个用于康复评分（深蹲动作质量评估）的轻量级时序框架，通过相位感知门控残差机制实现可解释的连续评估。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于康复医学领域的动作质量评分（rehabilitation scoring），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然提到了"resource-constrained settings"和轻量架构，但这是面向临床工作流的边缘部署，与星上在轨部署无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：连续康复质量评估（continuous rehabilitation quality assessment）；应用场景：临床康复评分；传感器类型：未说明（推测为RGB视频或骨骼关键点数据）；目标类别：深蹲动作（squatting protocol）。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 时序骨干网络（temporal backbone）+ 相位描述符（phase descriptors）+ 身体分组描述符（body-group descriptors），通过backbone-conditioned gated residual pathway连接。属于时序动作分析架构，非CNN/Transformer-based目标检测架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要提到"compact framework"和"resource-constrained settings"，但未明确说明使用了剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等具体轻量化方法。轻量性来源于架构设计（gated residual mechanism稳定特征表示），而非模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及"resource-constrained settings"和"support use in resource-constrained settings"，但无具体数据。与星上部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：UI-PRMD（deep-squat protocol）、KIMORE（squatting subset）；评价指标：RMSE（均方根误差）。均为康复医学数据集，与遥感数据集（DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）无关。

Experiments: 在UI-PRMD上RMSE为0.0230，相比基线降低88.9%；在KIMORE上表现良好，表明相位感知设计可迁移。无目标检测相关的mAP、FPS等指标。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未提供模型参数量、FLOPs等轻量化关键指标；3. 仅针对深蹲动作评估，泛化性有限；4. 强调辅助临床审查而非自主决策，与在轨自主检测需求不同。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的点是"backbone-conditioned gated residual mechanism"作为一种轻量特征融合思路，但需重新设计用于遥感目标检测场景。整体而言，该论文的研究问题、数据模态、任务目标与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化差异过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Rehabilitation scoring systems are most useful when their outputs can be reviewed and interpreted within clinical workflows. This study presents PhaseAware, a compact framework for continuous rehabilitation quality assessment that combines a temporal backbone with phase- and body-group descriptors through a backbone-conditioned gated residual pathway. The model was evaluated on the UI-PRMD deep-squat protocol and further tested on the KIMORE squatting subset. On UI-PRMD, PhaseAware achieved an RMSE of 0.0230, corresponding to an 88.9% reduction relative to the accepted baseline. It also maintained favorable performance on KIMORE, suggesting that the phase-aware design transfers across related squatting protocols. In addition to score prediction, PhaseAware generates structured review cues based on phase- and body-level sensitivity, highlighting the movement stages and body regions most relevant to each prediction. The architecture employs a backbone-conditioned gated residual mechanism to stabilize feature representation, supporting use in resource-constrained settings. These cues are intended to support clinician review, boundary-case monitoring, and human-in-the-loop triage rather than autonomous decision-making. Overall, PhaseAware offers a practical and interpretable approach to rehabilitation scoring that may help integrate automated assessment into information systems while preserving clinician oversight.

</details>


### [153] [Breaking the $T^{3/4}$ Barrier for Regret Minimization With Bi-Dimensional CDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20258)
*Matteo Castiglioni,Anna Lunghi,Alberto Marchesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在二维空间[0,1]²上学习CDF相关目标的遗憾最小化问题，提出一种算法将遗憾界从Õ(T^{3/4})改进到Õ(T^{7/10})，并应用于重复双边贸易中的利润最大化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是online learning / regret minimization / CDF estimation，属于机器学习理论（bandit/online convex optimization）范畴，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为regret minimization for CDF-related objectives，应用场景为repeated bilateral trade，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或任何视觉任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何神经网络或深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或任何模型压缩/加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文使用理论分析（regret bound），未涉及任何遥感或视觉数据集。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论工作，未报告实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。论文自身的局限是遗憾界与下界Ω(T^{2/3})之间仍存在差距。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于online learning理论，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉或可迁移的技术思路。

Abstract: We study regret minimization for learning CDF-related objectives of the form \[ g(x)\cdot\mathbb{P}_{X\sim\mathcal{D}}(X\le x), \] over $[0,1]^2$, where $g$ is a known Lipschitz function and $\mathcal{D}$ is an unknown distribution. At each round $t$, the learner selects a point $x_t$ and observes the binary feedback $\mathbb{I}(X_t\le x_t)$, where $X_t\sim\mathcal{D}$. We design an algorithm achieving regret $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$, improving over the previous best-known bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})$ and showing that the curse of dimensionality can be at least partially lifted for this class of objectives, though a gap remains with the $Ω(T^{2/3})$ lower bound. As an application, our techniques yield the same $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$ regret bound for profit maximization in repeated bilateral trade with fixed prices.

</details>


### [154] [Interpretable Fuzzy Rule-Based Regression Extension for Ex-Fuzzy Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20277)
*Cayan Deniz Kucuktopana,Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Richard Pitts,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Mamdani模糊推理的可解释回归方法，扩展Ex-Fuzzy库，使用Fuzzy C-Means聚类进行目标感知的划分初始化，在KEEL数据集上实现透明且可竞争的回归性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于可解释模糊回归模型，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。既不是遥感/地球观测场景，也不是目标检测任务，更不涉及任何轻量化技术（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：可解释回归（interpretable regression）；应用场景：安全关键与受监管领域的回归任务；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明遥感或卫星相关场景。

Model architecture: 基于Mamdani模糊推理系统（Mamdani fuzzy inference），使用标量后件（scalar consequents）；采用Fuzzy C-Means聚类进行目标感知的划分初始化（target-aware partition initialisation）；比较了高斯划分（Gaussian partitions）和梯形划分（trapezoidal partitions）策略。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：KEEL repository中的10个回归数据集；评价指标：决定系数（coefficient of determination, R²）；对比基线：线性回归（linear regression）、多层感知机（multilayer perceptron）、随机森林（random forests）。

Experiments: 高斯划分策略优于均匀梯形划分策略，平均R²约0.86；生成紧凑规则库（10-15条可读规则）；在预测性能上可与黑盒回归模型竞争。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。该论文面向的是可解释模糊回归，而非目标检测或分类任务；不涉及任何遥感数据或场景；无任何模型压缩或加速技术；无部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，模糊推理的可解释性思路或许可用于遥感模型的可解释性分析，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心目标（检测精度+轻量化+星上部署）无直接关系。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve high predictive accuracy in regression tasks, but their deployment in safety-critical and regulated domains requires interpretability. While fuzzy rule-based systems offer transparent, linguistically explicit interpretable models, Mamdani-style fuzzy regression remains underrepresented in modern machine learning software libraries. This paper presents an interpretable regression extension for the Ex-Fuzzy library, enabling Mamdani fuzzy inference with scalar consequents learned directly from data. For this, a target-aware partition initialisation strategy based on Fuzzy C-Means clustering is introduced, in which linguistic variables are derived from an augmented input-output space to emphasise output-relevant regions of the feature space. The proposed extension is evaluated on ten regression datasets from the KEEL repository, comparing Gaussian and trapezoidal partition strategies against standard baselines including linear regression, multilayer perceptron, and random forests. Experimental results show that Gaussian partitions consistently outperform uniform trapezoidal partitions, achieving a mean coefficient of determination of approximately 0.86 while producing compact rule bases of 10-15 human-readable rules. The proposed implementation provides a transparent and competitive alternative to black-box regression models, supporting practical interpretability with competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [155] [The Blessing of Dimensionality: How Near-Orthogonality in High-Dimensional Spaces Explains Temporal Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20301)
*Abigail Woodring,Adrian Chan,Rana Muhammad Shahroz Khan,Sukwon Yun,Chau-Wai Wong,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究PortLLM方法在大语言模型持续预训练后的长期时间可移植性，并给出理论解释，与遥感目标检测和轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。本文完全聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法PortLLM的时间可移植性研究，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）等任何与本研究方向相关的内容。属于纯自然语言处理领域的LLM微调理论分析。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM持续预训练后的参数高效微调可移植性分析。应用场景：自然语言处理。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）的PortLLM方法，使用Mistral、Gemma、Qwen等LLM作为基座模型。与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 本文涉及PEFT（参数高效微调）中的LoRA方法，但这属于LLM微调阶段的参数效率优化，而非遥感目标检测模型轻量化（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。与在轨部署轻量化完全无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息，未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及使用Mistral、Gemma、Qwen三个LLM基座模型进行10步持续预训练实验。

Experiments: 实验包括：(1) 10步持续预训练下PortLLM补丁的长期时间可移植性实证研究；(2) 从高维向量近正交性和损失景观几何视角给出理论分析。未与遥感目标检测方法进行任何对比。

Limitations: 本文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，无法直接借鉴。局限性在于：完全不涉及遥感图像、目标检测任务、轻量化压缩方法或星上部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。如果强行寻找可借鉴点：(1) LoRA作为参数高效微调的思想可迁移到遥感目标检测模型的领域自适应微调中，但本文未涉及遥感场景；(2) 高维向量近正交性的理论分析思路可能对遥感特征空间分析有启发，但关联度极低。总体而言，不建议在本课题中参考本文。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has been widely used to adapt large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific tasks. Parameter efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are frequently used to reduce computational costs. PortLLM is a training-free and data-free scheme used to adapt LLMs after continual pretraining. Although the initial PortLLM results show that LoRA patches exhibit short-term temporal portability, the long-term performance of PortLLM across several updates of continual pretraining remains underexplored. Furthermore, the intriguing effectiveness of PortLLM is not well understood from a theoretical standpoint. We address these two open questions by (1) performing an extensive empirical study of the long-term temporal portability of PortLLM patches across 10 continual pretraining steps using base models Mistral, Gemma, and Qwen; and (2) offering two theoretical analyses to explain our observation that the simple PortLLM method achieves competitive performance. We find empirically that the portability persists across longer time duration, indicating that repeated fine-tuning is not required when the base model is periodically updated. We find theoretically that near-orthogonality of high-dimensional vectors is a key justification for temporal portability. Our analyses also demonstrate a geometric perspective of the loss landscape in facilitating the theoretical comparison of different adaptation options.

</details>


### [156] [Classical Hardware Acceleration of Quantum Autoencoders for Real-Time Anomaly Detection in Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20302)
*Ivan Ge,Sagar Addepalli,Abhilasha Dave,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究变分量子自编码器在高能物理对撞机实时异常检测触发系统中的应用，并实现了FPGA部署。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文面向高能物理（HEP）对撞机数据，而非遥感/卫星/地球观测场景；虽然涉及目标检测中的异常检测（anomaly detection）和FPGA部署，但应用领域、数据模态（高维对撞数据 vs. 遥感图像）、任务目标完全不同，不具备直接借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：异常检测（anomaly detection）触发系统；应用场景：高能物理对撞机实时触发（trigger）；传感器类型：对撞机探测器；目标类别：异常物理事件。与遥感卫星场景无关。

Model architecture: 变分量子自编码器（variational quantum autoencoder），属于量子机器学习（QML）模型。

Lightweight method: 涉及FPGA硬件加速部署，将量子电路编译并合成为低延迟硬件加速器。但轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）未提及。

Onboard deployability: FPGA实现，满足资源使用和时序约束，适用于未来对撞机触发应用。但参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等具体部署指标摘要未说明。且部署场景为地面加速器而非星上/边缘。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 性能与最先进经典方法相当，FPGA综合后满足资源及时序约束。但具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 量子机器学习在遥感领域的适用性尚未验证；3. 未涉及遥感图像特有的小目标、旋转框、多光谱等问题；4. 未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟等量化部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的是其FPGA部署流程（量子电路编译→硬件合成→时序约束满足），但遥感领域已有大量经典CNN/Transformer的FPGA部署工作，量子方法在遥感在轨场景中尚不成熟且缺乏验证。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) algorithms in high energy physics (HEP) can efficiently represent and leverage long-range, high-order correlations in high-dimensional collider data, potentially with fewer parameters and favorable scaling relative to classical models. Deployment of QML in real-time collider applications such as trigger systems requires the ability to emulate and compile quantum circuits classically, then synthesize the resulting quantum gates onto low-latency hardware accelerators, namely field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). We present a study of variational quantum autoencoder models for real-time anomaly detection triggers in modern collider experiments. The models achieve performance comparable to state-of-the-art classical approaches and, after FPGA synthesis, satisfy resource usage and timing constraints consistent with trigger applications in future colliders. This work provides one of the first FPGA implementations of QML models for HEP triggers, enabling higher-capability models in today's classical data acquisition pipelines while advancing quantum readiness of collider experiment infrastructure.

</details>


### [157] [Multi-modal transformer for signal classification in nanopore blockade experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20323)
*Sandro Kuppel,Julian Hoßbach,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态深度学习架构，联合处理原始时间序列、小波图像和静态特征向量，用于纳米孔传感器中多肽/氨基酸分子的高精度识别。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于纳米孔单分子传感（生物医学/化学传感领域），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务或模型轻量化/部署方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：分子识别（多肽/氨基酸分类）；场景：纳米孔单分子传感（生物医学诊断）；传感器类型：纳米孔器件；目标类别：42种多肽/20种氨基酸。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 多模态深度学习架构，联合处理三种信号表示：原始时间序列数据、基于小波的图像、静态特征向量。摘要未说明具体网络结构（如CNN/Transformer/融合模块等细节）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向实验室生物传感场景，未考虑星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：42-peptide benchmark（42种多肽数据集）和20-amino-acid dataset（20种氨基酸数据集）；评价指标：准确率（accuracy），在42肽数据集上超过已有方法10个百分点以上，在20氨基酸数据集上达到近乎完美准确率。

Experiments: 主要结果：在42肽基准上超越现有方法10+个百分点；在20氨基酸数据集上达到近乎完美准确率。进行了注意力分析，表明时间序列和小波图像输入关注同一事件的不同特征。摘要未说明消融实验、对比方法细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术要素；2. 未涉及模型轻量化或部署优化；3. 未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等工程实用性指标；4. 仅在分子识别数据集上验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的技术启发是：多模态信号融合策略（原始信号+小波变换+特征向量）在遥感领域可类比为多模态遥感数据融合（如光学+SAR+高光谱），但该论文本身不涉及遥感场景，且未提供轻量化方案，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Nanopore devices have emerged as powerful tools for single-molecule sensing, with potential for rapid, portable diagnostics. They detect changes in ionic current as analytes enter nanometer-scale pores, providing a means of identifying diverse biomarkers from their characteristic signal patterns. However, these signals are highly complex, and reliably assigning them to specific molecules remains a major challenge. Here, we address this by introducing a multi-modal deep learning architecture that jointly processes multiple signal representations, including raw time-series data, wavelet-based images, and static feature vectors. Our approach surpasses existing methods by more than 10 percentage points on a 42-peptide benchmark and transfers to a 20-amino-acid dataset with near-perfect accuracy. The model integrates complementary information from these representations, with attention analysis showing that the time-series and wavelet-image inputs emphasize different features of the same event. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of machine learning to enable robust, high-accuracy molecular identification with nanopore sensors.

</details>


### [158] [Interval and fuzzy physics-augmented neural networks (iPANN and fPANN) for uncertainty quantification and propagation in constitutive modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20339)
*Somesh Pratap Singh,Govinda Anantha Padmanabha,Jingye Tan,Steven Yang,Reese E. Jones,D. Thomas Seidl,Nikolaos Bouklas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出区间物理增强神经网络(iPANNs)和模糊物理增强神经网络(fPANNs)，用于超弹性本构建模中的不确定性量化，属于固体力学领域。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于固体力学中的超弹性本构建模与不确定性量化，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Model architecture: iPANNs：稀疏下界、均值、上界自由能密度分支网络，通过自动微分获得应力；fPANNs：在iPANN基础上通过alpha-cut插值嵌入模糊集表示。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。文中提到的smoothed L0 regularization用于促进可解释的稀疏能量表示，并非模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成各向同性超弹性数据，含异方差噪声、不同随机实现、偏移噪声均值、不同噪声幅度；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，主要评估学习边界能否包围含噪应力观测并在测试集上泛化。

Experiments: 在合成数据上验证了iPANNs和fPANNs能学习包围含噪应力观测的边界并泛化到测试集；在有限元仿真中检验了不确定性传播。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术。该论文属于计算力学/本构建模领域。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务、场景、方法上均无交集。

Abstract: Constitutive modeling under uncertainty remains a central challenge for reliable mechanics simulations, particularly when the available stress-deformation data are sparse, noisy, or heterogeneous. We propose interval and fuzzy physics-augmented neural networks (iPANNs and fPANNs) for uncertainty-aware hyperelastic constitutive modeling. iPANNs learn sparse lower, mean, and upper free energy density branches whose stresses, obtained by automatic differentiation, ultimately enclose noisy stress observations. In contrast to this deterministic interval description, fPANNs embed the learned iPANN branches into a fuzzy-set representation through alpha-cut interpolation, yielding a nested family of admissible responses. iPANNs and fPANNs encode mechanistic constraints - preserving objectivity, consistency and promoting polyconvexity - and smoothed L0 regularization promotes interpretable energy representations. The bound models are trained through a two-stage transfer-learning procedure in which a sparse mean constitutive response is learned first and then fine-tuned into lower and upper energy branches. We evaluate the framework on synthetic isotropic hyperelastic data with heteroscedastic noise, varying random realizations, shifted noise means, and varying noise magnitudes. The results show that the learned bounds enclose noisy stress observations while generalizing to the test set. Further, we examine the propagation of uncertainty through the mean, upper and lower bound predictions of the learned iPANN models in a finite element setting. The proposed framework provides a compact, physics-consistent route for distribution-free aleatoric uncertainty quantification in hyperelastic constitutive modeling, and propagation in downstream finite element simulations.

</details>


### [159] [Variance-reduced Domain Adaptation using Paired Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20367)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对无监督域适应的配对采样方差缩减技术(PSDA)，用于降低分布对齐损失(相关对齐和最大均值差异)在minibatch训练中的高方差问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关系。它属于无监督域适应(UDA)领域的优化方法研究，核心贡献是方差缩减技术，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测模型或任何轻量化/部署方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无监督域适应(UDA)中的分布对齐损失优化；应用场景：通用域适应任务；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。方法PSDA是一种优化采样策略，不涉及特定网络架构设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量网络设计。PSDA是优化层面的方差缩减技术，不减少模型参数量或计算量。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该方法不针对边缘或星上部署场景设计。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：三个域迁移数据集（摘要未具体命名）；评价指标：目标域准确率(target domain accuracy)；实验设置：minibatch优化设置下的仿真和真实实验。

Experiments: 仿真实验：相比相关方法降低了梯度方差；真实实验：在三个域迁移数据集上提升了目标域准确率。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 未说明PSDA在大规模遥感域适应场景下的可扩展性；3. 未讨论该方法与轻量化模型的兼容性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。如果研究方向涉及遥感域适应（如不同卫星传感器间的目标检测模型迁移），PSDA的配对采样方差缩减思想可能对遥感UDA任务有潜在借鉴价值，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向（模型压缩、加速、边缘部署）无直接关联。

Abstract: Correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy are two widely used distribution-matching frameworks for unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA). However, high variance in these losses has been shown to undermine their effectiveness in minibatch optimisation settings. Furthermore, the losses lack finite-sum structure, which renders them incompatible with classical stochastic variance reduction (SVR) methods. This paper proposes Paired Sampling for Domain Adaptation (PSDA), a novel SVR technique tailored to such objectives. PSDA pairs observations both within and across domains, to form quadruplets that are always sampled together during training. The pairings are designed to minimise expected gradient variance, and reduce to solving a set of linear assignment problems. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on three domain shift datasets show improved target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [160] [Online Variance Reduction for Domain Adaptation on Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20374)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个针对MMD和CORAL损失函数的在线随机方差缩减算法ARROW，通过自适应重加权实现流式数据上的方差缩减。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是领域自适应中的方差缩减优化问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

<details>

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，属于领域自适应中的优化方法研究，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然论文提出的在线方差缩减思想在优化层面有一定理论价值，但与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。如果未来研究涉及遥感领域自适应中的在线学习场景，可参考其在线重加权策略，但当前阶段借鉴意义有限。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of stochastic variance reduction (SVR) for the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) and correlation alignment (CORAL) loss functions. Although various offline SVR algorithms for these losses have been proposed, these are incompatible with online, distributed, or incremental learning settings. This paper presents Adaptive vaRiance Reduction via Online reWeighting (ARROW), the first online SVR algorithm for the MMD and CORAL for streamed data. The method maintains moving average references of the alignment statistics, and adaptively reweights incoming minibatches so that the minibatch and reference statistics are aligned. Further, we propose a relaxed reweighting scheme so that the ensuing weight-optimisation problem is tractable. In experiments and simulations, we show that ARROW performs competitively with offline algorithms in terms of runtime, degree of variance reduction achieved, and target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [161] [PG-KINN: A Physics-Informed Petrov-Galerkin Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for Solving Forward and Inverse PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20378)
*Amirhossein Sadr,Nima Soltani,Vahideh Moghtadaiee,Aida Pakniyat,Dara Rahmati,Saeid Gorgin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PG-KINN，一种基于Petrov-Galerkin变分公式的物理信息KAN网络，用于求解偏微分方程，在计算力学中优于MLP和现有KAN方法。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于AI for Science领域的物理信息神经网络（PINN），用于求解偏微分方程（PDEs），属于计算力学范畴，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：物理信息神经网络求解偏微分方程（PDEs）；应用场景：计算力学（裂纹奇异性、应力集中、超弹性、逆参数识别等）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: PG-KINN：基于Kolmogorov Arnold Networks (KANs) 的物理信息网络，采用Petrov-Galerkin变分公式，试空间为KAN，测试空间为独立、紧支撑、分段多项式空间，使用Gauss-Legendre求积。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息。

Datasets and metrics: 基准测试：裂纹奇异性、应力集中、Neo-Hookean超弹性、异构介质逆参数识别、复杂几何形状；评价指标：与MLP基线及PIKAN等SOTA方法对比的精度指标（具体指标名称摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在多个计算力学基准上，PG-KINN一致优于传统MLP基线和基于KAN的强形式/能量形式/逆形式方法（PIKAN）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 未讨论模型计算效率、参数量、推理速度等部署相关指标；3. 仅适用于PDE求解场景，不具备通用视觉特征提取能力。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但KAN网络作为一种替代MLP的架构，其可学习样条激活函数在函数逼近方面有优势，如果未来有工作将KAN引入遥感目标检测的轻量特征提取网络，可关注其参数量与精度的权衡。不过本论文未涉及任何检测或轻量化内容，仅作为架构启发。

Abstract: Physics-informed learning of partial differential equations (PDEs) has been dominated by multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), whose spectral bias and dense parameterization limit both accuracy and interpretability. Kolmogorov Arnold Networks (KANs) mitigate these limitations because their learnable spline activations are structurally aligned with the piecewise-polynomial bases of classical discretizations. However, the way a PDE is cast into a loss functional is as decisive as the choice of approximator: strong-form residual minimization requires high-order derivatives and heavily weighted losses, the energy (Bubnov-Galerkin) form is restricted to self-adjoint operators and, as we show, collapses to a trivial solution for parameter-identification problems, and boundary integral forms require a known fundamental solution. We propose PG-KINN, a physics-informed KAN built on a Petrov-Galerkin formulation in which the trial space is a KAN and the test space is an independent, compactly supported, piecewise-polynomial space evaluated with Gauss-Legendre quadrature. Integration by parts lowers the differentiation order while retaining applicability to general non-self-adjoint, nonlinear, and inverse problems; the localized test functions turn the global residual into a set of element-wise weak residuals with favorable conditioning. On a suite of benchmarks spanning crack singularities, stress concentration, Neo-Hookean hyperelasticity, inverse parameter identification in heterogeneous media, and complex geometries, PG-KINN consistently outperforms legacy MLP baselines and state-of-the-art KAN-based strong/energy/inverse formulations (PIKAN). These results position the Petrov-Galerkin coupling of KAN trial spaces and polynomial test spaces as a robust and accurate route for AI-based computational mechanics.

</details>

### [162] [A Bayesian Framework for Built-in Input Dimension Reduction for Gaussian Process Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19498)
*Eric Herrison Gyamfi,Emily L. Kang,Bledar A. Konomi,Guang Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种贝叶斯框架，将高斯过程的降维与建模无缝集成，并扩展至深度高斯过程，用于高维输入下的预测与不确定性量化。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何关联。论文核心是高斯过程（GP）的贝叶斯降维与建模方法，属于统计机器学习/计算科学领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不推荐阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 层次贝叶斯模型（Hierarchical Bayesian model），基于Stiefel流形上的先验，通过Hamiltonian Monte Carlo（HMC）结合测地流进行后验推断。扩展部分引入深度高斯过程（Deep Gaussian Processes, DGP）实现内置降维。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及通过extensive numerical studies验证方法在预测性能和不确定性量化上的改进。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法。仅提及所提方法计算成本更高，但预测性能和不确定性量化优于现有方法。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 计算成本高，不适用于资源受限场景；3. 未涉及任何目标检测任务或图像数据；4. 未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 基本无可借鉴点。该论文属于纯统计机器学习方法，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的研究目标（检测精度-速度-模型大小的权衡）无直接关联。不建议投入时间深入阅读。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) modeling is widely used in computational science and engineering. However, fitting a GP to high-dimensional inputs remains challenging due to the curse of dimensionality. While various methods have been proposed to reduce input dimensionality, they typically follow a two-stage approach, performing dimension reduction and GP fitting separately. We introduce a Bayesian framework that seamlessly integrates dimensionality reduction with GP modeling and inference. Our approach, built on a hierarchical Bayesian model with priors on the Stiefel manifold, enforces orthonormality on the projection matrix and enables posterior inference via Hamiltonian Monte Carlo with geodesic flow. Additionally, we extend this framework by incorporating Deep Gaussian Processes (DGP) with built-in dimension reduction, providing a more flexible and powerful tool for complex datasets. Through extensive numerical studies, we demonstrate that while the proposed Bayesian method incurs higher computational costs, it improves predictive performance and uncertainty quantification, providing a principled and robust alternative to existing methods.

</details>


### [163] [Boltzmann-Expected Molecular Design with Decoupled Annealing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19519)
*Selma Moqvist,Richard Beckmann,Ross Irwin,Rocío Mercado,Simon Olsson*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DECAF（解耦退火流），将3D分子设计重新定义为玻尔兹曼期望设计，通过两个条件流模型交替优化分子图，使系综统计量（而非单构象属性）成为设计目标。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于计算化学/分子设计领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等任何遥感视觉任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何模型压缩或部署技术。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文属于计算化学/分子设计领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：3D分子设计（Boltzmann-expected design）；应用场景：药物分子设计；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：分子图（molecular graph）。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: DECAF（Decoupled Annealing Flows）：将图与坐标的联合分布分解为两个条件流模型——图条件流p(x|G)作为玻尔兹曼模拟器，坐标条件流p(G|x)从3D信息提出新图。采用模拟退火接受规则交替优化。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向分子设计而非边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GEOM-Drugs；评价指标：平均回转半径（mean radius of gyration）、溶剂可及表面积（solvent-accessible surface area）、系综属性的方差和偏度（variance and skewness）。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）完全无关。

Experiments: 在GEOM-Drugs上，系综感知优化产生的分子图的平均回转半径和溶剂可及表面积一致地向目标偏移，而单构象优化在玻尔兹曼分布最宽的大分子药物上性能退化。DECAF支持多目标权衡，并能进行高阶矩设计（联合优化系综属性的方差和偏度），通过全原子MD模拟验证了构象分布。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。摘要未说明计算开销、推理速度等。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无直接可借鉴点。其"系综感知优化"（ensemble-aware optimization）思想在分子设计领域有意义，但无法迁移到遥感目标检测的轻量化部署场景。

Abstract: Most 3D properties relevant to molecular design, including free energies and shape descriptors, are $\textit{expectations}$ over the Boltzmann distribution over 3D configurations of a molecular graph. However, existing property-guided generative models tie each property to a single structure, ignoring the underlying ensemble. We recast 3D molecular design as $\textbf{Boltzmann-expected design}$ and realise it with $\textbf{DECAF}$ (Decoupled Annealing Flows), which factorise the joint distribution over graphs and coordinates into two conditional flow models: a graph-conditioned flow $p(x\mid\mathcal{G})$, acting as a $\textit{Boltzmann emulator}$, and a coordinate-conditioned flow $p(\mathcal{G}\mid x)$, proposing new graphs from 3D information. By alternating the two flows, DECAF optimises molecular graphs with a simulated-annealing acceptance rule whose scoring function is evaluated on ensembles drawn from $p(x\mid\mathcal{G})$, making ensemble statistics, not single-conformer properties, the design target. The resulting loop requires no retraining to change objectives. On GEOM-Drugs, we show that ensemble-aware optimisation produces graphs whose mean radius of gyration and solvent-accessible surface area consistently shift toward targets, while single-conformer optimisation degrades on larger drug-like molecules where Boltzmann distributions are broadest. DECAF extends to multi-objective trade-offs and, uniquely among 3D generative models, to $\textbf{higher-moment design}$: jointly optimising an ensemble property's variance and skewness to produce flexible molecules biased to a prescribed conformational regime: we verify the conformational distributions of these higher-moment designs with all-atom MD simulations.

</details>


### [164] [RELTA-SGLD: Relative-Growth Localized Taming for Nonconvex Stochastic-Gradient Langevin Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19544)
*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种名为RELTA-SGLD的随机梯度Langevin动力学稳定化方案，通过阈值和相对增长原则控制超线性随机梯度更新，在非凸优化中证明多项式矩稳定性和一阶驻点精度。

Experiments: 在Fashion-MNIST上，RELTA-SGLD在主动稳定化压力下优于未驯化的SGLD和TUSLA，与调优后的AdamW竞争力相当；在普通训练模式下，其轻量化局部化分母减少了对原始更新的不必要扰动。

Abstract: We introduce RELTA-SGLD, a taming scheme that stabilizes superlinear stochastic-gradient updates while reducing unnecessary suppression of the original learning drift. A threshold determines where the taming turns on, while a relative-growth principle derived from the one-step Lyapunov stability condition determines the required taming strength. Together, they produce a lighter $λ$-scale denominator and preserve a nonvanishing far-tail return. As a consequence, we prove polynomial moment stability and first-order stationary accuracy in both $W_1$ and $W_2$ for nonconvex SGLD with superlinearly growing stochastic-gradient oracles, improving the corresponding half-order and quarter-order bounds for comparable stochastic-gradient tamed schemes. On Fashion-MNIST under active stabilization pressure, RELTA improves the mean learning metrics over both untamed SGLD and TUSLA and remains competitive with a tuned AdamW reference. In an ordinary-training regime, its lighter localized denominator reduces unnecessary perturbation of the original update and maintains nearly untamed learning dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: We study the problem of recalibrating an online predictor [KE17, OKS24]: given an arbitrary "hint" sequence of forecasts, the learner must output new predictions that are calibrated while incurring small excess error relative to the original forecasts, under a proper loss. We give an online algorithm that achieves $(\varepsilon, \varepsilon^2)$-recalibration for Lipschitz proper losses in
  $T \approx \varepsilon^{-3}$ rounds, using an imbalanced extension of the recent simultaneous Blackwell approachability reduction framework of [HTY26]. We show that this tradeoff is optimal by proving a matching lower bound for recalibrating against the squared loss. We also prove a companion $\mathcal{K}_2$-recalibration theorem that obtains the same tradeoffs up to a logarithmic factor.
  As our main application, we show how our recalibration algorithms can be combined with the online refinement method of [FH23] to obtain simultaneous $\varepsilon$-calibration and $\varepsilon^2$-calibeating for smooth proper losses at the same asymptotic rate, improving upon prior works that achieved these properties separately or with a worse $\varepsilon$ dependence. In particular, the $\mathcal{K}_2$ variant answers a question of [CHJL26] on simultaneously achieving near-optimal calibeating and calibration rates. We also derive extensions to settings with multiple hint sequences. Finally, we empirically evaluate our algorithms on a classification dataset undergoing distribution shift.

</details>

Abstract: Observational causal analyses increasingly pool records across sites, vendors, and collection systems, creating vulnerability to append-only attacks in which plausible records are strategically selected to alter a reported treatment effect. We develop a data-poisoning audit for augmented inverse-probability-weighted estimation. The analyst specifies a finite catalog of feasible records, an append budget, and nested source capacities, and the adversary selects a feasible subset to maximize movement in a prespecified direction. With preprocessing and nuisance fits held fixed, we propose a greedy scan that computes the exact finite-sample worst-case movement at every append budget. To account for nuisance refitting, we go on to derive a total-influence score combining each record's direct contribution with its effect through the propensity and outcome models. We further obtain a conservative finite-budget bound for the fully refitted estimate. Extensive simulations validate the exact result and show that total influence improves local refit prediction, while multisite and public-data analyses demonstrate material sensitivity at small append budgets. By translating adversarial data-composition risk into movement curves and critical budgets, the framework supports more reliable causal reporting and the design of source-level safeguards.

</details>

Abstract: Non--negative matrix factorization (NMF) has become an established dimensionality reduction technique for extracting latent structures from non--negative data and has found widespread applications in fields such as bioinformatics, text mining, image analysis, and recommender systems. As the popularity of NMF has increased, numerous \textit{R} packages implementing different optimization strategies and computational frameworks have been developed. Despite their widespread availability, comprehensive evaluations of these implementations under real--world data conditions remain limited. Consequently, researchers often lack objective guidance when selecting an appropriate package for practical applications. This study introduces a new \textit{R} package for NMF and offers asystematic performance comparison with two widely available \textit{R} packages for NMF analysis. Rather than relying on simulated datasets, the evaluation is conducted using real--world data to better reflect the complexity, heterogeneity, and noise characteristics encountered in practical analytical settings. The packages are assessed using a consistent experimental framework, with emphasis on computational efficiency, convergence behavior, reconstruction accuracy, memory utilization, and the stability of the resulting matrix factorization.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce the Directional Kernel Mean Difference (DKMD), a signed statistic for univariate distribution comparison that preserves the direction of distributional shifts. Unlike the squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), which discards directional information by squaring the RKHS distance, DKMD integrates the difference of kernel mean embeddings against a fixed odd weighting function. This construction yields three structural properties: antisymmetry, immunity to symmetric distributional differences, and directional monotonicity under stochastic dominance. We derive a data-driven Riemann estimator that ensures asymptotic consistency with the continuous formulation, strictly preserving the theoretical guarantees of the signed statistic in empirical evaluations. To overcome the quadratic computational cost of kernel methods, we develop an $O(N \log N)$ prefix--suffix scanning algorithm that exploits the total order of the real line while requiring only $O(N)$ memory. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that DKMD correctly isolates directional shifts from symmetric perturbations, remains robust to heavy-tailed outliers that can flip the sign of the mean difference, and scales to millions of samples in seconds.

</details>


### [169] [Statistical Inference for Rank Allocation in Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20205)

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）的参数高效微调方法LoRA的秩分配问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。虽然涉及"剪枝"（pruning）概念，但针对的是LoRA组件的秩剪枝，而非遥感目标检测模型的轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对统计假设检验驱动的剪枝策略有跨领域借鉴兴趣，否则优先级极低。

Model architecture: 基于LoRA的微调架构，涉及DeBERTaV3-base、BART-Large、Qwen2.5-7B等大语言模型。核心方法StatLoRA将秩分配建模为统计假设检验问题，为每个LoRA组件构建检验统计量，利用估计的p值决定组件保留或剪枝。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文面向大语言模型微调场景，不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Limitations: 1. 完全面向NLP领域，与遥感/卫星/目标检测无关；2. 未涉及任何遥感数据集或遥感场景；3. 未提供模型推理速度、参数量、FLOPs等部署关键指标；4. 方法针对LoRA微调阶段的秩分配，不适用于遥感目标检测模型的直接轻量化。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可能有间接借鉴价值：1. 将剪枝/分配问题建模为统计假设检验的思路，可迁移到遥感目标检测模型的通道剪枝或结构剪枝中，用p值决定哪些通道/层应保留或剪除；2. 论文中建立的随机优化器轨迹中心极限理论，对理解深度学习训练过程的统计性质有理论参考价值；3. 但整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点非常有限。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method for large language models. Since different modules and layers may contribute unequally to downstream adaptation, allocating rank resources under a fixed parameter budget is an important problem for balancing efficiency, expressiveness, and generalization. Existing adaptive rank methods address this problem mainly through carefully designed importance scores constructed from gradient-derived sensitivity and uncertainty measures, without an explicit statistical interpretation. In this paper, we formulate LoRA rank allocation as a statistical hypothesis testing problem and propose StatLoRA, a statistical inference-based rank allocation method. StatLoRA associates each LoRA component with a test statistic and uses estimated p-values to determine which components should be retained or pruned under a prescribed rank budget. The proposed testing procedure is supported by our central limit theory for stochastic optimizer trajectories. In particular, we establish asymptotic normality for a broad class of commonly used optimizers in deep learning, including AdamW, and derive the corresponding asymptotic distributions for the proposed component scores used in hypothesis testing. We evaluate StatLoRA on LoRA fine-tuning of DeBERTaV3-base, BART-Large, and Qwen2.5-7B across natural language understanding, natural language generation, and question answering tasks. Experiments show that StatLoRA achieves comparable or better performance than vanilla LoRA, AdaLoRA, and IGU-LoRA under matched rank budgets. Sensitivity analyses and empirical diagnostics further support the stability of the proposed hypothesis-testing-based allocation rule and provide empirical evidence for the asymptotic theory of component scores.

</details>

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未提及任何遥感或目标检测数据集。实验部分仅提到"Experiments show that the aggregate tracks strong experts without oracle expert selection"，未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Abstract: Bayesian online learning promises uncertainty-aware prediction on data streams, but its performance hinges on inferential choices, including learning rates, prior distributions and variational families, which are usually fixed before seeing the stream. We address this by treating Bayesian update rules as experts and aggregating the Bayesian experts according to sequential predictive losses. We prove that the resulting aggregate competes with the best expert in hindsight at an aggregation cost determined by how each expert's per-round performance is evaluated. We instantiate the framework in online conformal inference and Gaussian process regression. The conformal inference application yields a smoothed Bayesian counterpart of adaptive conformal inference with long-run randomized coverage, while the Gaussian process application gives an oracle inequality in cumulative predictive Kullback-Leibler risk and adaptation to unknown Hölder smoothness up to logarithmic factors. Experiments show that the aggregate tracks strong experts without oracle expert selection.

</details>


### [171] [Adaptive deep nonparametric regression from dependent data under covariate shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20309)
*William Kengne,Ehud Mossa Ockegna*

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论分析，未提及任何遥感或视觉数据集，也未提及mAP、FPS等检测指标。

Abstract: Covariate shift often occurs because, in many real applications, the source and the target observations may be generated from different distributions. In this case, the standard metric under the source distribution is not appropriate. This paper considers deep neural network estimators for nonparametric quantile and Huber regression under covariate shift and from dependent observations. We deal with a generalized Bernstein-type inequality that is satisfied by many classical models, including i.i.d. observations, $φ$-mixing, strong mixing, and $\mathcal{C}$-mixing processes. To perform the covariate shift phenomenon, we propose a sparse-penalized deep neural network (SPDNN) estimator that takes into account the discrepancy between the source and target distributions of the data. When the density ratio (between the source and target distributions of the covariate) is unknown, a two steps pre-training procedure is carried out: the first step is devoted to the construction of a least squares SPDNN estimator of the density ratio; which is used in the second step to perform a pre-training reweighted SPDNN estimator of the regression function. For both the quantile and the Huber regression, non-asymptotic error bounds of the proposed SPDNN estimators are established in the class of Hölder smooth functions. These estimators can adaptively attain (up to a logarithmic factor) the minimax optimal convergence rate from i.i.d. data as well as from several classical time series models.

</details>

### [172] [ST-DDA: Dynamic Channel Estimation in the Doppler-Delay-Angle Domain via Sparse Subspace Tracking for TDD Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19677)
*Xu Zhu,Tiejun Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出ST-DDA在线稀疏子空间跟踪框架，用于跳频TDD系统中多普勒-延迟-角度域的全频带信道重建，通过子空间交替方法和位置编码卷积重加权提升重建精度和计算效率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是无线通信中的信道估计与重建问题（frequency-hopping TDD systems, channel acquisition），与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。不涉及任何目标检测任务，也不涉及模型轻量化方法。属于通信信号处理领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，属于无线通信信道估计领域，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：跳频TDD系统全频带信道重建（channel acquisition）；场景：无线通信；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明任何遥感相关任务或场景。

Model architecture: ST-DDA框架，包含：交替子空间方法（alternating subspace method）、位置编码卷积重加权（position-encoded convolutional reweighting）、窗口化DDA域稀疏恢复。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"comparable per-slot runtime"作为计算效率指标，但未给出具体数值。与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的具体数据集名称。评价指标：重建精度和可靠性（more accurate and reliable reconstruction），与基线方法（dynamic compressed-sensing baselines）对比。摘要未说明具体数值指标。

Experiments: 实验结果表明：reweighted ST-DDA在更长的探测间隔（longer sounding intervals）和更大的跳频周期（larger frequency-hopping periods）下，比动态压缩感知基线方法更准确可靠，且每时隙运行时间相当。摘要未提供消融实验信息。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 仅针对通信信道估计场景，方法迁移到遥感视觉任务的可能性未讨论；3. 摘要未说明在真实硬件平台上的部署验证；4. 未讨论模型规模、推理速度等对遥感应用关键的性能指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能相关的点是：其"固定每时隙迭代预算"（fixed per-slot iteration budget）的思想，在星上资源受限场景下控制推理迭代次数有概念上的相似性，但方法本身（稀疏子空间跟踪、DDA域重建）与遥感目标检测的视觉特征提取和轻量化网络设计差异巨大，不建议深入参考。

Abstract: Accurate full-band channel acquisition in frequency-hopping time-division duplex (TDD) systems is challenging because each sounding slot observes only a limited frequency subband, while conventional single-slot recovery cannot fully exploit historical observations. We propose ST-DDA, an online sparse-subspace tracking framework for latest-slot reconstruction in the Doppler--delay--angle (DDA) domain. We first show that the Doppler-domain representation remains energy-concentrated under moderate channel variation, thereby supporting windowed DDA-domain sparse recovery. A local stability analysis further shows that the substantial overlap between adjacent windows enables the preceding-window estimate to warm-start each window-specific recovery problem, allowing the optimization progress to be carried across slots under a fixed per-slot iteration budget. For computational tractability, ST-DDA employs the alternating subspace method, which restricts the regularized least-squares fidelity updates to support-induced subspaces, together with position-encoded convolutional reweighting that exploits local angular and Doppler structures. Experiments show that reweighted ST-DDA achieves more accurate and reliable reconstruction than dynamic compressed-sensing baselines, particularly for longer sounding intervals and larger frequency-hopping periods, while maintaining comparable per-slot runtime.

</details>


### [173] [SpiRadar: Radar-Based Non-Contact Spirometry via Sparse Polynomial Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19798)
*Yonathan Eder,Yhonatan Kvich,Naama Golan,Adi Wagerhoff,Safit Levy,Lior Barbash,Ron Berant,Patrick Stafler,Yonina C. Eldar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于雷达的非接触式肺功能检测框架SpiRadar，用于儿科哮喘监测，不涉及遥感或目标检测。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联，稀疏优化（sparse optimization）方法在信号处理层面的思路或许可迁移至模型压缩中的权重稀疏化，但关联度极低。

Abstract: Chronic respiratory diseases affect hundreds of millions of people worldwide, with spirometry serving as the gold standard for pulmonary function assessment. However, conventional spirometry's reliance on mouthpieces and nose clips creates discomfort and technical challenges that can compromise test quality, particularly in pediatric populations where cooperation difficulties are amplified. This study presents SpiRadar, a comprehensive radar-based framework for non-contact spirometry that eliminates physical contact requirements while enabling accurate curve reconstruction, clinical parameter estimation, and bronchodilator response (BDR) assessment. Our key contributions include: (1) a methodological framework integrating physiologically-motivated preprocessing for robust signal extraction, a feature-dependent polynomial transformation linking radar-measured thoracic displacement to spirometric volume curves, and sparse optimization enabling generalization to unseen subjects without individual calibration; (2) a rigorous validation on a pediatric cohort of 39 subjects (ages 6-18 years) undergoing 58 spirometry trials, including healthy children and asthma patients tested pre- and post-bronchodilator, using subject-level leave-one-out cross-validation; (3) clinical-grade performance outperforming alternative non-contact methods, achieving accurate curve reconstruction (mean RMSE: 0.23 +- 0.12 L) and strong correlations (above 0.84) for key spirometric parameters. BDR classification achieved 89.5% accuracy with balanced sensitivity (90.9%) and specificity (87.5%). These results establish clinical feasibility for our non-contact spirometry approach, with particular promise for pediatric asthma monitoring and remote patient care.

</details>


### [174] [WARA: A Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Framework for Wireless Optimization Autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19822)
*Yuan Guo,Yilong Chen,Chao Hu,Xianghao Yu,Liang Hong,Jie Xu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出首个面向无线资源分配优化的端到端自动化研究框架WARA，基于多智能体LLM实现研究选题、建模、实验到论文产出的全流程闭环。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦无线通信领域的资源分配优化自动化研究，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 无线资源分配优化（wireless resource allocation optimization），非遥感/卫星场景。传感器类型、目标类别均不适用。

Model architecture: WARA（Wireless AutoResearch Agent），基于LLM的多智能体闭环系统，包含Controller、ScoringAgent等模块，采用artifact-mediated process和gate验证机制。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标为与已接收同行评审论文的质量对比（quality profile of recently accepted peer-reviewed papers）。

Experiments: WARA显著优于一次性LLM生成（one-shot LLM generation），接近已接收同行评审论文的质量水平。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何相关技术借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM驱动的自动化科研框架，与遥感目标检测轻量化方向无交集。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have shown growing capabilities in tool use, code execution, artifact inspection, and iterative revision, creating new opportunities for automating scientific research. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first end-to-end autoresearch framework for the wireless domain, with a particular focus on wireless resource allocation optimization, an essential area for characterizing the fundamental performance limits of wireless systems and enhancing their practical performance under dynamic channel and network conditions. Specifically, we propose the Wireless AutoResearch Agent (WARA), a closed-loop multi-agent system that transforms an initial research topic into a complete research package. WARA organizes the research workflow into three phases: 1) research gap identification and problem proposal, 2) optimization modeling, algorithm design, and experimentation, and 3) research deliverable construction. Each phase follows an artifact-mediated process, in which structured upstream artifacts are consumed to generate downstream outputs. Controller-managed gates validate these artifacts and maintain consistency among problem formulations, algorithms, experiments, and research claims. When validation fails, WARA repairs only the affected artifact instead of restarting the entire workflow. We further design an LLM-based ScoringAgent to evaluate manuscript-level research validity. Comparative results show that WARA substantially outperforms one-shot LLM generation and approaches the quality profile of recently accepted peer-reviewed papers. These results demonstrate the potential of closed-loop artifact control for end-to-end LLM-assisted wireless optimization research. The source code is available at https://github.com/guoyuan-dotcom/WARA_CUHKSZ

</details>


### [175] [Optimal Sensor Placement via Graph-constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19831)
*Feng Ji,Jingyang Dai,Wee Peng Tay,Sirajudeen Gulam Razul*

Model architecture: 流匹配（flow matching, FM）模型，学习从噪声到最优传感器坐标的连续映射；采用置换不变条件生成框架以支持固定锚点传感器部署。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Experiments: 在无线电传播模拟器上验证了所提框架的有效性。未提供与其他方法的定量对比结果。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。流匹配（flow matching）作为一种生成模型，理论上可探索用于遥感目标检测中的数据增强或锚点生成，但论文本身未朝此方向展开，且与轻量化主题无直接关联。

Abstract: Optimal sensor placement is a fundamental problem in graph signal processing (GSP), where a limited number of sensors are deployed to reconstruct a continuous signal field. Existing GSP methods rely on combinatorial optimization over discretized graphs, resulting in high computational cost and sensor locations restricted to graph vertices. We reformulate sensor placement as a continuous-space generative modeling problem. An offline GSP optimization routine first generates training samples of optimal sensor configurations, from which a flow matching (FM) model learns their distribution. At inference, the learned velocity field directly generates continuous sensor coordinates, eliminating online combinatorial optimization. We further develop a permutation-invariant conditional generation framework for deployment with fixed anchor sensors. Experiments on a realistic radio propagation simulator demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for sensor placement.

</details>

*Haoyang Wu,Yunfan Bai,Mei Shen,Yuwen Qian,Guangji Chen,Long Shi,Feng Shu,Jun Li*

Abstract: Satellite communication (SatCom), as an effective complement to terrestrial networks, has attracted considerable attention from both academia and industry owing to its wide coverage and high flexibility. However, the inherent openness of satellite links renders them highly vulnerable to eavesdropping, thereby posing significant security challenges. In this paper, we propose a satellite covert precision wireless communication (CPWC) system, where multiple intelligent reflecting surfaces (IRSs) cooperate to assist satellite transmissions, ensuring that confidential information is delivered to legitimate users while remaining undetectable to wardens. To further enhance covertness, an orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM)-based random subcarrier selection (RSCS) method is developed to concentrate the signal energy at the intended receiver. Under a practical satellite-terrestrial channel model, we derive closed-form covertness constraints for the CPWC system based on relative entropy and detection error probability. Under the relative-entropy constraint and the satellite power constraint, we maximize the covert rate by an alternating-optimization (AO) based semidefinite relaxation (SDR) iterative algorithm and obtain a high-quality feasible solution. Using this solution as a warm start, we further impose the detection-error-probability constraint and refine the beamformer through a sequential quadratic programming (SQP) based algorithm. Numerical results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed CPWC system, where the detection-error-probability-based scheme outperforms the second-order cone programming (SOCP) benchmark, the random-phase-shift design, the one-bit IRS quantized scheme, and the SDR baseline without precise communication (PC) in terms of covert rate.

</details>


### [177] [Graph Distribution-valued Signals in Wasserstein Spaces: Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20008)
*Yanan Zhao,Feng Ji,Xingchao Jian,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Wasserstein空间的图分布值信号（GDS）框架，将图信号表示为概率测度，以解决经典图信号处理中同步观测和拓扑不确定性等局限性。

Abstract: We introduce a framework for graph signal processing (GSP) in which signals are represented as graph distribution-valued signals (GDSs), i.e., probability measures in a Wasserstein space. This perspective addresses fundamental limitations of classical vector-based GSP, including the requirement for complete synchronous observations across vertices and the need for strict temporal correspondence in observed filter input--output pairs. Furthermore, by modeling the graph structure as a distribution conditioned on signal realizations, we provide a principled approach to signal-dependent graph structures, which are common in real-world applications, while explicitly encoding uncertainty in graph topology. Our framework inherently captures uncertainty and stochasticity while strictly generalizing traditional graph signals, which can be interpreted as Dirac delta measures. We develop a systematic correspondence between foundational GSP concepts and their GDS analogs, showing that classical formulations emerge as special cases of our framework. We establish theoretical continuity results for GDS transforms, providing stability guarantees for input perturbations and distribution approximations. We demonstrate the utility of this approach through example applications, including graph filter learning and anomaly detection, and validate its effectiveness through empirical studies.

</details>


### [178] [Drift-Aware RL-based Wavelet Denoising for Network-Traffic Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20011)
*Priyalakshmi Sheela,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种面向网络流量监测的漂移感知自适应小波去噪框架，利用强化学习(PPO)根据下游任务(异常检测和容量估计)效用选择去噪参数，而非追求重构保真度。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是网络流量监测(network monitoring)中的信号去噪问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测(object detection)或轻量化模型部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：网络流量监测中的异常检测(anomaly detection)和容量估计(capacity estimation)；场景：网络监控(network monitoring)；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：网络流量负载突发(transient load bursts)。

Model architecture: 自适应小波去噪(adaptive wavelet denoising)作为预处理层；四检测器门控(Page-Hinkley, variance-ratio, Jensen-Shannon, Anderson-Darling)判断何时调用学习策略；PPO(Proximal Policy Optimization)智能体选择每窗口小波配置。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法(剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等)。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未涉及星上/边缘部署相关信息，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：异常检测和容量估计的下游任务效用(downstream task utility)，而非重构保真度(reconstruction fidelity)；对比方法：低通移动平均滤波器、VisuShrink、SureShrink、BayesShrink、Wiener滤波器。

Experiments: 按漂移类型(drift type)和输入SNR进行基准测试；定义了清洁信号上的异常目标和污染上的漂移门控以保持两阶段非循环。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文聚焦网络流量信号处理，不涉及图像/遥感图像目标检测。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：(1) 将下游任务效用(downstream task utility)而非重构保真度作为优化目标的思路，在遥感目标检测中可类比为以检测精度(mAP)而非重建损失来指导去噪或超分预处理；(2) 自适应门控策略(adaptive gating)判断何时触发模型调整，可类比于星上根据成像条件(云量、光照、噪声水平)自适应切换轻量检测模型。但整体相关性很低。

Abstract: Traffic-utilisation measurements for network monitoring are corrupted by additive noise and statistical drift: time-dependent change in the signal's mean, variance, distributional shape, or tail behaviour. Static wavelet denoising, calibrated under stationary independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) Gaussian assumptions, becomes mismatched under drift and, at moderate-to-high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), over-suppresses useful structure and degrades monitoring decisions. We propose a drift-aware framework treating adaptive wavelet denoising as a preprocessing layer optimised for two tasks: anomaly detection, recovering the multi-scale transient load bursts that noise and drift obscure, and capacity estimation, recovering the operational required capacity $C_{95}$ (95th percentile of utilisation). Because localised bursts are multi-scale structure a wavelet preserves but a low-pass filter removes, detection discriminates denoiser families. A four-detector gate (Page-Hinkley, variance-ratio, Jensen-Shannon, Anderson-Darling) determines when to invoke a learned policy, and a Proximal Policy Optimization agent selects a per-window wavelet configuration over a mixed discrete-continuous action space. Unlike prior work, the reward is downstream task utility, not reconstruction fidelity. The denoiser is benchmarked, per drift type and input SNR, against a low-pass moving-average filter, VisuShrink, SureShrink, BayesShrink, and a Wiener filter. Defining the anomaly target on the clean signal and the drift gate on the corruption keeps both stages non-circular.

</details>


### [179] [SNR-Dependent Mismatched Filtering for Bistatic OFDM Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20012)
*Ying Zhang,Fan Liu,Yifeng Xiong,Jie Yang,Xinyi Wang,Shi Jin*

TL;DR: 研究双基地OFDM通感一体化系统的测距性能，重点分析解调误差导致的失配滤波对延迟域旁瓣的影响，并推导了不同调制方式下的期望旁瓣水平闭式表达式。

Abstract: This paper investigates the ranging performance of a bistatic integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) system employing orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM), in which an ISAC transmitter emits a communication waveform carrying random data symbols, and a separate receiver performs ranging by correlating the received signal with a locally demodulated symbol sequence. Owing to inevitable demodulation errors, the ranging processor operates under mismatched filtering rather than ideal matched filtering, resulting in a delay-domain correlation response whose sidelobe structure explicitly depends on the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Focusing on frequency-flat fading channels, we derive closed-form expressions for the expected sidelobe level (ESL) and the average mainlobe level of the resulting mismatched ranging response for BPSK, QPSK, and general square QAM constellations. The analysis quantitatively characterizes how SNR-driven symbol decision errors reshape the delay-domain sidelobe behavior, thereby providing analytical insight into the SNR-dependent scaling behavior of ranging performance in bistatic OFDM-based ISAC systems. Simulation results validate the theoretical derivations and confirm the accuracy of the proposed analysis.

</details>


### [180] [Uplink Positioning for PASS in Multipath Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20069)
*Yaoyu Zhang,Xin Sun,Tianwei Hou,Anna Li,Yuanwei Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于Pinching-antenna系统上行多载波定位的矩阵束和低复杂度Rank-1测距算法，以及两阶段加权非线性最小二乘定位算法，并进行了理论性能分析。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是无线通信中的Pinching-antenna系统定位问题，属于通信领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文提出的是信号处理算法（Matrix pencil-based和Rank-1测距算法），而非深度学习模型架构。

Experiments: 数值结果表明：1) MP算法比Rank-1算法精度和鲁棒性更高；2) Rank-1算法因多径偏差存在误差平台；3) MP算法定位精度随子载波数增加而提升。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供直接借鉴。论文聚焦通信定位，不涉及视觉感知、遥感图像处理或深度学习模型优化。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接参考的是"低复杂度算法设计"思路（如通过矩阵分解避免求逆），但应用场景和问题本质完全不同。

Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASS) enhance wireless propagation by activating or placing pinching antennas (PAs) near users. Therefore, accurate uplink positioning is essential for efficient communication. In this paper, an uplink multi-carrier positioning framework is established for PASS in multipath environments. Matrix pencil (MP)-based and low-complexity Rank-1 ranging algorithms are proposed to estimate the distances between the PAs and the user. For the MP-based ranging algorithm, the line-of-sight (LoS) component is separated from non-line-of-sight components by exploiting the shift-invariance property of the Hankel matrix, thereby enabling accurate distance estimation. For the Rank-1 ranging algorithm, the dominant LoS delay is directly isolated through truncated singular value decomposition, thereby avoiding matrix inversions. Subsequently, a two-stage weighted nonlinear least-squares (WNLS) positioning algorithm is designed to estimate the three-dimensional user position. To gain further insights, a comprehensive theoretical performance analysis of the proposed ranging and positioning algorithms is conducted. The closed-form ranging variances and position error bound (PEB) are derived to reveal the error propagation mechanism. Numerical results demonstrate that: i) The MP-based algorithm achieves higher accuracy and robustness than the Rank-1-based algorithm, while the Rank-1-based algorithm has lower computational complexity. ii) The positioning error of the MP-based algorithm follows the same trend as the derived PEB, whereas the Rank-1 algorithm exhibits an error floor due to multipath bias. iii) The positioning accuracy of the MP algorithm improves as the number of subcarriers increases.

</details>


### [181] [Temporal Broadening-Aware Multiplexing for Joint Sensing and Communication in THz Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20074)
*Saira Rafique,Ahmed Naeem,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种太赫兹频段下利用时域展宽效应复用的感知与通信共存框架，用于6G无线传感网络。
