Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型设计或压缩方法。摘要未讨论实际硬件实现、多用户场景扩展等问题。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于通信物理层研究，不涉及计算机视觉、目标检测或模型轻量化，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接或间接参考价值。

Abstract: High-resolution wireless sensing has become an integral component of futuristic 6G networks alongside high-rate communication. Terahertz (THz) band enables both functions through its extremely large bandwidth, providing sub-centimeter level sensing precision and multi-gigabit data rates. However, THz propagation suffers from severe channel impairments such as molecular absorption (MoA) and the resulting temporal broadening effect (TBE). For sensing, TBE causes temporal spreading of received echoes, leading to degraded range resolution and necessitating long guard intervals between consecutive sensing pulses to avoid overlap. These guards, while necessary for far sensing receiver (Rxsens), cause latency and inefficient temporal use. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a TBE-aware multiplexing framework that exploits the distance-dependent nature of TBE to enable interference-free coexistence of sensing and communication (S&C) pulses. A guard interval preallocated for the worst-case TBE at far Rxsens is opportunistically reused to embed a low-power single-carrier communication pulse for a nearby user experiencing minimal broadening. Limited TBE confines S&C pulses within their designated slots at short distances, while the broadened and attenuated communication pulse at the distant Rxsens becomes negligible, eliminating the need for successive interference cancellation (SIC). Simulation results reveal that compared with power-domain non-orthogonal multiple access (PD-NOMA) and fixed-guard alternative, the proposed scheme achieves superior bit-error rate, sensing accuracy, and latency performance, with up to 66.5% latency reduction under heavy traffic.

</details>


### [182] [WiFi Sensing via Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20103)
*Heping Wang,Zhongqin Wang,J. Andrew Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出ReWiS，一种面向WiFi感知的储层计算框架，通过冻结储层、仅微调轻量读出头实现跨域自适应和低计算开销部署。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦WiFi sensing（WiFi感知），利用CSI信号进行人体活动识别，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然涉及轻量化（0.72M可训练参数、低CPU延迟）和部署（AP/router/ESP平台），但场景和方法均与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对储层计算在嵌入式部署中的通用方法论感兴趣，可作为旁类参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：WiFi sensing（WiFi感知），具体为人体活动识别/行为识别。应用场景：室内WiFi环境，AP、路由器、ESP类嵌入式IoT平台。传感器类型：WiFi CSI（信道状态信息）。目标类别：摘要未说明具体活动类别。

Model architecture: ReWiS框架：将CSI转换为结构化微多普勒流（含公共、天线特定、差分运动线索），使用图耦合储层（graph-coupled reservoir）进行时序编码，后接轻量读出头（readout）进行分类。储层部分固定不变，仅读出头可训练。

Lightweight method: 采用Reservoir Computing（储层计算）范式：时序编码器（储层）固定，仅优化和更新轻量读出头（0.72M可训练参数）。部署后自适应时冻结储层，仅用少量标注目标样本微调读出头。属于轻量网络设计+参数冻结微调策略，不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 面向AP、路由器、ESP类嵌入式IoT平台的低计算预算场景。可训练参数量仅0.72M，CPU延迟较低（具体数值摘要未说明）。提及低功耗硬件实现的潜力。但未涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等具体部署硬件或工具链，未报告FLOPs、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 大规模WiFi sensing benchmark（数据集名称摘要未说明）。评价指标：in-domain macro-F1、cross-domain macro-F1。实验设置：域内测试、跨域测试、部署后自适应测试。

Experiments: 域内macro-F1 89.2%，平均跨域macro-F1 82.0%（仅0.72M可训练读出头）；轻量部署后自适应提升至88.5%跨域macro-F1；与近期深度基线方法对比（87.5%-89.2%平均跨域macro-F1），ReWiS在更低的优化成本和CPU延迟下达到竞争性能。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测领域，方法无法直接迁移。2. 储层计算在遥感图像目标检测任务中的适用性未经验证。3. 未报告FLOPs、模型总参数量（含固定储层）、推理延迟具体数值、能耗等部署关键指标。4. 仅针对WiFi CSI时序信号，不适用于遥感图像/多光谱/SAR等空间数据模态。

Ideas for my research: 1. 储层计算（Reservoir Computing）的"固定编码器+轻量可训练读出头"思想，可类比为遥感目标检测中"冻结backbone+轻量检测头"的轻量化微调策略，但需验证在图像/遥感数据上的有效性。2. 跨域自适应策略（冻结主体+少量目标样本微调轻量头）对遥感卫星在轨模型更新有参考价值，但需适配遥感图像的域偏移特性。3. 整体借鉴意义有限，因为WiFi sensing与遥感目标检测在数据模态、任务目标、网络结构上差异巨大。

Abstract: Practical WiFi sensing must handle clock-asynchronous links, cross-domain variation, and post-deployment updating under the limited compute budget of access point (AP), router, and embedded Internet-of-Things platforms such as ESP-class devices. Reservoir computing (RC) is attractive in this setting because its temporal encoder can remain fixed while only a lightweight readout needs to be optimized and updated. To address these deployment challenges under tight compute budgets, we present ReWiS, a WiFi-sensing-oriented reservoir framework that transforms channel state information (CSI) into structured micro-Doppler streams with common, antenna-specific, and differential motion cues, encodes them with a graph-coupled reservoir, and adapts to a new domain after deployment by freezing the reservoir and fine-tuning only a compact readout with a few labeled target samples. On a large-scale WiFi sensing benchmark, ReWiS achieves 89.2% in-domain macro-F1 and 82.0% mean cross-domain macro-F1 with only a 0.72M trainable readout, improves to 88.5\% after lightweight post-deployment adaptation, and remains competitive with recent deep baselines evaluated under the same protocol, which achieve 87.5%-89.2% mean cross-domain macro-F1, while requiring lower optimization cost and lower CPU latency. These results indicate that ReWiS provides a practical reservoir-based design for deployable WiFi sensing, with further potential for low-power hardware realization.

</details>


### [183] [Ambiguity-Resolved Micro-Doppler Construction for Asynchronous Bistatic Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20108)
*Yiyi Xu,Zhongqin Wang,Kai Wu,J. Andrew Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种微多普勒构建框架，通过循环差分和轻量级延迟-AoA-多普勒流水线解决双基地ISAC系统中的时钟异步和镜像模糊问题，在WiFi手势数据集上取得高精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是ISAC（集成感知与通信）中的WiFi手势识别，属于无线感知领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及模型轻量化在遥感卫星部署上的应用。

Reading priority: 1分。论文研究的是ISAC无线感知中的WiFi手势识别，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：微多普勒构建与手势识别；应用场景：双基地ISAC无线感知（WiFi手势数据集）；传感器类型：WiFi CSI；目标类别：手势动作。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提出的是一个信号处理框架，包含循环差分（cyclic differencing）和延迟-AoA-多普勒流水线（delay-AoA-Doppler pipeline），用于抑制镜像分量和残余项。

Lightweight method: 文中提到"lightweight delay-AoA-Doppler pipeline"，但这里的"轻量"是指信号处理流水线的计算复杂度低，并非深度学习模型压缩意义上的轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。与遥感模型轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提到"lightweight"，但未给出任何量化指标，且应用场景是WiFi手势识别而非星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：大规模WiFi手势数据集（large-scale WiFi gesture dataset）；评价指标：准确率（accuracy），达到90.1%-98.2%。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）无关。

Experiments: 消融实验验证了残余抑制和多维滤波的有效性；与代表性基线方法对比，平均提升约18个百分点。但基线方法未列出具体名称。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测方法；3. 不涉及模型压缩/加速技术；4. 仅在WiFi手势数据集上验证，未在遥感数据上测试；5. 未提供计算资源消耗的量化指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接参考的是其"轻量级信号处理流水线"的设计思路，即在信号/特征层面通过数学变换降低计算复杂度，但这种思路在遥感目标检测中通常已被CNN/Transformer的轻量网络设计所替代。不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) can turn wireless networks into pervasive sensing platforms, but bistatic deployments are impaired by transceiver clock asynchrony, which induces random phase fluctuations in channel state information (CSI). CSI-ratio sanitization introduces nonlinear distortion that limits multi-target scalability and complicates delay- and angle-of-arrival (AoA)-domain processing. Cross-antenna conjugate multiplication (CACC) preserves a linear structure, but leaves mirror ambiguity and second-order by-products that corrupt motion-induced Doppler signatures. We develop a micro-Doppler construction framework that resolves the ambiguity and suppresses these residual by-products. Cyclic differencing first attenuates the dominant mirror component. We then exploit the facts that residual terms occur at differenced delay coordinates and lack an ordered AoA steering structure, designing a lightweight delay-AoA-Doppler pipeline that isolates the desired kinematic response without coherently accumulating residuals at target bins. The filtered responses are aggregated into a higher-SNR micro-Doppler representation. Ablation studies confirm the value of residual suppression and multidimensional filtering. On a large-scale WiFi gesture dataset, the proposed representation generalizes across four domain factors, attaining 90.1%-98.2% accuracy and outperforming representative baselines by about 18 percentage points on average.

</details>

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提到SEC方法"accurate and efficient"，但未给出具体效率指标，且与星上部署场景无关。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文研究方向（近场定位+进化计算）与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题定义、应用场景、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Near-field localization has attracted significant attention in recent years due to the move toward higher frequencies and extremely large aperture arrays, which expand the near-field region and bring many sources into it. This implies that antenna arrays can be used to localize not only in angle but also in range. Although a wide range of localization methods has been developed, each comes with limitations that may hinder practical deployment. This article focuses on a class of techniques that has received relatively little attention in the prior literature despite its strong potential for accurate and efficient location estimation: swarm and evolutionary computation (SEC). These methods are well-suited to the complex optimization landscape of near-field localization and can offer important advantages over conventional approaches such as grid-based subspace methods and deep learning approaches.

</details>


### [185] [ISAC-Assisted Channel Knowledge Map Generation for Physical Layer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20176)
*Luca Bonaventura,Edoardo Gardin,Alessia Barison,Francesco Ardizzon,Stefano Tomasin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 利用ISAC技术重建多接收器周围物体布局，通过射线追踪构建信道知识地图（CKM），用于物理层认证（PLA）以验证合法发射机位置。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于无线通信/物理层安全领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然提到了"object"和"reconstruction"，但这里的"object"是指通信环境中的散射体/障碍物，而非遥感图像中的目标；"reconstruction"是环境布局重建，而非目标检测。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：物理层认证（PLA）、信道知识地图（CKM）构建；场景：无线通信环境感知；传感器类型：无线通信信号（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：通信环境中的散射体/障碍物布局重建。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱等任何遥感相关要素。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及射线追踪（ray tracing）用于信道建模，但未提及神经网络或深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：使用文献中的ISAC数据集；评价指标：虚警概率（false alarm probability）、漏检概率（missed detection probability）。

Experiments: 评估了ISAC引起的CKM重建误差和接收端信道估计噪声对PLA性能的影响，通过虚警和漏检概率进行验证。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型、方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于通信物理层安全方向，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enables the acquisition of environmental information by leveraging wireless signals transmitted for communication purposes. In this paper, we utilize this capability to reconstruct the layout of objects surrounding multiple receivers. Ray tracing is then applied to the reconstructed environment to infer the propagation channels for various transmitter positions, thereby constructing a channel knowledge map (CKM). The CKM is then used to verify the position of a legitimate transmitter, authenticating it against an adversarial device attempting to impersonate it from a different location. This physical layer authentication (PLA) mechanism utilizes the approximate known position of the legitimate transmitter, obtained, for instance, from the network as in cross-layer authentication, to compare the channel estimated from the received signal with the corresponding CKM data. We evaluate the impact on the PLA performance of both ISAC-induced CKM reconstruction errors and receiver-side channel estimation noise, in terms of false alarm and missed detection probabilities. Finally, the proposed approach is validated using an ISAC dataset from the literature.

</details>


### [186] [JEPA-CFM: A Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture-based Channel Foundation Model for Robust Fluid Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20202)
*Yuan Gao,Yiming Liu,Jun Jiang,Jianbo Du,Shunqing Zhang,Xiaoli Chu,Kai-Kit Wong*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于JEPA的通道基础模型(JEPA-CFM)，用于流体天线系统的信道外推和无线定位，通过预测隐空间抽象结构替代逐像素重建。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是6G无线通信中的流体天线系统(FAS)信道建模与定位问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：信道外推(channel extrapolation)和无线定位(wireless positioning)；场景：6G无线通信、流体天线系统(FAS)；传感器类型：天线阵列；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: JEPA-CFM：基于联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)的通道基础模型。包含编码器(encoder)提取高维隐层嵌入，预训练后冻结编码器，附加轻量任务头：解码器用于信道外推，全局平均池化+MLP回归头用于无线定位。

Lightweight method: 摘要提到预训练后冻结编码器，附加"lightweight task-specific heads"，但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。轻量性仅体现在任务头设计上，非核心贡献。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DeepMIMO urban scenario（真实城市场景仿真数据）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在DeepMIMO城市场景仿真中，JEPA-CFM在信道外推和无线定位任务上显著优于传统掩码自编码器(MAE)基线。消融实验和详细对比结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向所需的技术参考。摘要未说明模型参数量、推理速度等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。JEPA的隐空间预测思想（预测抽象结构而非逐像素重建）在遥感图像自监督预训练中可能有借鉴价值，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向关联度很低。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FAS) have emerged as a promising technology for sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks. By allowing antenna elements to move freely within a compact region, FAS can exploit rich spatial diversity without additional hardware. However, acquiring real-time channel state information (CSI), extrapolating channel values to unmeasured antenna ports, and determining accurate user positions remain major obstacles. These challenges stem mainly from strong spatial correlations within the limited aperture and the scarcity of observable data. To overcome these limitations, this paper introduces joint embedding predictive architecture (JEPA)-based channel foundation model (CFM) specifically designed for FAS. The model adopts JEPA to learn versatile representations by extracting high-level latent embeddings of masked or unobserved channel segments. Unlike conventional approaches that attempt pixel-by-pixel reconstruction of raw CSI coefficients, JEPA-CFM focuses on predicting abstract structures in a compact feature space. The pre-training objective combines three complementary loss terms: the standard masked autoencoder reconstruction loss, the JEPA latent prediction loss, and a sliced isotropic Gaussian regularization (SIGReg) term. Together, these components prevent representation collapse and significantly enhance robustness under severe spatial correlation and highly sparse observations. After pre-training, the encoder is frozen, and lightweight task-specific heads are attached: a decoder for channel extrapolation and a global average pooling layer followed by a multi-layer perceptron regression head for wireless positioning. Extensive simulations in the realistic DeepMIMO urban scenario demonstrate that JEPA-CFM substantially outperforms the conventional masked autoencoder baseline in channel extrapolation and wireless positioning.

</details>


### [187] [Joint Chirp Parameter Selection and Low-Complexity MMSE Receiver Design for AFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20227)
*Ruiyuan Mao,Qu Luo,Jianguo Li,Fabien Héliot,Tianqi Mao,Pei Xiao,Hee Wook Kim,Kai Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究AFDM波形在高速移动通信场景下的啁啾参数选择与低复杂度MMSE接收机设计，属于无线通信物理层领域，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是AFDM（仿射频分复用）波形在高速移动通信场景下的参数优化和低复杂度接收机设计，属于无线通信物理层技术。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通信波形参数优化与低复杂度接收机设计；应用场景：高速移动通信（高多普勒场景）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明深度学习模型结构。涉及AFDM通信系统的有效信道矩阵（ECM）结构分析、离散仿射傅里叶变换（DAFT）的循环对角结构、带状Cholesky分解等信号处理算法。

Lightweight method: 文中"低复杂度"方法包括：快速循环对角聚合（FCDA）算法用于c1参数选择、低复杂度带状MMSE（LC-BMMSE）接收机（利用带状Cholesky分解避免直接矩阵求逆）、层次搜索联合啁啾参数与结构化稀疏化（HS-JCPS）算法。但这些是通信信号处理中的计算复杂度降低方法，与深度学习模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及"计算复杂度约束"和"性能-复杂度权衡"，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：不适用（仿真验证）；评价指标：误码率（BER）、搜索时间（FCDA相比基于BER的准则降低一个数量级）。

Experiments: 仿真结果表明：FCDA算法将最优c1搜索时间降低一个数量级；HS-JCPS算法结合LC-BMMSE接收机能够找到近最优c1，并获得优越的性能-复杂度权衡。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的任何直接借鉴。论文局限在通信波形设计领域。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：文中利用矩阵结构特性（循环对角、带状稀疏化）降低计算复杂度的思路，与模型轻量化中利用结构化稀疏降低计算量的思想有表面相似性，但技术领域完全不同，难以直接迁移。

Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) has emerged as a promising waveform against doubly selective channels under high-mobility communication scenarios. Optimal chirp parameter selection and reduced-complexity receiver design in AFDM are essential for achieving satisfactory bit error rate (BER) performance with low computational complexity. In this paper, we investigate the joint optimization of chirp-parameter selection and low-complexity minimum mean square error (MMSE)-based receiver design by exploiting the structural characteristics of the AFDM effective channel matrix (ECM). First, a simplified BER performance metric is derived by leveraging the diagonal and circulant structure of the discrete affine Fourier transformation (DAFT), based on which a fast circulant-diagonal aggregation (FCDA) algorithm is developed for efficient $c_1$ selection. Then, a low-complexity banded MMSE (LC-BMMSE) receiver is developed by constructing a cyclic-banded ECM through path-wise structured sparsification, where banded Cholesky factorization is employed to avoid direct matrix inversion. Building upon the proposed BER metric and the LC-BMMSE receiver, a hierarchical-search-based joint chirp parameter and structured sparsification (HS-JCPS) algorithm is further proposed to jointly optimize the chirp parameter and sparsification pattern under a given complexity constraint. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed FCDA reduces the search time for the optimal $c_1$ by an order of magnitude compared with using a BER-based criterion. Moreover, the proposed HS-JCPS algorithm with the LC-BMMSE receiver can identify a near-optimal $c_1$, while attaining a superior performance-complexity tradeoff.

</details>

### [188] [Beyond Confidence in AI-Assisted Colonoscopy: A Spatial, Temporal and Quality-Aware Audit Framework for Endoscopic AI Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19372)
*Roberto Alcaraz Machado,Ana Lucía Rodríguez Blanco*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出EndoExplain，一个用于内窥镜AI辅助诊断的轻量级可审计框架，集成分类置信度、分割、CAM可视化、质量指标和时间摘要等多维信号。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文面向医学内窥镜（colonoscopy）场景，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测任务；虽然提到了"lightweight"（轻量级EfficientNet），但轻量化不是核心贡献，且与星上/边缘部署无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学图像分类+分割+可解释性审计；应用场景：内窥镜AI辅助诊断；传感器类型：内窥镜摄像头；目标类别：息肉（polyp）等10类内窥镜病变。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 分类器：EfficientNet-B0；分割器：U-Net++ with EfficientNet-B1 backbone；可解释性：CAM-style attribution（Eigen-CAM, Grad-CAM++等）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明专门的轻量化方法。仅使用了EfficientNet-B0/B1这类本身轻量的网络架构，但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然使用了轻量网络，但面向的是临床辅助诊断而非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：HyperKvasir（训练+视频测试）、ETIS-LaribPolypDB、CVC-ClinicDB（外部验证）；评价指标：分类准确率0.9280、ROC-AUC 0.9969、分割Dice 0.9318、IoU 0.8826、事件F1 0.8081。

Experiments: 分类和分割实验在HyperKvasir上完成；外部验证在ETIS-LaribPolypDB和CVC-ClinicDB上；时间基准测试在60个HyperKvasir视频上；临床专家外部合理性审查。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 是回顾性研究原型，非临床验证设备；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术；4. 未讨论实时推理或资源受限部署。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能参考的点是：其"多维度审计框架"的思路（置信度+定位+可视化+质量指标+时间上下文）在遥感在轨场景中可类比为"检测置信度+定位精度+图像质量评估+时序一致性"的多信号融合，但这是非常间接的启发。

Abstract: AI-assisted colonoscopy systems commonly report frame-level confidence scores, but confidence alone does not show whether a prediction is spatially plausible, temporally persistent or reliable under degraded image quality. We propose EndoExplain, a lightweight and reproducible audit framework for endoscopic AI review. The framework integrates classification confidence, lesion segmentation, CAM-style visual attribution, attribution-mask alignment, frame-quality indicators and temporal event summarisation, with the aim of separating signals that are often conflated in computer-aided detection pipelines. On HyperKvasir, the selected EfficientNet-B0 classifier reaches 0.9280 test accuracy over ten endoscopic classes and 0.9969 ROC-AUC for the polyp_family versus rest view. The selected U-Net++ EfficientNet-B1 segmenter reaches Dice 0.9318 and IoU 0.8826 on the segmented test split. A strict top-20% multi-method attribution audit shows that attribution method strongly changes explanation-mask agreement: Eigen-CAM gives the strongest overlap, whereas Grad-CAM++ remains weakly aligned. A frozen-model external sanity check on ETIS-LaribPolypDB and CVC-ClinicDB preserves this attribution-method ranking while showing dataset-dependent overlap. A human-reviewed clip-level temporal benchmark over 60 HyperKvasir videos reaches event F1 0.8081 at the pre-specified threshold 0.85 using one-to-one event matching with overlap >= 1 s. A clinician-informed external plausibility review supported the clinical readability of separating confidence, localisation, attribution, quality metadata and temporal context. The resulting cockpit-style review layer presents these signals as distinct auditable outputs. This is a retrospective research prototype and not a clinically validated medical device.

</details>


### [189] [Group-of-Latents: Perceptual Video Compression at Extreme Bitrates via Masked Latent Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19437)
*Shaokang Wang,Jinchang Xu,Peidong Jia,Zhijian Hao,Siyuan Qian,Fei Zhao,Rui Ma,Xiaozhu Ju,Jian Tang,Xiaodong Xie,Shanghang Zhang,Huizhu Jia*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散Transformer先验的统一生成框架，在极低比特率下实现高感知质量的视频压缩。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于视频压缩领域的极低比特率编码，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频压缩（极低比特率编码）；应用场景：通用视频压缩；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 基于Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 的生成框架，包含因果分词器（causal tokenizer）、Group-of-Latents (GoL) 策略、I-DCM（深度压缩模块）和U-LDM（统一潜在去噪模块）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文关注的是压缩率（bitrate）而非模型轻量化。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。Diffusion Transformer模型通常计算开销极大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标涉及bpp（bits per pixel）和感知质量（perceptual fidelity）。

Experiments: 在极低比特率（<0.005 bpp）下达到SOTA感知保真度，具有丰富的空间细节和鲁棒的时间一致性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关；2. Diffusion Transformer推理计算量大，实时性差；3. 仅关注视频压缩，不涉及检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1) 潜在空间分组策略（GoL）的思想或许可用于遥感视频流的在轨高效编码与传输；2) 生成式先验用于低码率重建的思路，在遥感图像压缩传输场景可能有启发。但整体与目标检测轻量化方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Most existing video compression algorithms follow a paradigm of transformation and quantization, optimizing the trade-off between distortion and bitrate. However, extremely low-bitrate compression remains an underexplored frontier where perceptual quality optimization under severely constrained coding resources has not been adequately addressed. In this paper, we propose a unified generative framework that leverages pre-trained Diffusion Transformer (DiT) priors to achieve high perceptual quality at extremely low bitrates. We first introduce a flexible Group-of-Latents (GoL) strategy within the latent space of a causal tokenizer, explicitly partitioning the latent stream into intra $I$-latents and inter $P$-latents. The Deep Compression Module (I-DCM) then encodes key $I$-latents to preserve perceptual anchors with minimal overhead. Building upon these anchors, the DiT-based Unified Latent Denoising Module (U-LDM) refines intra-frame textures and synthesizes $P$-latents from noise, reconstructing temporal dynamics at zero additional bitrate cost. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method uniquely operates in the extreme-low-bitrate regime (e.g., (<0.005) bpp), achieving state-of-the-art perceptual fidelity with rich spatial details and robust temporal consistency. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [190] [BLUE: Semantics-Preserving Video Compression for Efficient Vision-Language Surveillance Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19515)
*Shubham Baid,Akash James,Sahil Chachra,Nishant Sinha,Kunal Kislay*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文评估BLUE——一种面向固定监控视频的压缩方法，通过抑制静态背景冗余来减少存储和传输开销，同时保持VLM（视觉语言模型）的语义推理质量，并利用跳帧机制减少VLM推理调用次数。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。理由：(1) 场景是固定监控视频（surveillance video），不是遥感/卫星/航拍图像；(2) 任务是基于VLM的事件和异常理解（event and anomaly understanding），不是目标检测（object detection）；(3) 方法核心是视频压缩（BLUE压缩+跳帧），不涉及轻量化模型设计、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩技术；(4) 虽然涉及压缩和推理成本降低，但面向的是企业视频分析系统（enterprise video analytics），不是星上/边缘部署场景。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不推荐精读。仅对遥感视频卫星的压缩策略有极有限的参考价值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：VLM-based event and anomaly understanding（基于视觉语言模型的事件与异常理解）；场景：fixed-camera surveillance（固定监控摄像头）；传感器类型：普通监控摄像头（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：人类活动事件和异常行为。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体VLM架构。BLUE是一种压缩方法，不是检测模型。核心组件：BLUE压缩（抑制静态背景冗余）+ H.265编码 + packet-size-based frame skipping（基于包大小的跳帧机制）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化。本文方法属于数据层面的压缩（视频压缩+跳帧），而非模型层面的轻量化（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及：(1) 存储压缩（storage reduction）；(2) 跳帧可减少约53%的VLM推理调用（estimated 53% reduction in VLM calls）。但这些是针对企业监控系统，非星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：VIRAT（227个配对事件样本，来自106个片段）和CHAD（54个人类活动异常片段）。评价指标：VLM score（0-10分制，盲审协议）；压缩节省与VLM分数变化的相关系数（r=0.004）；跳帧比例（skip-heavy P-frames占比从1.4%提升至53.2%）。

Experiments: VIRAT上：原始H.265与BLUE压缩后的VLM平均分差约-0.01（0-10分制），几乎无变化。CHAD上：原始H.265得分为4.31，BLUE得分为4.26，接近等效。压缩节省与VLM分数变化无相关性（r=0.004）。BLUE将CHAD上skip-heavy P-frames占比从1.4%提升至53.2%，估计可减少53%的VLM调用。

Limitations: (1) 场景局限于固定监控摄像头，不适用于遥感卫星平台；(2) 任务为VLM语义理解，非目标检测；(3) 未涉及模型轻量化技术；(4) 未报告计算资源消耗（FLOPs、延迟、能耗等）；(5) 未讨论在轨/边缘部署的硬件约束；(6) 跳帧机制依赖包大小判断，可能不适用于遥感图像中稀疏小目标场景。

Ideas for my research: (1) 借鉴思路：遥感视频卫星（如吉林一号视频星）可参考BLUE的静态背景抑制+跳帧思路，减少冗余帧的传输和推理开销；(2) 但需注意：遥感视频中目标（舰船、飞机）通常较小且稀疏，静态背景抑制策略需要重新设计；(3) 本文的"machine-centric compression"（面向机器的压缩）理念可迁移到遥感在轨场景——设计面向检测模型而非人眼观看的压缩策略；(4) 整体借鉴价值有限，因为不涉及模型轻量化核心技术。

Abstract: Continuous surveillance video creates a growing storage, transmission, and inference burden for enterprise video analytics systems. While modern codecs such as H.265 reduce bitrate for human-viewable video, aggressive compression can degrade downstream computer-vision performance and does not necessarily reduce the number of vision-language model (VLM) inference calls required for semantic video understanding. This paper evaluates BLUE, a fixed-camera surveillance compression approach that suppresses static-background redundancy while preserving foreground activity, for its effect on VLM-based event and anomaly understanding. We compare raw H.265 and BLUE-compressed H.265 video on two surveillance datasets: VIRAT, comprising 227 paired event samples from 106 clips, and CHAD, comprising 54 human-activity anomaly clips. For each pair, the same frame index is evaluated using a VLM captioning pipeline, and outputs are scored against annotation-derived ground truth using a blind judging protocol. The results show no measurable degradation in semantic inference quality. On VIRAT, the mean VLM score remains effectively unchanged between raw H.265 and BLUE, with a mean difference of approximately -0.01 on a 0-10 scale. On CHAD, raw H.265 and BLUE obtain near-equivalent mean scores of 4.31 and 4.26, respectively. Compression saving is also uncorrelated with VLM score change on VIRAT (r = 0.004), indicating that higher BLUE compression does not predict semantic quality loss. Beyond storage reduction, BLUE increases the share of skip-heavy P-frames on CHAD from 1.4% to 53.2%, enabling an estimated 53% reduction in VLM calls through packet-size-based frame skipping. These findings suggest that BLUE functions as a machine-centric compression layer for surveillance video, reducing bandwidth and inference cost while preserving VLM semantic performance.

</details>


### [191] [PRISM-DR: Per-lesion Retinal Inference with Specialist Models for Diabetic Retinopathy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19864)
*Zübeyr Özeren,Tansel Uyar*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出PRISM-DR，一种针对糖尿病视网膜病变病灶的专用检测流水线，为每种病灶独立训练单类YOLO检测器，替代传统多类共享模型。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文面向医学眼底图像（fundus image）中的糖尿病视网膜病变病灶检测，属于医学图像分析领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能等遥感相关场景。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。仅作为"多专用检测器vs单共享模型"设计思路的参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学图像目标检测（病灶检测）；应用场景：糖尿病视网膜病变筛查；传感器类型：眼底相机（fundus camera）；目标类别：microaneurysms（微动脉瘤）、hemorrhages（出血点）、hard exudates（硬性渗出）、soft exudates（软性渗出）。

Model architecture: PRISM-DR流水线：ROI裁剪 → 眼底图像预处理 → 四个并行YOLO单类检测器（每个病灶一个） → tiling → 五折交叉验证集成 → 病灶间抑制（按物理尺寸和临床优先级而非置信度处理重叠）。每个病灶从YOLOv5/v6/v7/v8/v9中选最优版本，数据增强由贝叶斯优化调参。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确涉及轻量化方法。未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩技术。YOLO系列本身属于轻量级检测器，但论文未讨论模型大小、推理速度或部署优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。未讨论边缘部署或实时推理需求。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：IDRiD（Indian Diabetic Retinopathy Image Dataset），五折分层交叉验证；评价指标：mAP50（0.527）、F1（0.529）、各类别AP50（硬性渗出最高0.561）。

Experiments: 在IDRiD上达到mAP50=0.527, F1=0.529；硬性渗出AP50最高0.561；未微调情况下，在成像尺度接近IDRiD的数据集上迁移良好，视场和分辨率差异大时性能下降。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化或部署优化；3. 绝对性能较低（mAP50仅0.527），作者归因于小样本单源训练集和任务难度；4. 未讨论模型参数量、推理速度等工程指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但有以下可借鉴思路：1. "每个类别独立训练专用检测器"的策略——在遥感场景中，舰船、飞机、车辆等目标差异也很大（尺寸、长宽比、方向），可尝试为每类目标训练专用轻量检测器而非共享模型；2. 贝叶斯优化调参数据增强的策略可迁移到遥感数据增强中；3. 病灶间按物理尺寸和优先级抑制重叠的思路，可类比遥感中的旋转框NMS或类别优先级后处理。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy is a leading cause of preventable blindness; its early lesions are small, low contrast, and easily missed in manual screening. Most automated detectors handle the four non-proliferative DR lesions: microaneurysms, hemorrhages, hard exudates, and soft exudates, with a single multi-class model, even though these lesions differ sharply in size, color, morphology, and prevalence, so a shared model favors common, easy classes over rare, difficult ones. We present PRISM-DR, a lesion-specific pipeline that trains one single-class detector per lesion, each with its own configuration. From a raw fundus image, the pipeline applies region of interest cropping, fundus-specific preprocessing, four parallel YOLO detectors, tiling, per-lesion ensembling of five cross-validation folds, and an inter-lesion suppression step that resolves overlaps by physical lesion size and clinical priority rather than confidence. Per lesion, the best of five YOLO generations is selected, and augmentation is tuned by Bayesian optimization. Trained on IDRiD with stratified five-fold cross-validation, the system reaches a test mAP50 of 0.527 and F1 of 0.529, highest AP50 on hard exudates with 0.561. Without fine-tuning, the models transfer well where the imaging scale is close to IDRiD and degrade as field of view and resolution depart. These modest absolute results reflect a small single-source training set and a difficult task; however, treating each lesion as a separate detection problem is a practical alternative to a single multi-class model.

</details>

### [192] [FineServe: A Fine-Grained Dataset and Characterization of Global LLM Serving Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19349)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Shaoyuan Huang,Mingyuan Wang,Yunfeng Zhao,Xiaofei Wang,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了FineServe，一个从全球商业平台收集的多模型LLM服务负载数据集，用于细粒度分析LLM服务的工作负载动态，并开发了相应的负载生成器。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的在线服务系统优化（负载分析、调度、路由），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM服务负载分析与生成；应用场景：云上LLM推理服务系统；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 不适用。论文不涉及模型架构设计，而是关注LLM服务系统的负载特征分析。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何模型轻量化或压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文讨论的是云端LLM服务系统，与星上/边缘部署无关。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：FineServe（自建的多模型LLM服务负载数据集，来自全球商业平台）；评价指标：摘要未说明具体的性能评价指标，主要关注负载特征分析。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体的实验对比结果或消融实验。论文主要贡献是数据集和负载分析，未涉及模型性能对比。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题域、技术路线和应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as always-on online services, making efficient LLM serving a critical systems challenge. Achieving low latency and high throughput under volatile demand requires deep understanding of real-world serving workloads, yet existing studies often rely on proxy traces or coarse-grained characterizations that fail to capture the heterogeneity of modern multi-model LLM platforms. We present FineServe, an in-the-wild, multi-model LLM serving workload dataset collected from a global commercial marketplace, enabling fine-grained characterization of real-world serving dynamics across heterogeneous models and tasks. Leveraging FineServe, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of arrival dynamics and token behavior, revealing fundamentally different fluctuation regimes across model architectures, scales and task intents. Building on these insights, we develop the FineServe workload generator, which composes fine-grained model-aware workloads into configurable mixtures tailored for benchmarking multi-model serving platforms. By exposing these fine-grained workload dynamics, FineServe provides a realistic foundation for evaluating routing, scheduling, and capacity-planning strategies in LLM serving systems. FineServe is available at https://github.com/hihiztc1/FineServe.

</details>


### [193] [Hybrid LSTM-Graph Neural Framework for Robust Financial Fraud Detection and Adversarial Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19350)
*Mariam Zakaria Moussa Ali*

Model architecture: LSTM网络 + 手工设计的图拓扑特征（PageRank Centrality, In-Degree dynamics, Flow Ratio），混合架构。

Abstract: Financial institutions face significant challenges in detecting sophisticated money laundering patterns, such as smurfing and layering, due to extreme data imbalance (0.13% fraud rate) and evolving adversarial evasion tactics. This paper proposes FraudShield AI, a hybrid framework that integrates Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks with hand-crafted Graph Topological Features to capture both temporal sequences and structural relational context. By engineering network-centric features including PageRank Centrality, In-Degree dynamics, and a custom Flow Ratio, the system shifts the detection paradigm from isolated transaction analysis to network-level forensics. A Focal Loss objective is used to address class imbalance, and a dynamic thresholding mechanism is introduced to improve resilience against low-value smurfing attacks. Experimental evaluation on the PaySim dataset shows that the proposed hybrid model substantially outperforms Logistic Regression and XGBoost baselines in Precision, Recall, and F1-Score, particularly on hard-to-detect micro-transaction fraud patterns. An ablation study confirms the complementary contribution of both the temporal and topological components.

</details>


### [194] [OpenEvoShield: Dual Non-Stationary Continual Defense for Open-World Multi-Agent System Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19351)
*Litian Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Yuting Zhang,Zejian Chen,Bingyu Yan,Qiwei Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向LLM多智能体系统的共进化持续防御框架OpenEvoShield，应对对抗性注入攻击的分布漂移问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦LLM多智能体系统的安全防御（对抗性注入攻击检测），属于NLP/网络安全领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM多智能体系统的安全防御（对抗性注入攻击检测）；应用场景：安全关键型LLM-MAS部署；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: OpenEvoShield包含四个模块：M1（非对称速率控制器）、M2（正常边界更新器）、M3（EWC正则化策略集成）、M4（基于能量的多粒度检测器）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 5个基准测试、4种MAS拓扑结构；评价指标包括未见攻击检测率、假阳性率。

Experiments: 在100轮部署回合中，OpenEvoShield优于静态和持续学习基线方法，能检测大多数未见攻击并保持低假阳性率。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可借鉴内容。摘要未说明计算开销、实时性等部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。仅"持续学习/分布漂移适应"这一概念在遥感在轨场景中可能有间接启发（如模型在轨部署后需适应数据分布变化），但论文本身不涉及视觉任务或轻量化技术。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where adversaries inject malicious instructions through inter-agent communication to propagate harmful behaviors. Unlike static threats, these attacks are doubly dynamic: adversaries refine injection strategies against deployed defenses while normal-agent behavior drifts with system expansion. Existing defenses treat deployment as a closed-world problem and degrade rapidly once either distribution shifts beyond training coverage. We propose OpenEvoShield, a co-evolutionary continual defense framework for LLM-MAS. An asymmetric rate controller (M1) decouples fast attack-side and slow normal-side learning rates from dual drift signals. A normal-boundary updater (M2) maintains a dynamic behavioral boundary at the slow rate, while an EWC-regularized policy ensemble (M3) fast-adapts without catastrophic forgetting. An energy-based multi-granularity detector (M4) fuses node-, subgraph-, and graph-level evidence to classify novel attacks as out-of-distribution. Experiments over 100 deployment rounds across five benchmarks and four MAS topologies show that OpenEvoShield outperforms static and continual baselines, detecting most previously unseen attacks while keeping false positive rates low.

</details>


### [195] [Benchmarking Confidential GPU Inference on NVIDIA H100 under Intel TDX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19353)
*Wei Wang,Abdul Hyee Waqas,Burns Smith*

TL;DR: 该论文研究的是机密计算（confidential computing）模式下GPU加速大语言模型推理的性能开销，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Datasets and metrics: 不适用。论文使用LLM推理基准测试，指标包括TTFT（首Token延迟）、端到端请求延迟、每请求Token生成吞吐量、全局Token吞吐量、闭环请求吞吐量。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 不适用。实验在单张NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU上进行，对比机密模式与非机密模式。机密模式下Mistral-7B的TTFT增加21.8%，Qwen3-30B-A3B增加27.8%；全局Token吞吐量分别下降17.7%和21.1%。与遥感目标检测实验无关。

Limitations: 论文仅关注机密计算对LLM推理性能的影响，未涉及遥感、目标检测或轻量化任何方面，对本研究方向无参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不提供任何可借鉴的方法、思路或实验设计。

Abstract: Confidential computing is becoming a practical deployment requirement for AI inference workloads that process sensitive inputs or protect proprietary model assets. However, the performance cost of enabling confidential execution for GPU-accelerated large language model serving remains workload dependent and operationally important. This paper presents a benchmark study comparing standard non-confidential execution with confidential computing mode on a single NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU hosted in an Intel TDX confidential instance. The evaluation uses two representative language models, Mistral-7B v0.1 and Qwen3-30B-A3B, and measures time to first token, end-to-end request latency, per-request token generation throughput, global token throughput, and closed-loop request throughput under increasing concurrency. In fixed request-rate experiments, confidential mode increases average TTFT by 21.8% for Mistral-7B and 27.8% for Qwen3-30B-A3B, while global token throughput drops by 17.7% and 21.1%, respectively. In closed-loop concurrency experiments, throughput gaps remain in the 11.5-20.2% range, but the larger model reaches its saturation knee earlier under confidential mode. The results suggest that confidential GPU inference can retain usable throughput under load, but capacity planning must account for both the steady throughput penalty and the earlier saturation behavior observed for larger models.

</details>


### [196] [FormulaSPIN: Self-Play Fine-Tuning for Natural Language to Spreadsheet Formula Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19354)
*Cy Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FORMULASPIN自博弈框架，通过公式可执行性作为隐式监督信号，实现电子表格公式生成任务的自我迭代改进。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是电子表格公式生成（spreadsheet formula generation），属于自然语言处理/代码生成领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
